    82→
    83→// ParseHookInput parses Claude Code hook input from stdin.
    84→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ParseHookInput(hookType agent.HookType, reader io.Reader) (*agent.HookInput, error) {
    85→	data, err := io.ReadAll(reader)
    86→	if err != nil {
    87→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read input: %w", err)
    88→	}
    89→
    90→	if len(data) == 0 {
    91→		return nil, errors.New("empty input")
    92→	}
    93→
    94→	input := &agent.HookInput{
    95→		HookType:  hookType,
    96→		Timestamp: time.Now(),
    97→		RawData:   make(map[string]interface{}),
    98→	}
    99→
   100→	// Parse based on hook type
   101→	switch hookType {
   102→	case agent.HookUserPromptSubmit:
   103→		var raw userPromptSubmitRaw
   104→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   105→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse user prompt submit: %w", err)
   106→		}
   107→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   108→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   109→		input.UserPrompt = raw.Prompt
   110→
   111→	case agent.HookSessionStart, agent.HookSessionEnd, agent.HookStop:
   112→		var raw sessionInfoRaw
   113→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   114→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse session info: %w", err)
   115→		}
   116→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   117→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   118→
   119→	case agent.HookPreToolUse:
   120→		var raw taskHookInputRaw
   121→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   122→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse pre-tool input: %w", err)
   123→		}
   124→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   125→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   126→		input.ToolUseID = raw.ToolUseID
   127→		input.ToolInput = raw.ToolInput
   128→
   129→	case agent.HookPostToolUse:
   130→		var raw postToolHookInputRaw
   131→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   132→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse post-tool input: %w", err)
   133→		}
   134→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   135→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   136→		input.ToolUseID = raw.ToolUseID
   137→		input.ToolInput = raw.ToolInput
   138→		// Store agent ID in raw data for Task tool results
   139→		if raw.ToolResponse.AgentID != "" {
   140→			input.RawData["agent_id"] = raw.ToolResponse.AgentID
   141→		}
   142→	}
   143→
   144→	return input, nil
   145→}

   151→
   152→// ResolveSessionFile returns the path to a Claude session file.
   153→// Claude names files directly as <id>.jsonl.
   154→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string {
   155→	return filepath.Join(sessionDir, agentSessionID+".jsonl")
   156→}
   157→
   158→// ProtectedDirs returns directories that Claude uses for config/state.
   159→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ProtectedDirs() []string { return []string{".claude"} }
   160→
   161→// GetSessionDir returns the directory where Claude stores session transcripts.
   162→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {
   163→	// Check for test environment override
   164→	if override := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR"); override != "" {
   165→		return override, nil
   166→	}
   167→
   168→	homeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   169→	if err != nil {
   170→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get home directory: %w", err)
   171→	}
   172→
   173→	projectDir := SanitizePathForClaude(repoPath)
   174→	return filepath.Join(homeDir, ".claude", "projects", projectDir), nil
   175→}
   176→
   177→// ReadSession reads a session from Claude's storage (JSONL transcript file).
   178→// The session data is stored in NativeData as raw JSONL bytes.
   179→// ModifiedFiles is computed by parsing the transcript.
   180→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReadSession(input *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   181→	if input.SessionRef == "" {
   182→		return nil, errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   183→	}
   184→
   185→	// Read the raw JSONL file
   186→	data, err := os.ReadFile(input.SessionRef)
   187→	if err != nil {
   188→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   189→	}
   190→
   191→	// Parse to extract computed fields
   192→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(data)
   193→	if err != nil {
   194→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   195→	}
   196→
   197→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   198→		SessionID:     input.SessionID,
   199→		AgentName:     c.Name(),
   200→		SessionRef:    input.SessionRef,
   201→		StartTime:     time.Now(),
   202→		NativeData:    data,
   203→		ModifiedFiles: ExtractModifiedFiles(lines),
   204→	}, nil
   205→}
   206→
   207→// WriteSession writes a session to Claude's storage (JSONL transcript file).
   208→// Uses the NativeData field which contains raw JSONL bytes.
   209→// The session must have been created by Claude Code (AgentName check).
   210→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) WriteSession(session *agent.AgentSession) error {
   211→	if session == nil {
   212→		return errors.New("session is nil")
   213→	}
   214→
   215→	// Verify this session belongs to Claude Code
   216→	if session.AgentName != "" && session.AgentName != c.Name() {
   217→		return fmt.Errorf("session belongs to agent %q, not %q", session.AgentName, c.Name())
   218→	}
   219→
   220→	if session.SessionRef == "" {
   221→		return errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   222→	}
   223→
   224→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   225→		return errors.New("session has no native data to write")
   226→	}
   227→
   228→	// Write the raw JSONL data
   229→	if err := os.WriteFile(session.SessionRef, session.NativeData, 0o600); err != nil {
   230→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   231→	}
   232→
   233→	return nil
   234→}
   235→
   236→// FormatResumeCommand returns the command to resume a Claude Code session.
   237→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string {
   238→	return "claude -r " + sessionID
   239→}
   240→
   241→// Session helper methods - work on AgentSession with Claude's native JSONL data
   242→
   243→// GetLastUserPrompt extracts the last user prompt from the session.
   244→// Requires NativeData to be populated (call ReadSession first).
   245→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetLastUserPrompt(session *agent.AgentSession) string {
   246→	if session == nil || len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   247→		return ""
   248→	}
   249→
   250→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(session.NativeData)
   251→	if err != nil {
   252→		return ""
   253→	}
   254→
   255→	return ExtractLastUserPrompt(lines)
   256→}
   257→
   258→// TruncateAtUUID returns a new session truncated at the given UUID (inclusive).
   259→// This is used for rewind operations to restore transcript state.
   260→// Requires NativeData to be populated.
   261→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) TruncateAtUUID(session *agent.AgentSession, uuid string) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   262→	if session == nil {
   263→		return nil, errors.New("session is nil")
   264→	}
   265→
   266→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   267→		return nil, errors.New("session has no native data")
   268→	}
   269→
   270→	if uuid == "" {
   271→		// No truncation needed, return copy
   272→		return &agent.AgentSession{
   273→			SessionID:     session.SessionID,
   274→			AgentName:     session.AgentName,
   275→			RepoPath:      session.RepoPath,
   276→			SessionRef:    session.SessionRef,
   277→			StartTime:     session.StartTime,
   278→			NativeData:    session.NativeData,
   279→			ModifiedFiles: session.ModifiedFiles,
   280→		}, nil
   281→	}
   282→
   283→	// Parse, truncate, re-serialize
   284→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(session.NativeData)
   285→	if err != nil {
   286→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   287→	}
   288→
   289→	truncated := TruncateAtUUID(lines, uuid)
   290→
   291→	newData, err := SerializeTranscript(truncated)
   292→	if err != nil {
   293→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to serialize truncated transcript: %w", err)
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   297→		SessionID:     session.SessionID,
   298→		AgentName:     session.AgentName,
   299→		RepoPath:      session.RepoPath,
   300→		SessionRef:    session.SessionRef,
   301→		StartTime:     session.StartTime,
   302→		NativeData:    newData,
   303→		ModifiedFiles: ExtractModifiedFiles(truncated),
   304→	}, nil
   305→}
   306→
   307→// FindCheckpointUUID finds the UUID of the message containing the tool_result
   308→// for the given tool use ID. Used for task checkpoint rewind.
   309→// Returns the UUID and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.
   310→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) FindCheckpointUUID(session *agent.AgentSession, toolUseID string) (string, bool) {
   311→	if session == nil || len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   312→		return "", false
   313→	}
   314→
   315→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(session.NativeData)
   316→	if err != nil {
   317→		return "", false
   318→	}
   319→
   320→	return FindCheckpointUUID(lines, toolUseID)
   321→}
   322→
   323→// ReadSessionFromPath is a convenience method that reads a session directly from a file path.
   324→// This is useful when you have the path but not a HookInput.
   325→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReadSessionFromPath(transcriptPath, sessionID string) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   326→	return c.ReadSession(&agent.HookInput{
   327→		SessionID:  sessionID,
   328→		SessionRef: transcriptPath,
   329→	})
   330→}
   331→
   332→// SanitizePathForClaude converts a path to Claude's project directory format.
   333→// Claude replaces any non-alphanumeric character with a dash.
   334→var nonAlphanumericRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`[^a-zA-Z0-9]`)
   335→
   336→func SanitizePathForClaude(path string) string {
   337→	return nonAlphanumericRegex.ReplaceAllString(path, "-")
   338→}
   339→
   340→// TranscriptAnalyzer interface implementation
   341→
   342→// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current line count of a Claude Code transcript.
   343→// Claude Code uses JSONL format, so position is the number of lines.
   344→// This is a lightweight operation that only counts lines without parsing JSON.
   345→// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines (no size limit).
   346→// Returns 0 if the file doesn't exist or is empty.
   347→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
   348→	if path == "" {
   349→		return 0, nil
   350→	}
   351→
   352→	file, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from Claude Code transcript location
   353→	if err != nil {
   354→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   355→			return 0, nil
   356→		}
   357→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to open transcript file: %w", err)
   358→	}
   359→	defer file.Close()
   360→
   361→	reader := bufio.NewReader(file)
   362→	lineCount := 0
   363→
   364→	for {
   365→		line, err := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
   366→		if err != nil {
   367→			if err == io.EOF {
   368→				if len(line) > 0 {
   369→					lineCount++ // Count final line without trailing newline
   370→				}
   371→				break
   372→			}
   373→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   374→		}
   375→		lineCount++
   376→	}
   377→
   378→	return lineCount, nil
   379→}
   380→
   381→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given line number.
   382→// For Claude Code (JSONL format), offset is the starting line number.
   383→// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines (no size limit).
   384→// Returns:
   385→//   - files: list of file paths modified by Claude (from Write/Edit tools)
   386→//   - currentPosition: total number of lines in the file
   387→//   - error: any error encountered during reading
   388→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error) {
   389→	if path == "" {
   390→		return nil, 0, nil
   391→	}
   392→
   393→	file, openErr := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from Claude Code transcript location
   394→	if openErr != nil {
   395→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to open transcript file: %w", openErr)
   396→	}
   397→	defer file.Close()
   398→
   399→	reader := bufio.NewReader(file)
   400→	var lines []TranscriptLine
   401→	lineNum := 0
   402→
   403→	for {
   404→		lineData, readErr := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
   405→		if readErr != nil && readErr != io.EOF {
   406→			return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", readErr)
   407→		}
   408→
   409→		if len(lineData) > 0 {
   410→			lineNum++
   411→			if lineNum > startOffset {
   412→				var line TranscriptLine
   413→				if parseErr := json.Unmarshal(lineData, &line); parseErr == nil {
   414→					lines = append(lines, line)
   415→				}
   416→				// Skip malformed lines silently
   417→			}
   418→		}
   419→
   420→		if readErr == io.EOF {
   421→			break
   422→		}
   423→	}
   424→
   425→	return ExtractModifiedFiles(lines), lineNum, nil
   426→}
   427→
   428→// ChunkTranscript splits a JSONL transcript at line boundaries.
   429→// Claude Code uses JSONL format (one JSON object per line), so chunking
   430→// is done at newline boundaries to preserve message integrity.
   431→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
   432→	chunks, err := agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
   433→	if err != nil {
   434→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk JSONL transcript: %w", err)
   435→	}
   436→	return chunks, nil
   437→}
   438→
   439→// ReassembleTranscript concatenates JSONL chunks with newlines.
   440→//
   441→
   442→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
   443→	return agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks), nil
   444→}
   445→

    20→// HookNames returns the hook verbs Gemini CLI supports.

    22→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) HookNames() []string {
    23→	return g.GetHookNames()

    25→
    26→// ParseHookEvent translates a Gemini CLI hook into a normalized lifecycle Event.
    27→// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance (e.g., pass-through hooks).
    28→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
    29→	switch hookName {
    30→	case HookNameSessionStart:
    31→		return g.parseSessionStart(stdin)
    32→	case HookNameBeforeAgent:
    33→		return g.parseTurnStart(stdin)
    34→	case HookNameAfterAgent:
    35→		return g.parseTurnEnd(stdin)
    36→	case HookNameSessionEnd:
    37→		return g.parseSessionEnd(stdin)
    38→	case HookNamePreCompress:
    39→		return g.parseCompaction(stdin)
    40→	case HookNameBeforeTool, HookNameAfterTool, HookNameBeforeModel,
    41→		HookNameAfterModel, HookNameBeforeToolSelection, HookNameNotification:
    42→		// Acknowledged hooks with no lifecycle action
    43→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action
    44→	default:
    45→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // Unknown hooks have no lifecycle action
    46→	}
    47→}
    48→
    49→// ReadTranscript reads the raw JSON transcript bytes for a session.
    50→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {
    51→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    52→	if err != nil {
    53→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    54→	}
    55→	return data, nil
    56→}
    57→
    58→// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompts from the transcript starting at the given message offset.
    59→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error) {
    60→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    61→	if err != nil {
    62→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    63→	}
    64→
    65→	t, parseErr := ParseTranscript(data)
    66→	if parseErr != nil {
    67→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", parseErr)
    68→	}
    69→
    70→	var prompts []string
    71→	for i := fromOffset; i < len(t.Messages); i++ {
    72→		msg := t.Messages[i]
    73→		if msg.Type == MessageTypeUser && msg.Content != "" {
    74→			prompts = append(prompts, msg.Content)
    75→		}
    76→	}
    77→	return prompts, nil
    78→}
    79→
    80→// ExtractSummary extracts the last assistant message as a session summary.
    81→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error) {
    82→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    83→	if err != nil {
    84→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    85→	}
    86→	return ExtractLastAssistantMessage(data)
    87→}

    90→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) CalculateTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
    91→	return CalculateTokenUsageFromFile(sessionRef, fromOffset)
    92→}

    96→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) parseSessionStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
    97→	raw, err := readAndParse[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
    98→	if err != nil {
    99→		return nil, err
   100→	}
   101→	return &agent.Event{
   102→		Type:       agent.SessionStart,
   103→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   104→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   105→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   106→	}, nil
   107→}
   108→
   109→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) parseTurnStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   110→	raw, err := readAndParse[agentHookInputRaw](stdin)
   111→	if err != nil {
   112→		return nil, err
   113→	}
   114→	return &agent.Event{
   115→		Type:       agent.TurnStart,
   116→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   117→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   118→		Prompt:     raw.Prompt,
   119→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   120→	}, nil
   121→}
   122→
   123→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) parseTurnEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   124→	raw, err := readAndParse[agentHookInputRaw](stdin)
   125→	if err != nil {
   126→		return nil, err
   127→	}
   128→	return &agent.Event{
   129→		Type:       agent.TurnEnd,
   130→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   131→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   132→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   133→	}, nil
   134→}
   135→
   136→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) parseSessionEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   137→	raw, err := readAndParse[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   138→	if err != nil {
   139→		return nil, err
   140→	}
   141→	return &agent.Event{
   142→		Type:       agent.SessionEnd,

   147→}
   148→
   149→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) parseCompaction(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   150→	raw, err := readAndParse[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   151→	if err != nil {
   152→		return nil, err
   153→	}
   154→	return &agent.Event{
   155→		Type:       agent.Compaction,
   156→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   157→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   158→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   159→	}, nil
   160→}
   161→
   162→// readAndParse reads stdin and unmarshals JSON into the given type.
   163→func readAndParse[T any](stdin io.Reader) (*T, error) {
   164→	data, err := io.ReadAll(stdin)
   165→	if err != nil {
   166→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read hook input: %w", err)
   167→	}
   168→	if len(data) == 0 {
   169→		return nil, errors.New("empty hook input")
   170→	}
   171→	var result T
   172→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &result); err != nil {
   173→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   174→	}
   175→	return &result, nil
   176→}

    57→// ReadTranscript reads the raw JSONL transcript bytes for a session.
    58→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {
    59→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    60→	if err != nil {
    61→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    62→	}
    63→	return data, nil
    64→}
    65→
    66→// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompts from the transcript starting at the given line offset.
    67→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error) {
    68→	lines, _, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(sessionRef, fromOffset)
    69→	if err != nil {
    70→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
    71→	}
    72→
    73→	var prompts []string
    74→	for i := range lines {
    75→		if lines[i].Type != transcript.TypeUser {
    76→			continue
    77→		}
    78→		content := transcript.ExtractUserContent(lines[i].Message)
    79→		if content != "" {
    80→			prompts = append(prompts, textutil.StripIDEContextTags(content))
    81→		}
    82→	}
    83→	return prompts, nil
    84→}
    85→
    86→// ExtractSummary extracts the last assistant message as a session summary.
    87→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error) {
    88→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    89→	if err != nil {
    90→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	lines, parseErr := transcript.ParseFromBytes(data)
    94→	if parseErr != nil {
    95→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", parseErr)
    96→	}
    97→
    98→	// Walk backward to find last assistant text block
    99→	for i := len(lines) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   100→		if lines[i].Type != transcript.TypeAssistant {
   101→			continue
   102→		}
   103→		var msg transcript.AssistantMessage
   104→		if err := json.Unmarshal(lines[i].Message, &msg); err != nil {
   105→			continue
   106→		}
   107→		for _, block := range msg.Content {
   108→			if block.Type == transcript.ContentTypeText && block.Text != "" {
   109→				return block.Text, nil
   110→			}
   111→		}
   112→	}
   113→	return "", nil
   114→}
   115→
   116→// PrepareTranscript waits for Claude Code's async transcript flush to complete.
   117→// Claude writes a hook_progress sentinel entry after flushing all pending writes.
   118→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) PrepareTranscript(sessionRef string) error {
   119→	waitForTranscriptFlush(sessionRef, time.Now())
   120→	return nil
   121→}
   122→
   123→// CalculateTokenUsage computes token usage from the transcript starting at the given line offset.
   124→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) CalculateTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   125→	// Subagent transcripts live in <transcriptDir>/<sessionID>/subagents/
   126→	// but we don't have the sessionID here. The caller should pass the transcript path
   127→	// which may contain the session ID in its directory structure.
   128→	// For now, compute subagentsDir from the transcript path structure.
   129→	return CalculateTotalTokenUsage(sessionRef, fromOffset, "")
   130→}

   202→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) parseSubagentEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   203→	raw, err := readAndParse[postToolHookInputRaw](stdin)
   204→	if err != nil {

   206→	}
   207→	event := &agent.Event{

   210→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   211→		ToolUseID:  raw.ToolUseID,
   212→		ToolInput:  raw.ToolInput,
   213→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   214→	}
   215→	if raw.ToolResponse.AgentID != "" {
   216→		event.SubagentID = raw.ToolResponse.AgentID
   217→	}

   221→// readAndParse reads stdin and unmarshals JSON into the given type.
   222→func readAndParse[T any](stdin io.Reader) (*T, error) {
   223→	data, err := io.ReadAll(stdin)
   224→	if err != nil {
   225→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read hook input: %w", err)
   226→	}
   227→	if len(data) == 0 {
   228→		return nil, errors.New("empty hook input")
   229→	}
   230→	var result T
   231→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &result); err != nil {
   232→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   233→	}
   234→	return &result, nil
   235→}
   236→
   237→// --- Transcript flush sentinel ---
   238→
   239→// stopHookSentinel is the string that appears in Claude Code's hook_progress
   240→// entry when the stop hook has been invoked, indicating the transcript is fully flushed.
   241→const stopHookSentinel = "hooks claude-code stop"
   242→
   243→// waitForTranscriptFlush polls the transcript file for the stop hook sentinel.
   244→// Falls back silently after a timeout.
   245→func waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath string, hookStartTime time.Time) {
   246→	const (
   247→		maxWait      = 3 * time.Second
   248→		pollInterval = 50 * time.Millisecond
   249→		tailBytes    = 4096
   250→		maxSkew      = 2 * time.Second
   251→	)
   252→
   253→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "agent.claudecode")
   254→	deadline := time.Now().Add(maxWait)
   255→	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
   256→		if checkStopSentinel(transcriptPath, tailBytes, hookStartTime, maxSkew) {
   257→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel found",
   258→				slog.Duration("wait", time.Since(hookStartTime)),
   259→			)
   260→			return
   261→		}
   262→		time.Sleep(pollInterval)
   263→	}
   264→	logging.Warn(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel not found within timeout, proceeding",
   265→		slog.Duration("timeout", maxWait),
   266→	)
   267→}
   268→
   269→// checkStopSentinel reads the tail of the transcript file and looks for the sentinel.
   270→func checkStopSentinel(path string, tailBytes int64, hookStartTime time.Time, maxSkew time.Duration) bool {
   271→	f, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // path comes from agent hook input
   272→	if err != nil {
   273→		return false
   274→	}
   275→	defer f.Close()
   276→
   277→	info, err := f.Stat()
   278→	if err != nil {
   279→		return false
   280→	}
   281→	offset := info.Size() - tailBytes
   282→	if offset < 0 {
   283→		offset = 0
   284→	}
   285→	buf := make([]byte, info.Size()-offset)
   286→	if _, err := f.ReadAt(buf, offset); err != nil {
   287→		return false
   288→	}
   289→
   290→	lines := strings.Split(string(buf), "\n")
   291→	for _, line := range lines {
   292→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   293→		if line == "" || !strings.Contains(line, stopHookSentinel) {
   294→			continue
   295→		}
   296→
   297→		var entry struct {
   298→			Timestamp string `json:"timestamp"`
   299→		}
   300→		if json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry) != nil || entry.Timestamp == "" {
   301→			continue
   302→		}
   303→		ts, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, entry.Timestamp)
   304→		if err != nil {
   305→			ts, err = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, entry.Timestamp)
   306→			if err != nil {
   307→				continue
   308→			}
   309→		}
   310→		cutoff := hookStartTime.Add(-maxSkew)
   311→		if ts.After(cutoff) {
   312→			return true
   313→		}
   314→	}
   315→	return false
   316→}

    20→// HookHandlerFunc is a function that handles a specific hook event.
    21→type HookHandlerFunc func() error
    22→
    23→// hookRegistry maps (agentName, hookName) to handler functions.
    24→// This allows agents to define their hook vocabulary while keeping
    25→// handler logic in the CLI package (avoiding circular dependencies).
    26→var hookRegistry = map[agent.AgentName]map[string]HookHandlerFunc{}
    27→
    28→// RegisterHookHandler registers a handler for an agent's hook.
    29→func RegisterHookHandler(agentName agent.AgentName, hookName string, handler HookHandlerFunc) {
    30→	if hookRegistry[agentName] == nil {
    31→		hookRegistry[agentName] = make(map[string]HookHandlerFunc)
    32→	}
    33→	hookRegistry[agentName][hookName] = handler
    34→}
    35→
    36→// GetHookHandler returns the handler for an agent's hook, or nil if not found.
    37→func GetHookHandler(agentName agent.AgentName, hookName string) HookHandlerFunc {
    38→	if handlers, ok := hookRegistry[agentName]; ok {
    39→		return handlers[hookName]
    40→	}
    41→	return nil
    42→}

   196→// agentHookLogCleanup stores the cleanup function for agent hook logging.
   197→// Set by PersistentPreRunE, called by PersistentPostRunE.
   198→var agentHookLogCleanup func()
   199→
   200→// currentHookAgentName stores the agent name for the currently executing hook.
   201→// Set by newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging before calling the handler.
   202→// This allows handlers to know which agent invoked the hook without guessing.
   203→var currentHookAgentName agent.AgentName
   204→
   205→// GetCurrentHookAgent returns the agent for the currently executing hook.
   206→// Returns the agent based on the hook command structure (e.g., "entire hooks claude-code ...")
   207→// rather than guessing from directory presence.
   208→// Falls back to GetAgent() if not in a hook context.
   209→//
   210→
   211→func GetCurrentHookAgent() (agent.Agent, error) {
   212→	if currentHookAgentName == "" {
   213→		return nil, errors.New("not in a hook context: agent name not set")
   214→	}
   215→
   216→	ag, err := agent.Get(currentHookAgentName)
   217→	if err != nil {
   218→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("getting hook agent %q: %w", currentHookAgentName, err)
   219→	}
   220→	return ag, nil
   221→}
   222→
   223→// newAgentHooksCmd creates a hooks subcommand for an agent that implements HookHandler.
   224→// It dynamically creates subcommands for each hook the agent supports.
   225→func newAgentHooksCmd(agentName agent.AgentName, handler agent.HookHandler) *cobra.Command {
   226→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
   227→		Use:    string(agentName),
   228→		Short:  handler.Description() + " hook handlers",
   229→		Hidden: true,
   230→		PersistentPreRunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   231→			agentHookLogCleanup = initHookLogging()
   232→			return nil
   233→		},
   234→		PersistentPostRunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   235→			if agentHookLogCleanup != nil {
   236→				agentHookLogCleanup()
   237→			}
   238→			return nil
   239→		},
   240→	}
   241→
   242→	for _, hookName := range handler.GetHookNames() {
   243→		cmd.AddCommand(newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging(agentName, hookName))
   244→	}
   245→
   246→	return cmd
   247→}
   248→
   249→// getHookType returns the hook type based on the hook name.
   250→// Returns "subagent" for task-related hooks (pre-task, post-task, post-todo),
   251→// "tool" for tool-related hooks (before-tool, after-tool),
   252→// "agent" for all other agent hooks.
   253→func getHookType(hookName string) string {
   254→	switch hookName {
   255→	case claudecode.HookNamePreTask, claudecode.HookNamePostTask, claudecode.HookNamePostTodo:
   256→		return "subagent"
   257→	case geminicli.HookNameBeforeTool, geminicli.HookNameAfterTool:
   258→		return "tool"
   259→	default:
   260→		return "agent"
   261→	}
   262→}
   263→
   264→// newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging creates a command for a specific hook verb with structured logging.
   265→// It uses the lifecycle dispatcher (ParseHookEvent → DispatchLifecycleEvent) as the primary path,
   266→// falling back to the legacy handler registry for hooks that return nil events (e.g., PostTodo).
   267→func newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging(agentName agent.AgentName, hookName string) *cobra.Command {
   268→	return &cobra.Command{
   269→		Use:    hookName,
   270→		Hidden: true,
   271→		Short:  "Called on " + hookName,
   272→		RunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   273→			// Skip silently if not in a git repository - hooks shouldn't prevent the agent from working
   274→			if _, err := paths.RepoRoot(); err != nil {
   275→				return nil
   276→			}
   277→
   278→			// Skip if Entire is not enabled
   279→			enabled, err := IsEnabled()
   280→			if err == nil && !enabled {
   281→				return nil
   282→			}
   283→
   284→			start := time.Now()
   285→
   286→			// Initialize logging context with agent name
   287→			ctx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), agentName)
   288→
   289→			// Get strategy name for logging
   290→			strategyName := unknownStrategyName //nolint:ineffassign,wastedassign // already present in codebase
   291→			strategyName = GetStrategy().Name()
   292→
   293→			hookType := getHookType(hookName)
   294→
   295→			logging.Debug(ctx, "hook invoked",
   296→				slog.String("hook", hookName),
   297→				slog.String("hook_type", hookType),
   298→				slog.String("strategy", strategyName),
   299→			)
   300→
   301→			// Set the current hook agent so legacy handlers can retrieve it
   302→			currentHookAgentName = agentName
   303→			defer func() { currentHookAgentName = "" }()
   304→
   305→			// Try the lifecycle dispatcher path: ParseHookEvent → DispatchLifecycleEvent.
   306→			// Falls back to legacy handler registry if agent is unresolved or event is nil.
   307→			var hookErr error
   308→			ag, agentErr := agent.Get(agentName)
   309→			if agentErr == nil {
   310→				event, parseErr := ag.ParseHookEvent(hookName, os.Stdin)
   311→				if parseErr != nil {
   312→					return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook event: %w", parseErr)
   313→				}
   314→				if event != nil {
   315→					// Lifecycle event — use the generic dispatcher
   316→					hookErr = DispatchLifecycleEvent(ag, event)
   317→				} else {
   318→					// No lifecycle event — fall back to registered handler (e.g., PostTodo)
   319→					handler := GetHookHandler(agentName, hookName)
   320→					if handler != nil {
   321→						hookErr = handler()
   322→					}
   323→				}
   324→			} else {
   325→				// Agent not found — fall back to legacy handler registry
   326→				handler := GetHookHandler(agentName, hookName)
   327→				if handler == nil {
   328→					return fmt.Errorf("no handler for %s/%s and agent not found: %w", agentName, hookName, agentErr)
   329→				}
   330→				hookErr = handler()
   331→			}
   332→
   333→			logging.LogDuration(ctx, slog.LevelDebug, "hook completed", start,
   334→				slog.String("hook", hookName),
   335→				slog.String("hook_type", hookType),
   336→				slog.String("strategy", strategyName),
   337→				slog.Bool("success", hookErr == nil),
   338→			)
   339→
   340→			return hookErr
   341→		},
   342→	}
   343→}
   344→

    20→// PrePromptState stores the state captured before a user prompt
    21→type PrePromptState struct {
    22→	SessionID      string   `json:"session_id"`
    23→	Timestamp      string   `json:"timestamp"`
    24→	UntrackedFiles []string `json:"untracked_files"`
    25→
    26→	// TranscriptOffset is the unified transcript position when this state was captured.
    27→	// For Claude Code (JSONL), this is the line count.
    28→	// For Gemini CLI (JSON), this is the message count.
    29→	// Zero means not set or session just started.
    30→	TranscriptOffset int `json:"transcript_offset,omitempty"`
    31→
    32→	// LastTranscriptIdentifier is the agent-specific identifier at the transcript position.
    33→	// UUID for Claude Code, message ID for Gemini CLI. Optional metadata.
    34→	LastTranscriptIdentifier string `json:"last_transcript_identifier,omitempty"`
    35→
    36→	// Deprecated: StartMessageIndex is the old Gemini-specific field.
    37→	// Migrated to TranscriptOffset on load.
    38→	StartMessageIndex int `json:"start_message_index,omitempty"`
    39→
    40→	// Deprecated: StepTranscriptStart is the old Claude-specific field.
    41→	// Migrated to TranscriptOffset on load.
    42→	StepTranscriptStart int `json:"step_transcript_start,omitempty"`
    43→
    44→	// Deprecated: LastTranscriptLineCount is the oldest name for transcript position.
    45→	// Migrated to TranscriptOffset on load.
    46→	LastTranscriptLineCount int `json:"last_transcript_line_count,omitempty"`
    47→}
    48→
    49→// PreUntrackedFiles returns the untracked files list, or nil if the receiver is nil.
    50→// This nil-vs-empty distinction lets DetectFileChanges know whether to skip new-file detection.
    51→// When the receiver is non-nil but UntrackedFiles is nil (e.g., old state files deserialized with
    52→// "untracked_files": null), returns an empty non-nil slice so that all current untracked files
    53→// are correctly treated as new.
    54→func (s *PrePromptState) PreUntrackedFiles() []string {
    55→	if s == nil {
    56→		return nil
    57→	}
    58→	if s.UntrackedFiles == nil {
    59→		return []string{}
    60→	}
    61→	return s.UntrackedFiles
    62→}
    63→
    64→// normalizePrePromptState migrates deprecated fields after loading from JSON.
    65→func (s *PrePromptState) normalizePrePromptState() {
    66→	// Migrate the oldest field to the intermediate field first
    67→	if s.StepTranscriptStart == 0 && s.LastTranscriptLineCount > 0 {
    68→		s.StepTranscriptStart = s.LastTranscriptLineCount
    69→	}
    70→	s.LastTranscriptLineCount = 0
    71→
    72→	// Migrate all deprecated fields to the unified TranscriptOffset
    73→	if s.TranscriptOffset == 0 {
    74→		if s.StepTranscriptStart > 0 {
    75→			s.TranscriptOffset = s.StepTranscriptStart
    76→		} else if s.StartMessageIndex > 0 {
    77→			s.TranscriptOffset = s.StartMessageIndex
    78→		}
    79→	}
    80→	s.StepTranscriptStart = 0
    81→	s.StartMessageIndex = 0
    82→}
    83→
    84→// CapturePrePromptState captures current untracked files and transcript position before a prompt
    85→// and saves them to a state file.
    86→//
    87→// The agent parameter is used to determine the transcript position via TranscriptAnalyzer.
    88→// If the agent does not implement TranscriptAnalyzer, the transcript offset will be 0.
    89→// The sessionRef parameter is optional — if empty, transcript position won't be captured.
    90→//
    91→// Works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository.
    92→func CapturePrePromptState(ag agent.Agent, sessionID, sessionRef string) error {
    93→	if sessionID == "" {
    94→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
    95→	}
    96→
    97→	// Get absolute path for tmp directory
    98→	tmpDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireTmpDir)
    99→	if err != nil {
   100→		tmpDirAbs = paths.EntireTmpDir // Fallback to relative
   101→	}
   102→
   103→	// Create tmp directory if it doesn't exist
   104→	if err := os.MkdirAll(tmpDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
   105→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create tmp directory: %w", err)
   106→	}
   107→
   108→	// Get list of untracked files (excluding .entire directory itself)
   109→	untrackedFiles, err := getUntrackedFilesForState()
   110→	if err != nil {
   111→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get untracked files: %w", err)
   112→	}
   113→
   114→	// Get transcript position using TranscriptAnalyzer if available
   115→	var transcriptOffset int
   116→	if analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer); ok && sessionRef != "" {
   117→		pos, posErr := analyzer.GetTranscriptPosition(sessionRef)
   118→		if posErr != nil {
   119→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to get transcript position: %v\n", posErr)
   120→		} else {
   121→			transcriptOffset = pos
   122→		}
   123→	}
   124→
   125→	// Create state file
   126→	stateFile := prePromptStateFile(sessionID)
   127→	state := PrePromptState{
   128→		SessionID:        sessionID,
   129→		Timestamp:        time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),
   130→		UntrackedFiles:   untrackedFiles,
   131→		TranscriptOffset: transcriptOffset,
   132→	}
   133→
   134→	data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(state, "", "  ")
   135→	if err != nil {
   136→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal state: %w", err)
   137→	}
   138→
   139→	if err := os.WriteFile(stateFile, data, 0o600); err != nil {
   140→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write state file: %w", err)
   141→	}
   142→
   143→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Captured state before prompt: %d untracked files, transcript offset: %d\n",
   144→		len(untrackedFiles), transcriptOffset)
   145→	return nil
   146→}
   147→

   149→// Returns nil if no state file exists.
   150→func LoadPrePromptState(sessionID string) (*PrePromptState, error) {

     1→//go:generate go run gen_state_diagram.go
     2→
     3→package session
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"fmt"
     7→	"strings"
     8→	"time"
     9→)
    10→
    11→// Phase represents the lifecycle stage of a session.
    12→type Phase string
    13→
    14→const (
    15→	PhaseActive Phase = "active"
    16→	PhaseIdle   Phase = "idle"
    17→	PhaseEnded  Phase = "ended"
    18→)
    19→
    20→// allPhases is the canonical list of phases for enumeration (e.g., diagram generation).
    21→var allPhases = []Phase{PhaseIdle, PhaseActive, PhaseEnded}
    22→
    23→// PhaseFromString normalizes a phase string, treating empty or unknown values
    24→// as PhaseIdle for backward compatibility with pre-state-machine session files.
    25→func PhaseFromString(s string) Phase {
    26→	switch Phase(s) {
    27→	case PhaseActive, "active_committed":
    28→		// "active_committed" was removed with the 1:1 checkpoint model.
    29→		// It previously meant "agent active + commit happened during turn".
    30→		// Normalize to ACTIVE so HandleTurnEnd can finalize any pending checkpoints.
    31→		return PhaseActive
    32→	case PhaseIdle:
    33→		return PhaseIdle
    34→	case PhaseEnded:
    35→		return PhaseEnded
    36→	default:
    37→		// Backward compat: truly unknown phases normalize to idle.
    38→		return PhaseIdle
    39→	}
    40→}
    41→
    42→// IsActive reports whether the phase represents an active agent turn.
    43→func (p Phase) IsActive() bool {
    44→	return p == PhaseActive
    45→}
    46→
    47→// Event represents something that happened to a session.
    48→type Event int
    49→
    50→const (
    51→	EventTurnStart    Event = iota // Agent begins working on a prompt
    52→	EventTurnEnd                   // Agent finishes its turn
    53→	EventGitCommit                 // A git commit was made (PostCommit hook)
    54→	EventSessionStart              // Session process started (SessionStart hook)
    55→	EventSessionStop               // Session process ended (SessionStop hook)
    56→)
    57→
    58→// allEvents is the canonical list of events for enumeration.
    59→var allEvents = []Event{EventTurnStart, EventTurnEnd, EventGitCommit, EventSessionStart, EventSessionStop}
    60→
    61→// String returns a human-readable name for the event.
    62→func (e Event) String() string {
    63→	switch e {
    64→	case EventTurnStart:
    65→		return "TurnStart"
    66→	case EventTurnEnd:
    67→		return "TurnEnd"
    68→	case EventGitCommit:
    69→		return "GitCommit"
    70→	case EventSessionStart:
    71→		return "SessionStart"
    72→	case EventSessionStop:
    73→		return "SessionStop"
    74→	default:
    75→		return fmt.Sprintf("Event(%d)", int(e))
    76→	}
    77→}
    78→
    79→// Action represents a side effect that should be performed after a transition.
    80→// The caller is responsible for executing these -- the state machine only declares them.
    81→type Action int
    82→
    83→const (
    84→	ActionCondense               Action = iota // Condense session data to permanent storage
    85→	ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched               // Condense only if FilesTouched is non-empty
    86→	ActionDiscardIfNoFiles                     // Discard session if FilesTouched is empty
    87→	ActionWarnStaleSession                     // Warn user about stale session(s)
    88→	ActionClearEndedAt                         // Clear EndedAt timestamp (session re-entering)
    89→	ActionUpdateLastInteraction                // Update LastInteractionTime
    90→)
    91→
    92→// String returns a human-readable name for the action.
    93→func (a Action) String() string {
    94→	switch a {
    95→	case ActionCondense:
    96→		return "Condense"
    97→	case ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched:
    98→		return "CondenseIfFilesTouched"
    99→	case ActionDiscardIfNoFiles:
   100→		return "DiscardIfNoFiles"

   403→// handleClaudeCodePostTodo handles the PostToolUse[TodoWrite] hook for subagent checkpoints.
   404→// Creates a checkpoint if we're in a subagent context (active pre-task file exists).
   405→// Skips silently if not in subagent context (main agent).
   406→func handleClaudeCodePostTodo() error {
   407→	input, err := parseSubagentCheckpointHookInput(os.Stdin)
   408→	if err != nil {
   409→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PostToolUse[TodoWrite] input: %w", err)
   410→	}
   411→
   412→	// Get agent for logging context
   413→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   414→	if err != nil {
   415→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   416→	}
   417→
   418→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   419→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-todo",
   420→		slog.String("hook", "post-todo"),
   421→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   422→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   423→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.TranscriptPath),
   424→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   425→	)
   426→
   427→	// Check if we're in a subagent context by looking for an active pre-task file
   428→	taskToolUseID, found := FindActivePreTaskFile()
   429→	if !found {
   430→		// Not in subagent context - this is a main agent TodoWrite, skip
   431→		return nil
   432→	}
   433→
   434→	// Skip on default branch to avoid polluting main/master history
   435→	if skip, branchName := ShouldSkipOnDefaultBranch(); skip {
   436→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping incremental checkpoint on branch '%s'\n", branchName)
   437→		return nil
   438→	}
   439→
   440→	// Detect file changes since last checkpoint
   441→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(nil)
   442→	if err != nil {
   443→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to detect changed files: %v\n", err)
   444→		return nil
   445→	}
   446→
   447→	// If no file changes, skip creating a checkpoint
   448→	if len(changes.Modified) == 0 && len(changes.New) == 0 && len(changes.Deleted) == 0 {
   449→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping incremental checkpoint\n")
   450→		return nil
   451→	}
   452→
   453→	// Get git author
   454→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   455→	if err != nil {
   456→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to get git author: %v\n", err)
   457→		return nil
   458→	}
   459→
   460→	// Get the active strategy
   461→	strat := GetStrategy()
   462→
   463→	// Get the session ID from the transcript path or input, then transform to Entire session ID
   464→	sessionID := input.SessionID
   465→	if sessionID == "" {
   466→		sessionID = paths.ExtractSessionIDFromTranscriptPath(input.TranscriptPath)
   467→	}
   468→
   469→	// Get next checkpoint sequence
   470→	seq := GetNextCheckpointSequence(sessionID, taskToolUseID)
   471→
   472→	// Extract the todo content from the tool_input.
   473→	// PostToolUse receives the NEW todo list where the just-completed work is
   474→	// marked as "completed". The last completed item is the work that was just done.
   475→	todoContent := ExtractLastCompletedTodoFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)
   476→	if todoContent == "" {
   477→		// No completed items - this is likely the first TodoWrite (planning phase).
   478→		// Check if there are any todos at all to avoid duplicate messages.
   479→		todoCount := CountTodosFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)
   480→		if todoCount > 0 {
   481→			// Use "Planning: N todos" format for the first TodoWrite
   482→			todoContent = fmt.Sprintf("Planning: %d todos", todoCount)
   483→		}
   484→		// If todoCount == 0, todoContent remains empty and FormatIncrementalMessage
   485→		// will fall back to "Checkpoint #N" format
   486→	}
   487→
   488→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   489→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   490→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   491→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   492→	}
   493→
   494→	// Build incremental checkpoint context
   495→	ctx := strategy.TaskCheckpointContext{
   496→		SessionID:           sessionID,
   497→		ToolUseID:           taskToolUseID,
   498→		ModifiedFiles:       changes.Modified,
   499→		NewFiles:            changes.New,
   500→		DeletedFiles:        changes.Deleted,
   501→		TranscriptPath:      input.TranscriptPath,
   502→		AuthorName:          author.Name,
   503→		AuthorEmail:         author.Email,
   504→		IsIncremental:       true,
   505→		IncrementalSequence: seq,
   506→		IncrementalType:     input.ToolName,
   507→		IncrementalData:     input.ToolInput,
   508→		TodoContent:         todoContent,
   509→		AgentType:           agentType,
   510→	}
   511→
   512→	// Save incremental checkpoint
   513→	if err := strat.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {
   514→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to save incremental checkpoint: %v\n", err)
   515→		return nil
   516→	}
   517→
   518→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Created incremental checkpoint #%d for %s (task: %s)\n",
   519→		seq, input.ToolName, taskToolUseID[:min(12, len(taskToolUseID))])
   520→	return nil
   521→}

    31→// parseAndLogHookInput parses the hook input and sets up logging context.
    32→func parseAndLogHookInput() (*hookInputData, error) {
    33→	// Get the agent from the hook command context (e.g., "entire hooks claude-code ...")

    45→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())

    61→		input:     input,
    62→		sessionID: sessionID,
    63→	}, nil
    64→}

    92→		}
    93→	}

    95→	return nil

  "content": "// hooks_claudecode_posttodo.go contains the PostTodo hook handler for Claude Code.\n// This is a Claude-specific hook that creates incremental checkpoints during subagent execution.\n// It's not part of the generic lifecycle dispatcher because it requires special handling:\n// - Only fires for TodoWrite tool invocations\n// - Creates incremental checkpoints (not full checkpoints)\n// - Only activates when in subagent context (pre-task file exists)\npackage cli\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"log/slog\"\n\t\"os\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy\"\n)\n\n// handleClaudeCodePostTodo handles the PostToolUse[TodoWrite] hook for subagent checkpoints.\n// Creates a checkpoint if we're in a subagent context (active pre-task file exists).\n// Skips silently if not in subagent context (main agent).\nfunc handleClaudeCodePostTodo() error {\n\tinput, err := parseSubagentCheckpointHookInput(os.Stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse PostToolUse[TodoWrite] input: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Get agent for logging context\n\tag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get agent: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tlogCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), \"hooks\"), ag.Name())\n\tlogging.Info(logCtx, \"post-todo\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"hook\", \"post-todo\"),\n\t\tslog.String(\"hook_type\", \"subagent\"),\n\t\tslog.String(\"model_session_id\", input.SessionID),\n\t\tslog.String(\"transcript_path\", input.TranscriptPath),\n\t\tslog.String(\"tool_use_id\", input.ToolUseID),\n\t)\n\n\t// Check if we're in a subagent context by looking for an active pre-task file\n\ttaskToolUseID, found := FindActivePreTaskFile()\n\tif !found {\n\t\t// Not in subagent context - this is a main agent TodoWrite, skip\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Skip on default branch to avoid polluting main/master history\n\tif skip, branchName := ShouldSkipOnDefaultBranch(); skip {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Entire: skipping incremental checkpoint on branch '%s'\\n\", branchName)\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Detect file changes since last checkpoint\n\tchanges, err := DetectFileChanges(nil)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to detect changed files: %v\\n\", err)\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// If no file changes, skip creating a checkpoint\n\tif len(changes.Modified) == 0 && len(changes.New) == 0 && len(changes.Deleted) == 0 {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[entire] No file changes detected, skipping incremental checkpoint\\n\")\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Get git author\n\tauthor, err := GetGitAuthor()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to get git author: %v\\n\", err)\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Get the active strategy\n\tstrat := GetStrategy()\n\n\t// Get the session ID from the transcript path or input, then transform to Entire session ID\n\tsessionID := input.SessionID\n\tif sessionID == \"\" {\n\t\tsessionID = paths.ExtractSessionIDFromTranscriptPath(input.TranscriptPath)\n\t}\n\n\t// Get next checkpoint sequence\n\tseq := GetNextCheckpointSequence(sessionID, taskToolUseID)\n\n\t// Extract the todo content from the tool_input.\n\t// PostToolUse receives the NEW todo list where the just-completed work is\n\t// marked as \"completed\". The last completed item is the work that was just done.\n\ttodoContent := ExtractLastCompletedTodoFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)\n\tif todoContent == \"\" {\n\t\t// No completed items - this is likely the first TodoWrite (planning phase).\n\t\t// Check if there are any todos at all to avoid duplicate messages.\n\t\ttodoCount := CountTodosFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)\n\t\tif todoCount > 0 {\n\t\t\t// Use \"Planning: N todos\" format for the first TodoWrite\n\t\t\ttodoContent = fmt.Sprintf(\"Planning: %d todos\", todoCount)\n\t\t}\n\t\t// If todoCount == 0, todoContent remains empty and FormatIncrementalMessage\n\t\t// will fall back to \"Checkpoint #N\" format\n\t}\n\n\t// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)\n\tvar agentType agent.AgentType\n\tif hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {\n\t\tagentType = hookAgent.Type()\n\t}\n\n\t// Build incremental checkpoint context\n\tctx := strategy.TaskCheckpointContext{\n\t\tSessionID:           sessionID,\n\t\tToolUseID:           taskToolUseID,\n\t\tModifiedFiles:       changes.Modified,\n\t\tNewFiles:            changes.New,\n\t\tDeletedFiles:        changes.Deleted,\n\t\tTranscriptPath:      input.TranscriptPath,\n\t\tAuthorName:          author.Name,\n\t\tAuthorEmail:         author.Email,\n\t\tIsIncremental:       true,\n\t\tIncrementalSequence: seq,\n\t\tIncrementalType:     input.ToolName,\n\t\tIncrementalData:     input.ToolInput,\n\t\tTodoContent:         todoContent,\n\t\tAgentType:           agentType,\n\t}\n\n\t// Save incremental checkpoint\n\tif err := strat.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to save incremental checkpoint: %v\\n\", err)\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[entire] Created incremental checkpoint #%d for %s (task: %s)\\n\",\n\t\tseq, input.ToolName, taskToolUseID[:min(12, len(taskToolUseID))])\n\treturn nil\n}\n"
}

    20→// HookHandlerFunc is a function that handles a specific hook event.
    21→type HookHandlerFunc func() error
    22→
    23→// hookRegistry maps (agentName, hookName) to handler functions.
    24→// This allows agents to define their hook vocabulary while keeping
    25→// handler logic in the CLI package (avoiding circular dependencies).
    26→var hookRegistry = map[agent.AgentName]map[string]HookHandlerFunc{}
    27→
    28→// RegisterHookHandler registers a handler for an agent's hook.
    29→func RegisterHookHandler(agentName agent.AgentName, hookName string, handler HookHandlerFunc) {
    30→	if hookRegistry[agentName] == nil {
    31→		hookRegistry[agentName] = make(map[string]HookHandlerFunc)
    32→	}
    33→	hookRegistry[agentName][hookName] = handler
    34→}
    35→
    36→// GetHookHandler returns the handler for an agent's hook, or nil if not found.
    37→func GetHookHandler(agentName agent.AgentName, hookName string) HookHandlerFunc {
    38→	if handlers, ok := hookRegistry[agentName]; ok {
    39→		return handlers[hookName]
    40→	}
    41→	return nil
    42→}

   195→
   196→// agentHookLogCleanup stores the cleanup function for agent hook logging.
   197→// Set by PersistentPreRunE, called by PersistentPostRunE.
   198→var agentHookLogCleanup func()
   199→
   200→// currentHookAgentName stores the agent name for the currently executing hook.
   201→// Set by newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging before calling the handler.
   202→// This allows handlers to know which agent invoked the hook without guessing.
   203→var currentHookAgentName agent.AgentName
   204→
   205→// GetCurrentHookAgent returns the agent for the currently executing hook.
   206→// Returns the agent based on the hook command structure (e.g., "entire hooks claude-code ...")
   207→// rather than guessing from directory presence.
   208→// Falls back to GetAgent() if not in a hook context.
   209→//
   210→
   211→func GetCurrentHookAgent() (agent.Agent, error) {
   212→	if currentHookAgentName == "" {
   213→		return nil, errors.New("not in a hook context: agent name not set")
   214→	}
   215→
   216→	ag, err := agent.Get(currentHookAgentName)
   217→	if err != nil {
   218→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("getting hook agent %q: %w", currentHookAgentName, err)
   219→	}
   220→	return ag, nil
   221→}
   222→
   223→// newAgentHooksCmd creates a hooks subcommand for an agent that implements HookHandler.
   224→// It dynamically creates subcommands for each hook the agent supports.
   225→func newAgentHooksCmd(agentName agent.AgentName, handler agent.HookHandler) *cobra.Command {
   226→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
   227→		Use:    string(agentName),
   228→		Short:  handler.Description() + " hook handlers",
   229→		Hidden: true,
   230→		PersistentPreRunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   231→			agentHookLogCleanup = initHookLogging()
   232→			return nil
   233→		},
   234→		PersistentPostRunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   235→			if agentHookLogCleanup != nil {
   236→				agentHookLogCleanup()
   237→			}
   238→			return nil
   239→		},
   240→	}
   241→
   242→	for _, hookName := range handler.GetHookNames() {
   243→		cmd.AddCommand(newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging(agentName, hookName))
   244→	}
   245→
   246→	return cmd
   247→}
   248→
   249→// getHookType returns the hook type based on the hook name.
   250→// Returns "subagent" for task-related hooks (pre-task, post-task, post-todo),
   251→// "tool" for tool-related hooks (before-tool, after-tool),
   252→// "agent" for all other agent hooks.
   253→func getHookType(hookName string) string {
   254→	switch hookName {
   255→	case claudecode.HookNamePreTask, claudecode.HookNamePostTask, claudecode.HookNamePostTodo:
   256→		return "subagent"
   257→	case geminicli.HookNameBeforeTool, geminicli.HookNameAfterTool:
   258→		return "tool"
   259→	default:
   260→		return "agent"
   261→	}
   262→}
   263→
   264→// newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging creates a command for a specific hook verb with structured logging.
   265→// It uses the lifecycle dispatcher (ParseHookEvent → DispatchLifecycleEvent) as the primary path,
   266→// falling back to the legacy handler registry for hooks that return nil events (e.g., PostTodo).
   267→func newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging(agentName agent.AgentName, hookName string) *cobra.Command {
   268→	return &cobra.Command{
   269→		Use:    hookName,
   270→		Hidden: true,
   271→		Short:  "Called on " + hookName,
   272→		RunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   273→			// Skip silently if not in a git repository - hooks shouldn't prevent the agent from working
   274→			if _, err := paths.RepoRoot(); err != nil {
   275→				return nil
   276→			}
   277→
   278→			// Skip if Entire is not enabled
   279→			enabled, err := IsEnabled()
   280→			if err == nil && !enabled {
   281→				return nil
   282→			}
   283→
   284→			start := time.Now()
   285→
   286→			// Initialize logging context with agent name
   287→			ctx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), agentName)
   288→
   289→			// Get strategy name for logging
   290→			strategyName := unknownStrategyName //nolint:ineffassign,wastedassign // already present in codebase
   291→			strategyName = GetStrategy().Name()
   292→
   293→			hookType := getHookType(hookName)
   294→
   295→			logging.Debug(ctx, "hook invoked",
   296→				slog.String("hook", hookName),
   297→				slog.String("hook_type", hookType),
   298→				slog.String("strategy", strategyName),
   299→			)
   300→
   301→			// Set the current hook agent so legacy handlers can retrieve it
   302→			currentHookAgentName = agentName
   303→			defer func() { currentHookAgentName = "" }()
   304→
   305→			// Try the lifecycle dispatcher path: ParseHookEvent → DispatchLifecycleEvent.
   306→			// Falls back to legacy handler registry if agent is unresolved or event is nil.
   307→			var hookErr error
   308→			ag, agentErr := agent.Get(agentName)
   309→			if agentErr == nil {
   310→				event, parseErr := ag.ParseHookEvent(hookName, os.Stdin)
   311→				if parseErr != nil {
   312→					return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook event: %w", parseErr)
   313→				}
   314→				if event != nil {
   315→					// Lifecycle event — use the generic dispatcher
   316→					hookErr = DispatchLifecycleEvent(ag, event)
   317→				} else {
   318→					// No lifecycle event — fall back to registered handler (e.g., PostTodo)
   319→					handler := GetHookHandler(agentName, hookName)
   320→					if handler != nil {
   321→						hookErr = handler()
   322→					}
   323→				}
   324→			} else {
   325→				// Agent not found — fall back to legacy handler registry
   326→				handler := GetHookHandler(agentName, hookName)
   327→				if handler == nil {
   328→					return fmt.Errorf("no handler for %s/%s and agent not found: %w", agentName, hookName, agentErr)
   329→				}
   330→				hookErr = handler()
   331→			}
   332→
   333→			logging.LogDuration(ctx, slog.LevelDebug, "hook completed", start,
   334→				slog.String("hook", hookName),
   335→				slog.String("hook_type", hookType),
   336→				slog.String("strategy", strategyName),
   337→				slog.Bool("success", hookErr == nil),
   338→			)
   339→
   340→			return hookErr
   341→		},
   342→	}
   343→}

  "content": "// hook_registry.go provides hook command registration for agents.\n// The lifecycle dispatcher (DispatchLifecycleEvent) handles all lifecycle events.\n// PostTodo is the only hook that's handled directly (not via lifecycle dispatcher).\npackage cli\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"log/slog\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths\"\n\n\t\"github.com/spf13/cobra\"\n)\n\n// agentHookLogCleanup stores the cleanup function for agent hook logging.\n// Set by PersistentPreRunE, called by PersistentPostRunE.\nvar agentHookLogCleanup func()\n\n// currentHookAgentName stores the agent name for the currently executing hook.\n// Set by newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging before calling the handler.\n// This allows handlers to know which agent invoked the hook without guessing.\nvar currentHookAgentName agent.AgentName\n\n// GetCurrentHookAgent returns the agent for the currently executing hook.\n// Returns the agent based on the hook command structure (e.g., \"entire hooks claude-code ...\")\n// rather than guessing from directory presence.\n// Falls back to GetAgent() if not in a hook context.\nfunc GetCurrentHookAgent() (agent.Agent, error) {\n\tif currentHookAgentName == \"\" {\n\t\treturn nil, errors.New(\"not in a hook context: agent name not set\")\n\t}\n\n\tag, err := agent.Get(currentHookAgentName)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"getting hook agent %q: %w\", currentHookAgentName, err)\n\t}\n\treturn ag, nil\n}\n\n// newAgentHooksCmd creates a hooks subcommand for an agent that implements HookHandler.\n// It dynamically creates subcommands for each hook the agent supports.\nfunc newAgentHooksCmd(agentName agent.AgentName, handler agent.HookHandler) *cobra.Command {\n\tcmd := &cobra.Command{\n\t\tUse:    string(agentName),\n\t\tShort:  handler.Description() + \" hook handlers\",\n\t\tHidden: true,\n\t\tPersistentPreRunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {\n\t\t\tagentHookLogCleanup = initHookLogging()\n\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t},\n\t\tPersistentPostRunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {\n\t\t\tif agentHookLogCleanup != nil {\n\t\t\t\tagentHookLogCleanup()\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, hookName := range handler.GetHookNames() {\n\t\tcmd.AddCommand(newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging(agentName, hookName))\n\t}\n\n\treturn cmd\n}\n\n// getHookType returns the hook type based on the hook name.\n// Returns \"subagent\" for task-related hooks (pre-task, post-task, post-todo),\n// \"tool\" for tool-related hooks (before-tool, after-tool),\n// \"agent\" for all other agent hooks.\nfunc getHookType(hookName string) string {\n\tswitch hookName {\n\tcase claudecode.HookNamePreTask, claudecode.HookNamePostTask, claudecode.HookNamePostTodo:\n\t\treturn \"subagent\"\n\tcase geminicli.HookNameBeforeTool, geminicli.HookNameAfterTool:\n\t\treturn \"tool\"\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn \"agent\"\n\t}\n}\n\n// newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging creates a command for a specific hook verb with structured logging.\n// It uses the lifecycle dispatcher (ParseHookEvent → DispatchLifecycleEvent) as the primary path.\n// PostTodo is handled directly as it's Claude-specific and not part of the lifecycle dispatcher.\nfunc newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging(agentName agent.AgentName, hookName string) *cobra.Command {\n\treturn &cobra.Command{\n\t\tUse:    hookName,\n\t\tHidden: true,\n\t\tShort:  \"Called on \" + hookName,\n\t\tRunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {\n\t\t\t// Skip silently if not in a git repository - hooks shouldn't prevent the agent from working\n\t\t\tif _, err := paths.RepoRoot(); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// Skip if Entire is not enabled\n\t\t\tenabled, err := IsEnabled()\n\t\t\tif err == nil && !enabled {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tstart := time.Now()\n\n\t\t\t// Initialize logging context with agent name\n\t\t\tctx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), \"hooks\"), agentName)\n\n\t\t\t// Get strategy name for logging\n\t\t\tstrategyName := unknownStrategyName //nolint:ineffassign,wastedassign // already present in codebase\n\t\t\tstrategyName = GetStrategy().Name()\n\n\t\t\thookType := getHookType(hookName)\n\n\t\t\tlogging.Debug(ctx, \"hook invoked\",\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"hook\", hookName),\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"hook_type\", hookType),\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"strategy\", strategyName),\n\t\t\t)\n\n\t\t\t// Set the current hook agent so handlers can retrieve it\n\t\t\tcurrentHookAgentName = agentName\n\t\t\tdefer func() { currentHookAgentName = \"\" }()\n\n\t\t\t// Use the lifecycle dispatcher for all hooks\n\t\t\tvar hookErr error\n\t\t\tag, agentErr := agent.Get(agentName)\n\t\t\tif agentErr != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get agent %q: %w\", agentName, agentErr)\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tevent, parseErr := ag.ParseHookEvent(hookName, os.Stdin)\n\t\t\tif parseErr != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse hook event: %w\", parseErr)\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tif event != nil {\n\t\t\t\t// Lifecycle event — use the generic dispatcher\n\t\t\t\thookErr = DispatchLifecycleEvent(ag, event)\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\t// No lifecycle event — handle special cases directly\n\t\t\t\t// PostTodo is Claude-specific: creates incremental checkpoints during subagent execution\n\t\t\t\tif agentName == agent.AgentNameClaudeCode && hookName == claudecode.HookNamePostTodo {\n\t\t\t\t\thookErr = handleClaudeCodePostTodo()\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t// Other pass-through hooks (nil event, no special handling) are no-ops\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tlogging.LogDuration(ctx, slog.LevelDebug, \"hook completed\", start,\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"hook\", hookName),\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"hook_type\", hookType),\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"strategy\", strategyName),\n\t\t\t\tslog.Bool(\"success\", hookErr == nil),\n\t\t\t)\n\n\t\t\treturn hookErr\n\t\t},\n\t}\n}\n"
}

    28→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
    29→	switch hookName {
    30→	case HookNameSessionStart:
    31→		return g.parseSessionStart(stdin)
    32→	case HookNameBeforeAgent:
    33→		return g.parseTurnStart(stdin)
    34→	case HookNameAfterAgent:
    35→		return g.parseTurnEnd(stdin)
    36→	case HookNameSessionEnd:
    37→		return g.parseSessionEnd(stdin)
    38→	case HookNamePreCompress:
    39→		return g.parseCompaction(stdin)
    40→	case HookNameBeforeTool, HookNameAfterTool, HookNameBeforeModel,
    41→		HookNameAfterModel, HookNameBeforeToolSelection, HookNameNotification:
    42→		// Acknowledged hooks with no lifecycle action
    43→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action
    44→	default:
    45→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // Unknown hooks have no lifecycle action
    46→	}
    47→}
    48→
    49→// ReadTranscript reads the raw JSON transcript bytes for a session.
    50→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {
    51→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    52→	if err != nil {
    53→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    54→	}
    55→	return data, nil
    56→}

    25→func TestNewAgentHookVerbCmd_LogsInvocation(t *testing.T) {
    26→	// Setup: Create a temp directory with git repo structure
    27→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
    28→	t.Chdir(tmpDir)
    29→
    30→	// Initialize git repo (required for paths.RepoRoot to work)
    31→	gitInit := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), "git", "init")
    32→	if err := gitInit.Run(); err != nil {
    33→		t.Fatalf("failed to init git repo: %v", err)
    34→	}
    35→
    36→	// Create .entire directory
    37→	entireDir := filepath.Join(tmpDir, paths.EntireDir)
    38→	if err := os.MkdirAll(entireDir, 0o755); err != nil {
    39→		t.Fatalf("failed to create .entire directory: %v", err)
    40→	}
    41→
    42→	// Create logs directory
    43→	logsDir := filepath.Join(entireDir, "logs")
    44→	if err := os.MkdirAll(logsDir, 0o755); err != nil {
    45→		t.Fatalf("failed to create logs directory: %v", err)
    46→	}
    47→
    48→	// Create session state file in .git/entire-sessions/
    49→	sessionID := "test-claudecode-hook-session"
    50→	writeTestSessionState(t, tmpDir, sessionID)
    51→
    52→	// Enable debug logging
    53→	t.Setenv(logging.LogLevelEnvVar, "DEBUG")
    54→
    55→	// Initialize logging (normally done by PersistentPreRunE)
    56→	cleanup := initHookLogging()
    57→	defer cleanup()
    58→
    59→	// Register a test handler
    60→	testHandlerCalled := false
    61→	RegisterHookHandler(agent.AgentName("test-agent"), "test-hook", func() error {
    62→		testHandlerCalled = true
    63→		return nil
    64→	})
    65→
    66→	// Create the command with logging
    67→	cmd := newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging(agent.AgentName("test-agent"), "test-hook")
    68→
    69→	// Execute the command
    70→	err := cmd.Execute()
    71→	if err != nil {
    72→		t.Fatalf("command execution failed: %v", err)
    73→	}
    74→
    75→	if !testHandlerCalled {
    76→		t.Error("expected test handler to be called")
    77→	}
    78→
    79→	// Close logging to flush
    80→	cleanup()
    81→
    82→	// Verify log file was created and contains expected content
    83→	logFile := filepath.Join(logsDir, "entire.log")
    84→	content, err := os.ReadFile(logFile)
    85→	if err != nil {
    86→		t.Fatalf("failed to read log file: %v", err)
    87→	}
    88→
    89→	logContent := string(content)
    90→	t.Logf("log content: %s", logContent)
    91→
    92→	// Parse each log line as JSON
    93→	lines := strings.Split(strings.TrimSpace(logContent), "\n")
    94→	if len(lines) == 0 {
    95→		t.Fatal("expected at least one log line")
    96→	}
    97→
    98→	// Check for hook invocation log
    99→	foundInvocation := false
   100→	foundCompletion := false
   101→	for _, line := range lines {
   102→		var entry map[string]interface{}
   103→		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry); err != nil {
   104→			t.Errorf("failed to parse log line as JSON: %v", err)
   105→			continue
   106→		}
   107→
   108→		if entry["hook"] == "test-hook" {
   109→			msg, msgOK := entry["msg"].(string)
   110→			if !msgOK {
   111→				continue
   112→			}
   113→			if strings.Contains(msg, "invoked") {
   114→				foundInvocation = true
   115→				// Verify component is set
   116→				if entry["component"] != "hooks" {
   117→					t.Errorf("expected component='hooks', got %v", entry["component"])
   118→				}
   119→				// Verify session_id is set
   120→				if entry["session_id"] != sessionID {
   121→					t.Errorf("expected session_id=%q, got %v", sessionID, entry["session_id"])
   122→				}
   123→			}
   124→			if strings.Contains(msg, "completed") {
   125→				foundCompletion = true
   126→				// Verify duration_ms is present
   127→				if _, ok := entry["duration_ms"]; !ok {
   128→					t.Error("expected duration_ms in completion log")
   129→				}
   130→				// Verify success status
   131→				if entry["success"] != true {
   132→					t.Errorf("expected success=true, got %v", entry["success"])
   133→				}
   134→			}
   135→		}
   136→	}

   331→// writeTestSessionState creates a session state file in .git/entire-sessions/ for testing.
   332→func writeTestSessionState(t *testing.T, repoDir, sessionID string) {
   333→	t.Helper()

   336→		t.Fatalf("failed to create session state directory: %v", err)
   337→	}
   338→
   339→	now := time.Now()
   340→	state := session.State{
   341→		SessionID:           sessionID,
   342→		StartedAt:           now,
   343→		LastInteractionTime: &now,
   344→		Phase:               session.PhaseActive,
   345→	}
   346→	data, err := json.Marshal(state)
   347→	if err != nil {
   348→		t.Fatalf("failed to marshal state: %v", err)
   349→	}
   350→	stateFile := filepath.Join(stateDir, sessionID+".json")
   351→	if err := os.WriteFile(stateFile, data, 0o600); err != nil {
   352→		t.Fatalf("failed to write session state file: %v", err)
   353→	}
   354→	t.Cleanup(func() { os.Remove(stateFile) })
   355→}

   101→	// Execute the command
   102→	err := cmd.Execute()
   103→	if err != nil {
   104→		t.Fatalf("command execution failed: %v", err)
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	// Close logging to flush
   108→	cleanup()
   109→
   110→	// Verify log file was created and contains expected content
   111→	logFile := filepath.Join(logsDir, "entire.log")
   112→	content, err := os.ReadFile(logFile)
   113→	if err != nil {
   114→		t.Fatalf("failed to read log file: %v", err)
   115→	}
   116→
   117→	logContent := string(content)
   118→	t.Logf("log content: %s", logContent)
   119→
   120→	// Parse each log line as JSON
   121→	lines := strings.Split(strings.TrimSpace(logContent), "\n")
   122→	if len(lines) == 0 {
   123→		t.Fatal("expected at least one log line")
   124→	}
   125→
   126→	// Check for hook invocation log
   127→	foundInvocation := false
   128→	foundCompletion := false
   129→	for _, line := range lines {
   130→		var entry map[string]interface{}
   131→		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry); err != nil {
   132→			t.Errorf("failed to parse log line as JSON: %v", err)
   133→			continue
   134→		}
   135→
   136→		if entry["hook"] == claudecode.HookNameSessionStart {
   137→			msg, msgOK := entry["msg"].(string)
   138→			if !msgOK {
   139→				continue
   140→			}
   141→			if strings.Contains(msg, "invoked") {
   142→				foundInvocation = true
   143→				// Verify component is set
   144→				if entry["component"] != "hooks" {
   145→					t.Errorf("expected component='hooks', got %v", entry["component"])
   146→				}
   147→			}
   148→			if strings.Contains(msg, "completed") {
   149→				foundCompletion = true
   150→				// Verify duration_ms is present
   151→				if _, ok := entry["duration_ms"]; !ok {
   152→					t.Error("expected duration_ms in completion log")
   153→				}
   154→				// Verify success status
   155→				if entry["success"] != true {
   156→					t.Errorf("expected success=true, got %v", entry["success"])
   157→				}
   158→			}
   159→		}
   160→	}
   161→
   162→	if !foundInvocation {
   163→		t.Error("expected to find hook invocation log")
   164→	}
   165→	if !foundCompletion {
   166→		t.Error("expected to find hook completion log")
   167→	}
   168→}

   170→func TestClaudeCodeHooksCmd_HasLoggingHooks(t *testing.T) {
   171→	// This test verifies that the claude-code hooks command has PersistentPreRunE
   172→	// and PersistentPostRunE for logging initialization and cleanup
   173→
   174→	// Get the actual hooks command which contains the claude-code subcommand
   175→	hooksCmd := newHooksCmd()
   176→
   177→	// Find the claude-code subcommand
   178→	var claudeCodeCmd *cobra.Command
   179→	for _, sub := range hooksCmd.Commands() {
   180→		if sub.Use == testAgentName {
   181→			claudeCodeCmd = sub
   182→			break
   183→		}
   184→	}
   185→
   186→	if claudeCodeCmd == nil {
   187→		t.Fatal("expected to find claude-code subcommand under hooks")
   188→	}
   189→
   190→	// Verify PersistentPreRunE is set
   191→	if claudeCodeCmd.PersistentPreRunE == nil {
   192→		t.Error("expected PersistentPreRunE to be set for logging initialization")
   193→	}
   194→
   195→	// Verify PersistentPostRunE is set
   196→	if claudeCodeCmd.PersistentPostRunE == nil {
   197→		t.Error("expected PersistentPostRunE to be set for logging cleanup")
   198→	}
   199→}
   200→

   305→// writeTestSessionState creates a session state file in .git/entire-sessions/ for testing.
   306→func writeTestSessionState(t *testing.T, repoDir, sessionID string) {
   307→	t.Helper()
   308→	stateDir := filepath.Join(repoDir, ".git", session.SessionStateDirName)
   309→	if err := os.MkdirAll(stateDir, 0o755); err != nil {
   310→		t.Fatalf("failed to create session state directory: %v", err)
   311→	}
   312→
   313→	now := time.Now()
   314→	state := session.State{
   315→		SessionID:           sessionID,
   316→		StartedAt:           now,
   317→		LastInteractionTime: &now,
   318→		Phase:               session.PhaseActive,
   319→	}
   320→	data, err := json.Marshal(state)
   321→	if err != nil {
   322→		t.Fatalf("failed to marshal state: %v", err)
   323→	}
   324→	stateFile := filepath.Join(stateDir, sessionID+".json")
   325→	if err := os.WriteFile(stateFile, data, 0o600); err != nil {
   326→		t.Fatalf("failed to write session state file: %v", err)
   327→	}
   328→	t.Cleanup(func() { os.Remove(stateFile) })
   329→}
