   607→// createContextFile creates a context.md file for the session checkpoint.
   608→// This is a unified version that works for all agents.
   609→func createContextFile(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID string, prompts []string, summary string) error {
   610→	var sb strings.Builder
   611→
   612→	sb.WriteString("# Session Context\n\n")
   613→	sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("Session ID: %s\n", sessionID))
   614→	sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("Commit Message: %s\n\n", commitMessage))
   615→
   616→	if len(prompts) > 0 {
   617→		sb.WriteString("## Prompts\n\n")
   618→		for i, p := range prompts {
   619→			sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("### Prompt %d\n\n%s\n\n", i+1, p))
   620→		}
   621→	}
   622→
   623→	if summary != "" {
   624→		sb.WriteString("## Summary\n\n")
   625→		sb.WriteString(summary)
   626→		sb.WriteString("\n")
   627→	}
   628→
   629→	if err := os.WriteFile(contextFile, []byte(sb.String()), 0o600); err != nil {
   630→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write context file: %w", err)
   631→	}
   632→	return nil
   633→}
   634→
   635→// parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID is a thin wrapper around transcript parsing for checkpoint UUID lookup.
   636→// Returns parsed transcript lines for use with FindCheckpointUUID.
   637→func parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID(transcriptPath string) ([]transcriptLine, int, error) {
   638→	lines, total, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(transcriptPath, 0)
   639→	if err != nil {
   640→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("parsing transcript for checkpoint UUID: %w", err)
   641→	}
   642→	return lines, total, nil
   643→}
   644→

  "new_string": "// parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID is a thin wrapper around transcript parsing for checkpoint UUID lookup.\n// Returns parsed transcript lines for use with FindCheckpointUUID.\nfunc parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID(transcriptPath string) ([]transcriptLine, int, error) {\n\tlines, total, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(transcriptPath, 0)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, 0, fmt.Errorf(\"parsing transcript for checkpoint UUID: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn lines, total, nil\n}\n\n// transitionSessionTurnEnd transitions the session phase to IDLE and dispatches turn-end actions.\nfunc transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID string) {\n\tturnState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)\n\tif loadErr != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to load session state for turn end: %v\\n\", loadErr)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif turnState == nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif err := strategy.TransitionAndLog(turnState, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); err != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: turn-end transition failed: %v\\n\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Always dispatch to strategy for turn-end handling. The strategy reads\n\t// work items from state (e.g. TurnCheckpointIDs), not the action list.\n\tstrat := GetStrategy()\n\tif handler, ok := strat.(strategy.TurnEndHandler); ok {\n\t\tif err := handler.HandleTurnEnd(turnState); err != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: turn-end action dispatch failed: %v\\n\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(turnState); updateErr != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to update session phase on turn end: %v\\n\", updateErr)\n\t}\n}\n\n// markSessionEnded transitions the session to ENDED phase via the state machine.\nfunc markSessionEnded(sessionID string) error {\n\tstate, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to load session state: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tif state == nil {\n\t\treturn nil // No state file, nothing to update\n\t}\n\n\tif transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStop, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: session stop transition failed: %v\\n\", transErr)\n\t}\n\n\tnow := time.Now()\n\tstate.EndedAt = &now\n\n\tif err := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to save session state: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// logFileChanges logs the files modified, created, and deleted during a session.\nfunc logFileChanges(modified, newFiles, deleted []string) {\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Files modified during session (%d):\\n\", len(modified))\n\tfor _, file := range modified {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"  - %s\\n\", file)\n\t}\n\tif len(newFiles) > 0 {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"New files created (%d):\\n\", len(newFiles))\n\t\tfor _, file := range newFiles {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"  - %s\\n\", file)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif len(deleted) > 0 {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Files deleted (%d):\\n\", len(deleted))\n\t\tfor _, file := range deleted {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"  - %s\\n\", file)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}"
}

nil),\n\t\t\t)\n\n\t\t\treturn hookErr\n\t\t},\n\t}",
  "new_string": "\treturn &cobra.Command{\n\t\tUse:    hookName,\n\t\tHidden: true,\n\t\tShort:  \"Called on \" + hookName,\n\t\tRunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {\n\t\t\t// Skip silently if not in a git repository - hooks shouldn't prevent the agent from working\n\t\t\tif _, err := paths.RepoRoot(); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// Skip if Entire is not enabled\n\t\t\tenabled, err := IsEnabled()\n\t\t\tif err == nil && !enabled {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tstart := time.Now()\n\n\t\t\t// Initialize logging context with agent name\n\t\t\tctx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), \"hooks\"), agentName)\n\n\t\t\t// Get strategy name for logging\n\t\t\tstrategyName := unknownStrategyName //nolint:ineffassign,wastedassign // already present in codebase\n\t\t\tstrategyName = GetStrategy().Name()\n\n\t\t\thookType := getHookType(hookName)\n\n\t\t\tlogging.Debug(ctx, \"hook invoked\",\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"hook\", hookName),\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"hook_type\", hookType),\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"strategy\", strategyName),\n\t\t\t)\n\n\t\t\t// Set the current hook agent so handlers can retrieve it\n\t\t\tcurrentHookAgentName = agentName\n\t\t\tdefer func() { currentHookAgentName = \"\" }()\n\n\t\t\t// Use the lifecycle dispatcher for all hooks\n\t\t\tvar hookErr error\n\t\t\tag, agentErr := agent.Get(agentName)\n\t\t\tif agentErr != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get agent %q: %w\", agentName, agentErr)\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// Use cmd.InOrStdin() to support testing with cmd.SetIn()\n\t\t\tevent, parseErr := ag.ParseHookEvent(hookName, cmd.InOrStdin())\n\t\t\tif parseErr != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse hook event: %w\", parseErr)\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tif event != nil {\n\t\t\t\t// Lifecycle event — use the generic dispatcher\n\t\t\t\thookErr = DispatchLifecycleEvent(ag, event)\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\t// No lifecycle event — handle special cases directly\n\t\t\t\t// PostTodo is Claude-specific: creates incremental checkpoints during subagent execution\n\t\t\t\tif agentName == agent.AgentNameClaudeCode && hookName == claudecode.HookNamePostTodo {\n\t\t\t\t\thookErr = handleClaudeCodePostTodo()\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t// Other pass-through hooks (nil event, no special handling) are no-ops\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tlogging.LogDuration(ctx, slog.LevelDebug, \"hook completed\", start,\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"hook\", hookName),\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"hook_type\", hookType),\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"strategy\", strategyName),\n\t\t\t\tslog.Bool(\"success\", hookErr == nil),\n\t\t\t)\n\n\t\t\treturn hookErr\n\t\t},\n\t}"
}

   240→// handleSessionStartCommon is the shared implementation for session start hooks.
   241→// Used by both Claude Code and Gemini CLI handlers.
   242→func handleSessionStartCommon() error {
   243→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   244→	if err != nil {
   245→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   246→	}
   247→
   248→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookSessionStart, os.Stdin)
   249→	if err != nil {
   250→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   251→	}
   252→
   253→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   254→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-start",
   255→		slog.String("hook", "session-start"),
   256→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   257→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   258→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
   259→	)
   260→
   261→	if input.SessionID == "" {
   262→		return errors.New("no session_id in input")
   263→	}
   264→
   265→	// Build informational message
   266→	message := "\n\nPowered by Entire:\n  This conversation will be linked to your next commit."
   267→
   268→	// Check for concurrent sessions and append count if any
   269→	strat := GetStrategy()
   270→	if concurrentChecker, ok := strat.(strategy.ConcurrentSessionChecker); ok {
   271→		if count, err := concurrentChecker.CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints(input.SessionID); err == nil && count > 0 {
   272→			message += fmt.Sprintf("\n  %d other active conversation(s) in this workspace will also be included.\n  Use 'entire status' for more information.", count)
   273→		}
   274→	}
   275→
   276→	// Output informational message using agent-specific format
   277→	if err := outputHookResponse(message); err != nil {
   278→		return err
   279→	}
   280→
   281→	// Fire EventSessionStart for the current session (if state exists).
   282→	// This handles ENDED → IDLE (re-entering a session).
   283→	// TODO(ENT-221): dispatch ActionWarnStaleSession for ACTIVE sessions.
   284→	if state, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(input.SessionID); loadErr != nil {
   285→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state on start: %v\n", loadErr)
   286→	} else if state != nil {
   287→		if transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStart, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
   288→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: session start transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
   289→		}
   290→		if saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); saveErr != nil {
   291→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state on start: %v\n", saveErr)
   292→		}
   293→	}
   294→
   295→	return nil
   296→}

   175→// extractLastAssistantMessage extracts the last text message from the assistant
   176→func extractLastAssistantMessage(transcript []transcriptLine) string {
   177→	for i := len(transcript) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   178→		if transcript[i].Type == transcriptTypeAssistant {
   179→			var msg assistantMessage
   180→			if err := json.Unmarshal(transcript[i].Message, &msg); err != nil {
   181→				continue
   182→			}
   183→
   184→			for _, block := range msg.Content {
   185→				if block.Type == contentTypeText && block.Text != "" {
   186→					return block.Text
   187→				}
   188→			}
   189→		}
   190→	}
   191→	return ""
   192→}
   193→
   194→// findLastUserUUID finds the UUID of the last user message (that has string content)
   195→func findLastUserUUID(transcript []transcriptLine) string {
   196→	for i := len(transcript) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   197→		if transcript[i].Type == transcriptTypeUser {
   198→			var msg userMessage
   199→			if err := json.Unmarshal(transcript[i].Message, &msg); err != nil {
   200→				continue
   201→			}
   202→			// Only count messages with string content (not tool results)
   203→			if _, ok := msg.Content.(string); ok {
   204→				return transcript[i].UUID
   205→			}
   206→		}
   207→	}
   208→	return ""
   209→}
   210→
   211→// filterTranscriptAfterUUID returns transcript lines after the given UUID
   212→func filterTranscriptAfterUUID(transcript []transcriptLine, uuid string) []transcriptLine {
   213→	if uuid == "" {
   214→		return transcript
   215→	}
   216→
   217→	foundIndex := -1
   218→	for i, line := range transcript {
   219→		if line.UUID == uuid {
   220→			foundIndex = i
   221→			break
   222→		}
   223→	}
   224→
   225→	if foundIndex == -1 || foundIndex == len(transcript)-1 {
   226→		return transcript
   227→	}
   228→
   229→	return transcript[foundIndex+1:]
   230→}
   231→
   232→// extractModifiedFiles extracts the list of files modified by tool calls
   233→func extractModifiedFiles(transcript []transcriptLine) []string {
   234→	fileSet := make(map[string]bool)
   235→	var files []string
   236→
   237→	for _, line := range transcript {
   238→		if line.Type == transcriptTypeAssistant {
   239→			var msg assistantMessage
   240→			if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   241→				continue
   242→			}
   243→
   244→			for _, block := range msg.Content {
   245→				if block.Type == contentTypeToolUse {
   246→					isModifyTool := false
   247→					for _, name := range claudecode.FileModificationTools {
   248→						if block.Name == name {
   249→							isModifyTool = true
   250→							break
   251→						}
   252→					}
   253→
   254→					if isModifyTool {
   255→						var input toolInput
   256→						if err := json.Unmarshal(block.Input, &input); err != nil {
   257→							continue
   258→						}
   259→
   260→						file := input.FilePath
   261→						if file == "" {
   262→							file = input.NotebookPath
   263→						}
   264→
   265→						if file != "" && !fileSet[file] {
   266→							fileSet[file] = true
   267→							files = append(files, file)
   268→						}
   269→					}
   270→				}
   271→			}
   272→		}
   273→	}
   274→
   275→	return files
   276→}

    11→// Phase represents the lifecycle stage of a session.

    22→
    23→// PhaseFromString normalizes a phase string, treating empty or unknown values
    24→// as PhaseIdle for backward compatibility with pre-state-machine session files.
    25→func PhaseFromString(s string) Phase {
    26→	switch Phase(s) {
    27→	case PhaseActive, "active_committed":
    28→		// "active_committed" was removed with the 1:1 checkpoint model.
    29→		// It previously meant "agent active + commit happened during turn".
    30→		// Normalize to ACTIVE so HandleTurnEnd can finalize any pending checkpoints.
    31→		return PhaseActive
    32→	case PhaseIdle:
    33→		return PhaseIdle
    34→	case PhaseEnded:
    35→		return PhaseEnded
    36→	default:
    37→		// Backward compat: truly unknown phases normalize to idle.
    38→		return PhaseIdle
    39→	}
    40→}
    41→
    42→// IsActive reports whether the phase represents an active agent turn.
    43→func (p Phase) IsActive() bool {
    44→	return p == PhaseActive
    45→}
    46→
    47→// Event represents something that happened to a session.
    48→type Event int

    61→// String returns a human-readable name for the event.

   370→// handleLifecycleCompaction handles context compaction: saves like TurnEnd,
   371→// then resets the transcript offset since the transcript may be truncated.
   372→func handleLifecycleCompaction(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   373→	// Compaction triggers the same save logic as TurnEnd
   374→	if err := handleLifecycleTurnEnd(ag, event); err != nil {
   375→		return err
   376→	}
   377→
   378→	// Reset transcript offset in session state since the transcript may be reorganized
   379→	sessionState, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(event.SessionID)
   380→	if err != nil {
   381→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state for compaction reset: %v\n", err)
   382→		return nil
   383→	}
   384→	if sessionState != nil {
   385→		sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0
   386→		if saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); saveErr != nil {

   122→// TransitionResult holds the outcome of a state machine transition.
   123→type TransitionResult struct {
   124→	NewPhase Phase
   125→	Actions  []Action
   126→}
   127→
   128→// Transition computes the next phase and required actions given the current
   129→// phase and an event. This is a pure function with no side effects.
   130→//
   131→// Empty or unknown phase values are normalized to PhaseIdle for backward
   132→// compatibility with session state files created before phase tracking.
   133→func Transition(current Phase, event Event, ctx TransitionContext) TransitionResult {
   134→	// Normalize empty/unknown phase to idle for backward compatibility.
   135→	current = PhaseFromString(string(current))
   136→
   137→	switch current {
   138→	case PhaseIdle:
   139→		return transitionFromIdle(event, ctx)
   140→	case PhaseActive:
   141→		return transitionFromActive(event, ctx)
   142→	case PhaseEnded:
   143→		return transitionFromEnded(event, ctx)
   144→	default:
   145→		// PhaseFromString guarantees we only get known phases, but the
   146→		// exhaustive linter requires handling the default case.
   147→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseIdle}
   148→	}
   149→}
   150→
   151→func transitionFromIdle(event Event, ctx TransitionContext) TransitionResult {
   152→	switch event {
   153→	case EventTurnStart:
   154→		return TransitionResult{
   155→			NewPhase: PhaseActive,
   156→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   157→		}
   158→	case EventTurnEnd:
   159→		// Turn end while idle is a no-op (no active turn to end).
   160→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseIdle}
   161→	case EventGitCommit:
   162→		if ctx.IsRebaseInProgress {
   163→			return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseIdle}
   164→		}
   165→		return TransitionResult{
   166→			NewPhase: PhaseIdle,
   167→			Actions:  []Action{ActionCondense, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   168→		}
   169→	case EventSessionStart:

   172→	case EventSessionStop:
   173→		return TransitionResult{
   174→			NewPhase: PhaseEnded,
   175→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   176→		}
   177→	default:
   178→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseIdle}
   179→	}
   180→}

   370→// handleLifecycleCompaction handles context compaction: saves like TurnEnd,
   371→// then resets the transcript offset since the transcript may be truncated.
   372→func handleLifecycleCompaction(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   373→	// Compaction triggers the same save logic as TurnEnd
   374→	if err := handleLifecycleTurnEnd(ag, event); err != nil {
   375→		return err
   376→	}
   377→
   378→	// Reset transcript offset in session state since the transcript may be reorganized
   379→	sessionState, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(event.SessionID)

   381→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state for compaction reset: %v\n", err)
   382→		return nil
   383→	}
   384→	if sessionState != nil {
   385→		sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0
   386→		if saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); saveErr != nil {
   387→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to reset transcript offset after compaction: %v\n", saveErr)
   388→		}
   389→	}
   390→
   391→	return nil
   392→}

   646→// transitionSessionTurnEnd transitions the session phase to IDLE and dispatches turn-end actions.
   647→func transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID string) {
   648→	turnState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   649→	if loadErr != nil {
   650→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state for turn end: %v\n", loadErr)
   651→		return
   652→	}
   653→	if turnState == nil {
   654→		return
   655→	}
   656→	if err := strategy.TransitionAndLog(turnState, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); err != nil {
   657→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end transition failed: %v\n", err)
   658→	}
   659→
   660→	// Always dispatch to strategy for turn-end handling. The strategy reads
   661→	// work items from state (e.g. TurnCheckpointIDs), not the action list.
   662→	strat := GetStrategy()
   663→	if handler, ok := strat.(strategy.TurnEndHandler); ok {
   664→		if err := handler.HandleTurnEnd(turnState); err != nil {
   665→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end action dispatch failed: %v\n", err)
   666→		}
   667→	}
   668→
   669→	if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(turnState); updateErr != nil {
   670→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session phase on turn end: %v\n", updateErr)
   671→	}
   672→}
   673→
   674→// markSessionEnded transitions the session to ENDED phase via the state machine.
   675→func markSessionEnded(sessionID string) error {
   676→	state, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   677→	if err != nil {
   678→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   679→	}
   680→	if state == nil {
   681→		return nil // No state file, nothing to update
   682→	}
   683→
   684→	if transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStop, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
   685→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: session stop transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
   686→	}
   687→
   688→	now := time.Now()
   689→	state.EndedAt = &now
   690→
   691→	if err := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   692→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   693→	}
   694→	return nil

  "old_string": "// handleLifecycleCompaction handles context compaction: saves like TurnEnd,\n// then resets the transcript offset since the transcript may be truncated.\nfunc handleLifecycleCompaction(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {\n\t// Compaction triggers the same save logic as TurnEnd\n\tif err := handleLifecycleTurnEnd(ag, event); err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\t// Reset transcript offset in session state since the transcript may be reorganized\n\tsessionState, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(event.SessionID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to load session state for compaction reset: %v\\n\", err)\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tif sessionState != nil {\n\t\tsessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0\n\t\tif saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); saveErr != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to reset transcript offset after compaction: %v\\n\", saveErr)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}",
  "new_string": "// handleLifecycleCompaction handles context compaction: saves current progress\n// but stays in ACTIVE phase (unlike TurnEnd which transitions to IDLE).\n// Also resets the transcript offset since the transcript may be truncated.\nfunc handleLifecycleCompaction(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {\n\tlogCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), \"lifecycle\"), ag.Name())\n\tlogging.Info(logCtx, \"compaction\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"event\", event.Type.String()),\n\t\tslog.String(\"session_id\", event.SessionID),\n\t)\n\n\t// Fire EventCompaction to trigger ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched (stays in ACTIVE)\n\tsessionID := event.SessionID\n\tsessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)\n\tif loadErr != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to load session state for compaction: %v\\n\", loadErr)\n\t}\n\tif sessionState != nil {\n\t\tif transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(sessionState, session.EventCompaction, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: compaction transition failed: %v\\n\", transErr)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Reset transcript offset since the transcript may be truncated/reorganized\n\t\tsessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0\n\n\t\tif saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); saveErr != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to save session state after compaction: %v\\n\", saveErr)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Context compaction: transcript offset reset\\n\")\n\treturn nil\n}"
}
```

   312→// Strategy defines the interface for git operation strategies.
   313→// Different implementations can use commits, branches, stashes, etc.
   314→//
   315→// Note: State capture (tracking untracked files before a session) is handled
   316→// by the CLI layer, not the strategy. The strategy receives pre-computed
   317→// file lists in SaveContext.
   318→type Strategy interface {
   319→	// Name returns the strategy identifier (e.g., "commit", "branch", "stash")
   320→	Name() string
   321→
   322→	// Description returns a human-readable description for the setup wizard
   323→	Description() string
   324→
   325→	// ValidateRepository checks if the repository is in a valid state
   326→	// for this strategy to operate. Returns an error if validation fails.
   327→	ValidateRepository() error
   328→
   329→	// SaveChanges is called on Stop to save all session changes
   330→	// using this strategy's approach (commit, branch, stash, etc.)
   331→	SaveChanges(ctx SaveContext) error
   332→
   333→	// SaveTaskCheckpoint is called by PostToolUse[Task] hook when a subagent completes.
   334→	// Creates a checkpoint commit with task metadata for later rewind.
   335→	// Different strategies may handle this differently:
   336→	// - Commit strategy: commits to active branch
   337→	// - Manual-commit strategy: commits to shadow branch
   338→	// - Auto-commit strategy: commits logs to shadow only (code deferred to Stop)
   339→	SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx TaskCheckpointContext) error
   340→
   341→	// GetRewindPoints returns available points to rewind to.
   342→	// The limit parameter controls the maximum number of points to return.
   343→	GetRewindPoints(limit int) ([]RewindPoint, error)
   344→
   345→	// Rewind restores the repository to the given rewind point.
   346→	// The metadataDir in the point is used to restore the session transcript.
   347→	Rewind(point RewindPoint) error
   348→
   349→	// CanRewind checks if rewinding is currently possible.
   350→	// Returns (canRewind, reason if not, error)
   351→	CanRewind() (bool, string, error)
   352→
   353→	// PreviewRewind returns what will happen if rewinding to the given point.
   354→	// This allows showing warnings about files that will be deleted before the rewind.
   355→	// Returns nil if preview is not supported (e.g., auto-commit strategy).
   356→	PreviewRewind(point RewindPoint) (*RewindPreview, error)
   357→
   358→	// GetTaskCheckpoint returns the task checkpoint for a given rewind point.
   359→	// For strategies that store checkpoints in git (auto-commit), this reads from the branch.

    17→// ErrNoMetadata is returned when a commit does not have an Entire metadata trailer.
    18→var ErrNoMetadata = errors.New("commit has no entire metadata")
    19→
    20→// ErrNoSession is returned when no session info is available.
    21→var ErrNoSession = errors.New("no session info available")
    22→
    23→// ErrNotTaskCheckpoint is returned when a rewind point is not a task checkpoint.
    24→var ErrNotTaskCheckpoint = errors.New("not a task checkpoint")
    25→
    26→// ErrNotImplemented is returned when a feature is not yet implemented.
    27→var ErrNotImplemented = errors.New("not implemented")
    28→
    29→// ErrEmptyRepository is returned when the repository has no commits yet.
    30→var ErrEmptyRepository = errors.New("repository has no commits yet")
    31→
    32→// SessionIDConflictError is returned when trying to start a new session
    33→// but the shadow branch already has commits from a different session ID.
    34→// This prevents orphaning existing session work.
    35→type SessionIDConflictError struct {
    36→	ExistingSession string // Session ID found in the shadow branch
    37→	NewSession      string // Session ID being initialized
    38→	ShadowBranch    string // The shadow branch name (e.g., "entire/abc1234")
    39→}
    40→
    41→func (e *SessionIDConflictError) Error() string {
    42→	return "session ID conflict: shadow branch has commits from a different session"
    43→}
    44→
    45→// ExtractToolUseIDFromTaskMetadataDir extracts the ToolUseID from a task metadata directory path.
    46→// Task metadata dirs have format: .entire/metadata/<session>/tasks/<toolUseID>
    47→// Returns empty string if not a task metadata directory.
    48→func ExtractToolUseIDFromTaskMetadataDir(metadataDir string) string {
    49→	parts := strings.Split(metadataDir, "/")
    50→	if len(parts) >= 2 && parts[len(parts)-2] == "tasks" {
    51→		return parts[len(parts)-1]
    52→	}
    53→	return ""
    54→}
    55→
    56→// SessionInfo contains information about the current session state.
    57→// This is used to generate trailers for linking commits to their AI session.
    58→type SessionInfo struct {
    59→	// SessionID is the session identifier extracted from the latest commit's metadata
    60→	SessionID string
    61→
    62→	// Reference is a strategy-specific reference string.
    63→	// For manual-commit strategy: "entire/abc1234" (the shadow branch name)
    64→	// Empty for commit strategy (metadata is in the same commit).
    65→	Reference string
    66→
    67→	// CommitHash is the full SHA of the commit containing the session metadata.

    70→}

    97→	IsLogsOnly bool
    98→
    99→	// CheckpointID is the stable 12-hex-char identifier for logs-only points.

   101→	// Empty for shadow branch checkpoints (uncommitted).
   102→	CheckpointID id.CheckpointID

   288→// TaskMetadataDir returns the path to a task's metadata directory

   293→
   294→// ReadTaskCheckpoint reads the checkpoint.json file from a task metadata directory.
   295→// This is used during rewind to get the checkpoint UUID for transcript truncation.
   296→func ReadTaskCheckpoint(taskMetadataDir string) (*TaskCheckpoint, error) {
   297→	checkpointFile := taskMetadataDir + "/checkpoint.json"
   298→
   299→	data, err := os.ReadFile(checkpointFile) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled git metadata path
   300→	if err != nil {
   301→		return nil, err //nolint:wrapcheck // already present in codebase
   302→	}
   303→
   304→	var checkpoint TaskCheckpoint
   305→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &checkpoint); err != nil {
   306→		return nil, err //nolint:wrapcheck // already present in codebase
   307→	}
   308→
   309→	return &checkpoint, nil
   310→}

   313→// Different implementations can use commits, branches, stashes, etc.
   314→//
   315→// Note: State capture (tracking untracked files before a session) is handled
   316→// by the CLI layer, not the strategy. The strategy receives pre-computed
   317→// file lists in SaveContext.
   318→type Strategy interface {
   319→	// Name returns the strategy identifier (e.g., "commit", "branch", "stash")
   320→	Name() string
   321→
   322→	// Description returns a human-readable description for the setup wizard
   323→	Description() string
   324→
   325→	// ValidateRepository checks if the repository is in a valid state
   326→	// for this strategy to operate. Returns an error if validation fails.
   327→	ValidateRepository() error
   328→
   329→	// SaveChanges is called on Stop to save all session changes
   330→	// using this strategy's approach (commit, branch, stash, etc.)
   331→	SaveChanges(ctx SaveContext) error
   332→
   333→	// SaveTaskCheckpoint is called by PostToolUse[Task] hook when a subagent completes.
   334→	// Creates a checkpoint commit with task metadata for later rewind.
   335→	// Different strategies may handle this differently:
   336→	// - Commit strategy: commits to active branch
   337→	// - Manual-commit strategy: commits to shadow branch
   338→	// - Auto-commit strategy: commits logs to shadow only (code deferred to Stop)
   339→	SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx TaskCheckpointContext) error
   340→
   341→	// GetRewindPoints returns available points to rewind to.
   342→	// The limit parameter controls the maximum number of points to return.
   343→	GetRewindPoints(limit int) ([]RewindPoint, error)
   344→
   345→	// Rewind restores the repository to the given rewind point.
   346→	// The metadataDir in the point is used to restore the session transcript.
   347→	Rewind(point RewindPoint) error
   348→
   349→	// CanRewind checks if rewinding is currently possible.
   350→	// Returns (canRewind, reason if not, error)
   351→	CanRewind() (bool, string, error)
   352→
   353→	// PreviewRewind returns what will happen if rewinding to the given point.
   354→	// This allows showing warnings about files that will be deleted before the rewind.
   355→	// Returns nil if preview is not supported (e.g., auto-commit strategy).
   356→	PreviewRewind(point RewindPoint) (*RewindPreview, error)
   357→
   358→	// GetTaskCheckpoint returns the task checkpoint for a given rewind point.
   359→	// For strategies that store checkpoints in git (auto-commit), this reads from the branch.
   360→	// For strategies that store checkpoints on disk (commit, manual-commit), this reads from the filesystem.
   361→	// Returns nil, nil if not a task checkpoint or checkpoint not found.
   362→	GetTaskCheckpoint(point RewindPoint) (*TaskCheckpoint, error)
   363→
   364→	// GetTaskCheckpointTranscript returns the session transcript for a task checkpoint.
   365→	// For strategies that store transcripts in git (auto-commit), this reads from the branch.
   366→	// For strategies that store transcripts on disk (commit, manual-commit), this reads from the filesystem.
   367→	GetTaskCheckpointTranscript(point RewindPoint) ([]byte, error)
   368→
   369→	// GetSessionInfo returns session information for linking commits.
   370→	// This is used by the context command to generate trailers.
   371→	// Returns ErrNoSession if no session info is available.
   372→	GetSessionInfo() (*SessionInfo, error)
   373→
   374→	// EnsureSetup ensures the strategy's required setup is in place,
   375→	// installing any missing pieces (git hooks, gitignore entries, etc.).
   376→	// Returns nil if setup is complete or was successfully installed.
   377→	EnsureSetup() error
   378→
   379→	// NOTE: ListSessions and GetSession are standalone functions in session.go.
   380→	// They read from entire/checkpoints/v1 and merge with SessionSource if implemented.
   381→
   382→	// GetMetadataRef returns a reference to the metadata commit for the given checkpoint.
   383→	// Format: "<branch>@<commit-sha>" (e.g., "entire/checkpoints/v1@abc123").
   384→	// Returns empty string if not applicable (e.g., commit strategy with filesystem metadata).
   385→	GetMetadataRef(checkpoint Checkpoint) string
   386→
   387→	// GetSessionMetadataRef returns a reference to the most recent metadata commit for a session.
   388→	// Format: "<branch>@<commit-sha>" (e.g., "entire/checkpoints/v1@abc123").
   389→	// Returns empty string if not applicable or session not found.
   390→	GetSessionMetadataRef(sessionID string) string
   391→
   392→	// GetSessionContext returns the context.md content for a session.
   393→	// Returns empty string if not available.
   394→	GetSessionContext(sessionID string) string
   395→
   396→	// GetCheckpointLog returns the session transcript for a specific checkpoint.
   397→	// For strategies that store transcripts in git branches (auto-commit, manual-commit),
   398→	// this reads from the checkpoint's commit tree.
   399→	// For strategies that store on disk (commit), reads from the filesystem.
   400→	// Returns ErrNoMetadata if transcript is not available.
   401→	GetCheckpointLog(checkpoint Checkpoint) ([]byte, error)
   402→}
   403→
   404→// SessionInitializer is an optional interface for strategies that need to
   405→// initialize session state when a user prompt is submitted.
   406→// Strategies like manual-commit use this to create session state files that
   407→// the git prepare-commit-msg hook can detect.
   408→type SessionInitializer interface {
   409→	// InitializeSession creates session state for a new session.
   410→	// Called during UserPromptSubmit hook before any checkpoints are created.
   411→	// agentType is the human-readable name of the agent (e.g., "Claude Code").
   412→	// transcriptPath is the path to the live transcript file (for mid-session commit detection).
   413→	// userPrompt is the user's prompt text (stored truncated as FirstPrompt for display).
   414→	InitializeSession(sessionID string, agentType agent.AgentType, transcriptPath string, userPrompt string) error
   415→}
   416→
   417→// PrepareCommitMsgHandler is an optional interface for strategies that need to
   418→// handle the git prepare-commit-msg hook.
   419→type PrepareCommitMsgHandler interface {
   420→	// PrepareCommitMsg is called by the git prepare-commit-msg hook.
   421→	// It can modify the commit message file to add trailers, etc.
   422→	// The source parameter indicates how the commit was initiated:
   423→	//   - "" or "template": normal editor flow
   424→	//   - "message": using -m or -F flag
   425→	//   - "merge": merge commit
   426→	//   - "squash": squash merge
   427→	//   - "commit": amend with -c/-C
   428→	// Should return nil on errors to not block commits (log warnings to stderr).
   429→	PrepareCommitMsg(commitMsgFile string, source string) error
   430→}
   431→
   432→// PostCommitHandler is an optional interface for strategies that need to
   433→// handle the git post-commit hook.
   434→type PostCommitHandler interface {
   435→	// PostCommit is called by the git post-commit hook after a commit is created.
   436→	// Used to perform actions like condensing session data after commits.
   437→	// Should return nil on errors to not block subsequent operations (log warnings to stderr).
   438→	PostCommit() error
   439→}
   440→
   441→// CommitMsgHandler is an optional interface for strategies that need to
   442→// handle the git commit-msg hook.
   443→type CommitMsgHandler interface {
   444→	// CommitMsg is called by the git commit-msg hook after the user edits the message.
   445→	// Used to validate or modify the final commit message before the commit is created.
   446→	// If this returns an error, the commit is aborted.
   447→	CommitMsg(commitMsgFile string) error
   448→}
   449→
   450→// PrePushHandler is an optional interface for strategies that need to
   451→// handle the git pre-push hook.
   452→type PrePushHandler interface {
   453→	// PrePush is called by the git pre-push hook before pushing to a remote.
   454→	// Used to push session branches (e.g., entire/checkpoints/v1) alongside user pushes.
   455→	// The remote parameter is the name of the remote being pushed to.
   456→	// Should return nil on errors to not block pushes (log warnings to stderr).
   457→	PrePush(remote string) error
   458→}
   459→
   460→// TurnEndHandler is an optional interface for strategies that need to
   461→// perform work when an agent turn ends (ACTIVE → IDLE).
   462→// For example, manual-commit strategy uses this to finalize checkpoints
   463→// with the full session transcript.
   464→type TurnEndHandler interface {
   465→	// HandleTurnEnd performs strategy-specific cleanup at the end of a turn.
   466→	// Work items are read from state (e.g. TurnCheckpointIDs), not from the
   467→	// action list. The state has already been updated by ApplyTransition;
   468→	// the caller saves it after this method returns.
   469→	HandleTurnEnd(state *session.State) error
   470→}
   471→
   472→// RestoredSession describes a single session that was restored by RestoreLogsOnly.
   473→// Each session may come from a different agent, so callers use this to print
   474→// per-session resume commands without re-reading the metadata tree.
   475→type RestoredSession struct {
   476→	SessionID string
   477→	Agent     agent.AgentType
   478→	Prompt    string
   479→}
   480→
   481→// LogsOnlyRestorer is an optional interface for strategies that support
   482→// restoring session logs without file state restoration.
   483→// This is used for "logs-only" rewind points where only the session transcript
   484→// can be restored (file state requires git checkout).
   485→type LogsOnlyRestorer interface {
   486→	// RestoreLogsOnly restores session logs from a logs-only rewind point.
   487→	// Does not modify the working directory - only restores the transcript
   488→	// to the agent's session directory (determined per-session from checkpoint metadata).
   489→	// If force is false, prompts for confirmation when local logs have newer timestamps.
   490→	// Returns info about each restored session so callers can print correct resume commands.
   491→	RestoreLogsOnly(point RewindPoint, force bool) ([]RestoredSession, error)
   492→}
   493→
   494→// SessionResetter is an optional interface for strategies that support
   495→// resetting session state and shadow branches.
   496→// This is used by the "reset" command to clean up shadow branches
   497→// and session state when a user wants to start fresh.
   498→type SessionResetter interface {
   499→	// Reset deletes the shadow branch and session state for the current HEAD.
   500→	// Returns nil if there's nothing to reset (no shadow branch).
   501→	Reset() error
   502→
   503→	// ResetSession clears the state for a single session and cleans up
   504→	// the shadow branch if no other sessions reference it.
   505→	// File changes remain in the working directory.
   506→	ResetSession(sessionID string) error
   507→}
   508→
   509→// SessionCondenser is an optional interface for strategies that support
   510→// force-condensing a session. This is used by "entire doctor" to
   511→// salvage stuck sessions by condensing their data to permanent storage.
   512→type SessionCondenser interface {
   513→	// CondenseSessionByID force-condenses a session and cleans up.
   514→	// Generates a new checkpoint ID, condenses to entire/checkpoints/v1,
   515→	// updates the session state, and removes the shadow branch
   516→	// if no other active sessions need it.
   517→	CondenseSessionByID(sessionID string) error
   518→}
   519→
   520→// ConcurrentSessionChecker is an optional interface for strategies that support
   521→// counting concurrent sessions with uncommitted changes.
   522→// This is used by the SessionStart hook to show an informational message about
   523→// how many other active conversations will be included in the next commit.
   524→type ConcurrentSessionChecker interface {
   525→	// CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints returns the number of other active sessions
   526→	// with uncommitted checkpoints on the same base commit.
   527→	// Returns 0, nil if no such sessions exist.
   528→	CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints(currentSessionID string) (int, error)
   529→}
   530→
   531→// SessionSource is an optional interface for strategies that provide additional
   532→// sessions beyond those stored on the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   533→// For example, manual-commit strategy provides active sessions from .git/entire-sessions/
   534→// that haven't yet been condensed to entire/checkpoints/v1.
   535→//
   536→// ListSessions() automatically discovers all registered strategies, checks if they
   537→// implement SessionSource, and merges their additional sessions by ID.
   538→type SessionSource interface {
   539→	// GetAdditionalSessions returns sessions not yet on entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   540→	GetAdditionalSessions() ([]*Session, error)
   541→}
   542→
   543→// OrphanedItemsLister is an optional interface for strategies that can identify
   544→// orphaned items (shadow branches, session states, checkpoints) that should be
   545→// cleaned up. This is used by the "entire session cleanup" command.
   546→//
   547→// ListAllCleanupItems() automatically discovers all registered strategies, checks
   548→// if they implement OrphanedItemsLister, and combines their orphaned items.
   549→type OrphanedItemsLister interface {
   550→	// ListOrphanedItems returns items created by this strategy that are now orphaned.
   551→	// Each strategy defines what "orphaned" means for its own data structures.
   552→	ListOrphanedItems() ([]CleanupItem, error)
   553→}

    21→// SaveChanges saves a checkpoint to the shadow branch.
    22→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.WriteTemporary for git operations.
    23→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) SaveChanges(ctx SaveContext) error {
    24→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
    25→	if err != nil {
    26→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
    27→	}
    28→
    29→	// Extract session ID from metadata dir
    30→	sessionID := filepath.Base(ctx.MetadataDir)
    31→
    32→	// Load or initialize session state
    33→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
    34→	if err != nil {
    35→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
    36→	}
    37→	// Initialize if state is nil OR BaseCommit is empty (can happen with partial state from warnings)
    38→	if state == nil || state.BaseCommit == "" {
    39→		agentType := resolveAgentType(ctx.AgentType, state)
    40→		state, err = s.initializeSession(repo, sessionID, agentType, "", "") // No transcript/prompt in fallback
    41→		if err != nil {
    42→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to initialize session: %w", err)
    43→		}
    44→	}
    45→
    46→	// Check if HEAD has changed (e.g., Claude did a rebase via tool call) and migrate if needed
    47→	if err := s.migrateAndPersistIfNeeded(repo, state); err != nil {
    48→		return err
    49→	}
    50→

     1→package strategy
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"encoding/json"
     6→	"errors"
     7→	"fmt"
     8→	"log/slog"
     9→	"os"
    10→	"path/filepath"
    11→	"strings"
    12→	"sync"
    13→	"time"
    14→
    15→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    16→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/buildinfo"
    17→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint"
    18→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint/id"
    19→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging"
    20→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    21→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/trailers"
    22→
    23→	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5"
    24→	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing"
    25→	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/object"
    26→)
    27→
    28→// isNotFoundError checks if an error represents a "not found" condition in go-git.
    29→// This includes entry not found, file not found, directory not found, and object not found.
    30→func isNotFoundError(err error) bool {
    31→	return errors.Is(err, object.ErrEntryNotFound) ||
    32→		errors.Is(err, object.ErrFileNotFound) ||
    33→		errors.Is(err, object.ErrDirectoryNotFound) ||
    34→		errors.Is(err, plumbing.ErrObjectNotFound) ||
    35→		errors.Is(err, plumbing.ErrReferenceNotFound)
    36→}
    37→
    38→// commitOrHead attempts to create a commit. If the commit would be empty (files already
    39→// committed), it returns HEAD hash instead. This handles the case where files were
    40→// modified during a session but already committed by the user before the hook runs.
    41→func commitOrHead(repo *git.Repository, worktree *git.Worktree, msg string, author *object.Signature) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
    42→	commitHash, err := worktree.Commit(msg, &git.CommitOptions{Author: author})
    43→	if errors.Is(err, git.ErrEmptyCommit) {
    44→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No changes to commit (files already committed)\n")
    45→		head, err := repo.Head()
    46→		if err != nil {
    47→			return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
    48→		}
    49→		return head.Hash(), nil
    50→	}
    51→	if err != nil {
    52→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to commit: %w", err)
    53→	}
    54→	return commitHash, nil
    55→}
    56→
    57→// AutoCommitStrategy implements the auto-commit strategy:
    58→// - Code changes are committed to the active branch (like commit strategy)
    59→// - Session logs are committed to a shadow branch (like manual-commit strategy)
    60→// - Code commits can reference the shadow branch via trailers
    61→type AutoCommitStrategy struct {
    62→	// checkpointStore manages checkpoint data on entire/checkpoints/v1 branch
    63→	checkpointStore *checkpoint.GitStore
    64→	// checkpointStoreOnce ensures thread-safe lazy initialization
    65→	checkpointStoreOnce sync.Once
    66→	// checkpointStoreErr captures any error during initialization
    67→	checkpointStoreErr error
    68→}
    69→
    70→// getCheckpointStore returns the checkpoint store, initializing it lazily if needed.
    71→// Thread-safe via sync.Once.
    72→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) getCheckpointStore() (*checkpoint.GitStore, error) {
    73→	s.checkpointStoreOnce.Do(func() {
    74→		repo, err := OpenRepository()
    75→		if err != nil {
    76→			s.checkpointStoreErr = fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
    77→			return
    78→		}
    79→		s.checkpointStore = checkpoint.NewGitStore(repo)
    80→	})
    81→	return s.checkpointStore, s.checkpointStoreErr
    82→}
    83→
    84→// NewAutoCommitStrategy creates a new AutoCommitStrategy instance
    85→//
    86→
    87→func NewAutoCommitStrategy() Strategy {
    88→	return &AutoCommitStrategy{}
    89→}
    90→
    91→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) Name() string {
    92→	return StrategyNameAutoCommit
    93→}
    94→
    95→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) Description() string {
    96→	return "Auto-commits code to active branch with metadata on entire/checkpoints/v1"
    97→}
    98→
    99→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) ValidateRepository() error {
   100→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   101→	if err != nil {
   102→		return fmt.Errorf("not a git repository: %w", err)
   103→	}
   104→
   105→	_, err = repo.Worktree()
   106→	if err != nil {
   107→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to access worktree: %w", err)
   108→	}
   109→
   110→	return nil
   111→}
   112→
   113→// PrePush is called by the git pre-push hook before pushing to a remote.
   114→// It pushes the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch alongside the user's push.
   115→// Configuration options (stored in .entire/settings.json under strategy_options.push_sessions):
   116→//   - "auto": always push automatically
   117→//   - "prompt" (default): ask user with option to enable auto
   118→//   - "false"/"off"/"no": never push
   119→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) PrePush(remote string) error {
   120→	return pushSessionsBranchCommon(remote, paths.MetadataBranchName)
   121→}
   122→
   123→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) SaveChanges(ctx SaveContext) error {
   124→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   125→	if err != nil {
   126→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   127→	}
   128→
   129→	// Generate checkpoint ID for this commit
   130→	cpID, err := id.Generate()
   131→	if err != nil {
   132→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate checkpoint ID: %w", err)
   133→	}
   134→	// Step 1: Commit code changes to active branch with checkpoint ID trailer
   135→	// We do code first to avoid orphaned metadata if this step fails.
   136→	// If metadata commit fails after this, the code commit exists but GetRewindPoints
   137→	// already handles missing metadata gracefully (skips commits without metadata).
   138→	codeResult, err := s.commitCodeToActive(repo, ctx, cpID)
   139→	if err != nil {
   140→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to commit code to active branch: %w", err)
   141→	}
   142→
   143→	// If no code commit was created (no changes), skip metadata creation
   144→	// This prevents orphaned metadata commits that don't correspond to any code commit
   145→	if !codeResult.Created {
   146→		logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   147→		logging.Info(logCtx, "checkpoint skipped (no changes)",
   148→			slog.String("strategy", "auto-commit"),
   149→			slog.String("checkpoint_type", "session"),
   150→		)
   151→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipped checkpoint (no changes since last commit)\n")
   152→		return nil
   153→	}
   154→
   155→	// Step 2: Commit metadata to entire/checkpoints/v1 branch using sharded path
   156→	// Path is <checkpointID[:2]>/<checkpointID[2:]>/ for direct lookup
   157→	_, err = s.commitMetadataToMetadataBranch(repo, ctx, cpID)
   158→	if err != nil {
   159→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to commit metadata to entire/checkpoints/v1 branch: %w", err)
   160→	}
   161→
   162→	// Log checkpoint creation
   163→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   164→	logging.Info(logCtx, "checkpoint saved",
   165→		slog.String("strategy", "auto-commit"),
   166→		slog.String("checkpoint_type", "session"),
   167→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpID.String()),
   168→		slog.Int("modified_files", len(ctx.ModifiedFiles)),
   169→		slog.Int("new_files", len(ctx.NewFiles)),
   170→		slog.Int("deleted_files", len(ctx.DeletedFiles)),
   171→	)
   172→
   173→	return nil
   174→}
   175→
   176→// commitCodeResult contains the result of committing code to the active branch.
   177→type commitCodeResult struct {
   178→	CommitHash plumbing.Hash
   179→	Created    bool // True if a new commit was created, false if skipped (no changes)
   180→}
   181→
   182→// commitCodeToActive commits code changes to the active branch.
   183→// Adds an Entire-Checkpoint trailer for metadata lookup that survives amend/rebase.
   184→// Returns the result containing commit hash and whether a commit was created.
   185→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) commitCodeToActive(repo *git.Repository, ctx SaveContext, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (commitCodeResult, error) {
   186→	// Check if there are any code changes to commit
   187→	if len(ctx.ModifiedFiles) == 0 && len(ctx.NewFiles) == 0 && len(ctx.DeletedFiles) == 0 {
   188→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No code changes to commit to active branch\n")
   189→		// Return current HEAD hash but mark as not created
   190→		head, err := repo.Head()
   191→		if err != nil {
   192→			return commitCodeResult{}, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   193→		}
   194→		return commitCodeResult{CommitHash: head.Hash(), Created: false}, nil
   195→	}
   196→
   197→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   198→	if err != nil {
   199→		return commitCodeResult{}, fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree: %w", err)
   200→	}
   201→
   202→	// Get HEAD hash before commit to detect if commitOrHead actually creates a new commit
   203→	// (commitOrHead returns HEAD hash without error when git.ErrEmptyCommit occurs)
   204→	headBefore, err := repo.Head()
   205→	if err != nil {
   206→		return commitCodeResult{}, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   207→	}
   208→
   209→	// Stage code changes
   210→	StageFiles(worktree, ctx.ModifiedFiles, ctx.NewFiles, ctx.DeletedFiles, StageForSession)
   211→
   212→	// Add checkpoint ID trailer to commit message
   213→	commitMsg := ctx.CommitMessage + "\n\n" + trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ": " + checkpointID.String()
   214→
   215→	author := &object.Signature{
   216→		Name:  ctx.AuthorName,
   217→		Email: ctx.AuthorEmail,
   218→		When:  time.Now(),
   219→	}
   220→	commitHash, err := commitOrHead(repo, worktree, commitMsg, author)
   221→	if err != nil {
   222→		return commitCodeResult{}, err
   223→	}
   224→
   225→	// Check if a new commit was actually created by comparing with HEAD before
   226→	created := commitHash != headBefore.Hash()
   227→	if created {
   228→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Committed code changes to active branch (%s)\n", commitHash.String()[:7])
   229→	}
   230→	return commitCodeResult{CommitHash: commitHash, Created: created}, nil
   231→}
   232→
   233→// commitMetadataToMetadataBranch commits session metadata to the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   234→// Metadata is stored at sharded path: <checkpointID[:2]>/<checkpointID[2:]>/
   235→// This allows direct lookup from the checkpoint ID trailer on the code commit.
   236→// Uses checkpoint.WriteCommitted for git operations.
   237→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) commitMetadataToMetadataBranch(repo *git.Repository, ctx SaveContext, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
   238→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   239→	if err != nil {
   240→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
   241→	}
   242→
   243→	// Extract session ID from metadata dir
   244→	sessionID := filepath.Base(ctx.MetadataDir)
   245→
   246→	// Get current branch name
   247→	branchName := GetCurrentBranchName(repo)
   248→
   249→	// Combine all file changes into FilesTouched (same as manual-commit)
   250→	filesTouched := mergeFilesTouched(nil, ctx.ModifiedFiles, ctx.NewFiles, ctx.DeletedFiles)

    22→
    23→// TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_CondensesImmediately verifies that PostCommit on
    24→// an ACTIVE session condenses immediately and stays ACTIVE.
    25→// With the 1:1 checkpoint model, each commit gets its own checkpoint right away.
    26→func TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_CondensesImmediately(t *testing.T) {
    27→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
    28→	t.Chdir(dir)
    29→
    30→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
    31→	require.NoError(t, err)
    32→
    33→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
    34→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-active"
    35→
    36→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint so there is shadow branch content
    37→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
    38→
    39→	// Set phase to ACTIVE (simulating agent mid-turn)
    40→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
    41→	require.NoError(t, err)
    42→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
    43→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))

   558→// commitTaskCodeToActive commits task code changes to the active branch.
   559→// Adds an Entire-Checkpoint trailer for metadata lookup that survives amend/rebase.
   560→// Skips commit creation if there are no file changes.
   561→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) commitTaskCodeToActive(repo *git.Repository, ctx TaskCheckpointContext, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
   562→	hasFileChanges := len(ctx.ModifiedFiles) > 0 || len(ctx.NewFiles) > 0 || len(ctx.DeletedFiles) > 0
   563→
   564→	// If no file changes, skip code commit
   565→	if !hasFileChanges {

   567→		// Return current HEAD hash so metadata can still be stored
   568→		head, err := repo.Head()
   569→		if err != nil {
   570→			return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   571→		}
   572→		return head.Hash(), nil

   106→// CondenseSession condenses a session's shadow branch to permanent storage.
   107→// checkpointID is the 12-hex-char value from the Entire-Checkpoint trailer.

   111→// For mid-session commits (no Stop/SaveChanges called yet), the shadow branch may not exist.
   112→// In this case, data is extracted from the live transcript instead.
   113→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CondenseSession(repo *git.Repository, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, state *SessionState, committedFiles map[string]struct{}) (*CondenseResult, error) {
   114→	// Get shadow branch (may not exist for mid-session commits)

   403→### 4c. Step/Checkpoint terminology rename
   404→
   405→Rename strategy interface methods to align with Step/Checkpoint terminology:
   406→
   407→| Current | New | Notes |
   408→|---------|-----|-------|
   409→| `SaveChanges(SaveContext)` | `SaveStep(StepContext)` | Core strategy method |
   410→| `SaveTaskCheckpoint(TaskCheckpointContext)` | `SaveTaskStep(TaskStepContext)` | Core strategy method |
   411→| `SaveContext` struct | `StepContext` | Rename type |
   412→| `TaskCheckpointContext` struct | `TaskStepContext` | Rename type |
   413→| `GetRewindPoints()` | Keep as-is | Rewind works from both steps and checkpoints |
   414→| `checkpoint.WriteTemporary()` | Keep as-is | Already semantically "step write" |
   415→| `checkpoint.WriteCommitted()` | Keep as-is | Already semantically "checkpoint write" |
   416→
   417→This rename propagates through:
   418→- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/strategy.go` — interface definition, `StepContext`/`TaskStepContext` structs
   419→- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit.go` — `SaveStep()` implementation
   420→- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/auto_commit.go` — `SaveStep()` implementation

   424→- CLAUDE.md documentation
   425→
   426→### 4d. PostTodo pass-through
   427→
   428→Claude Code's PostTodo hook stays as a **direct handler** outside the generic dispatcher. In `hook_registry.go`, register it as a special case:
   429→
   430→```go
   431→// PostTodo is Claude-specific: incremental subagent checkpoints.

     1→// hook_registry.go provides hook command registration for agents.
     2→// The lifecycle dispatcher (DispatchLifecycleEvent) handles all lifecycle events.
     3→// PostTodo is the only hook that's handled directly (not via lifecycle dispatcher).
     4→package cli
     5→
     6→import (
     7→	"context"

    20→)
    21→
    22→// agentHookLogCleanup stores the cleanup function for agent hook logging.
    23→// Set by PersistentPreRunE, called by PersistentPostRunE.
    24→var agentHookLogCleanup func()
    25→
    26→// currentHookAgentName stores the agent name for the currently executing hook.
    27→// Set by newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging before calling the handler.
    28→// This allows handlers to know which agent invoked the hook without guessing.
    29→var currentHookAgentName agent.AgentName
    30→
    31→// GetCurrentHookAgent returns the agent for the currently executing hook.
    32→// Returns the agent based on the hook command structure (e.g., "entire hooks claude-code ...")
    33→// rather than guessing from directory presence.
    34→// Falls back to GetAgent() if not in a hook context.
    35→func GetCurrentHookAgent() (agent.Agent, error) {
    36→	if currentHookAgentName == "" {
    37→		return nil, errors.New("not in a hook context: agent name not set")
    38→	}
    39→
    40→	ag, err := agent.Get(currentHookAgentName)
    41→	if err != nil {
    42→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("getting hook agent %q: %w", currentHookAgentName, err)
    43→	}
    44→	return ag, nil
    45→}
    46→
    47→// newAgentHooksCmd creates a hooks subcommand for an agent that implements HookHandler.
    48→// It dynamically creates subcommands for each hook the agent supports.
    49→func newAgentHooksCmd(agentName agent.AgentName, handler agent.HookHandler) *cobra.Command {
    50→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
    51→		Use:    string(agentName),
    52→		Short:  handler.Description() + " hook handlers",
    53→		Hidden: true,
    54→		PersistentPreRunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
    55→			agentHookLogCleanup = initHookLogging()
    56→			return nil
    57→		},
    58→		PersistentPostRunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
    59→			if agentHookLogCleanup != nil {
    60→				agentHookLogCleanup()
    61→			}
    62→			return nil
    63→		},
    64→	}
    65→
    66→	for _, hookName := range handler.GetHookNames() {
    67→		cmd.AddCommand(newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging(agentName, hookName))
    68→	}
    69→
    70→	return cmd
    71→}
    72→
    73→// getHookType returns the hook type based on the hook name.
    74→// Returns "subagent" for task-related hooks (pre-task, post-task, post-todo),
    75→// "tool" for tool-related hooks (before-tool, after-tool),
    76→// "agent" for all other agent hooks.
    77→func getHookType(hookName string) string {
    78→	switch hookName {
    79→	case claudecode.HookNamePreTask, claudecode.HookNamePostTask, claudecode.HookNamePostTodo:
    80→		return "subagent"
    81→	case geminicli.HookNameBeforeTool, geminicli.HookNameAfterTool:
    82→		return "tool"
    83→	default:
    84→		return "agent"
    85→	}
    86→}
    87→
    88→// newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging creates a command for a specific hook verb with structured logging.
    89→// It uses the lifecycle dispatcher (ParseHookEvent → DispatchLifecycleEvent) as the primary path.
    90→// PostTodo is handled directly as it's Claude-specific and not part of the lifecycle dispatcher.
    91→func newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging(agentName agent.AgentName, hookName string) *cobra.Command {
    92→	return &cobra.Command{
    93→		Use:    hookName,
    94→		Hidden: true,
    95→		Short:  "Called on " + hookName,
    96→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
    97→			// Skip silently if not in a git repository - hooks shouldn't prevent the agent from working
    98→			if _, err := paths.RepoRoot(); err != nil {
    99→				return nil
   100→			}

     1→// hooks_claudecode_posttodo.go contains the PostTodo hook handler for Claude Code.
     2→// This is a Claude-specific hook that creates incremental checkpoints during subagent execution.
     3→// It's not part of the generic lifecycle dispatcher because it requires special handling:
     4→// - Only fires for TodoWrite tool invocations
     5→// - Creates incremental checkpoints (not full checkpoints)
     6→// - Only activates when in subagent context (pre-task file exists)
     7→package cli
     8→
     9→import (
    10→	"context"
    11→	"fmt"
    12→	"log/slog"
    13→	"os"
    14→
    15→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    16→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging"
    17→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    18→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy"
    19→)
    20→
    21→// handleClaudeCodePostTodo handles the PostToolUse[TodoWrite] hook for subagent checkpoints.
    22→// Creates a checkpoint if we're in a subagent context (active pre-task file exists).
    23→// Skips silently if not in subagent context (main agent).
    24→func handleClaudeCodePostTodo() error {
    25→	input, err := parseSubagentCheckpointHookInput(os.Stdin)
    26→	if err != nil {
    27→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PostToolUse[TodoWrite] input: %w", err)
    28→	}
    29→
    30→	// Get agent for logging context
    31→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
    32→	if err != nil {
    33→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
    34→	}
    35→
    36→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
    37→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-todo",
    38→		slog.String("hook", "post-todo"),
    39→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
    40→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
    41→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.TranscriptPath),
    42→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
    43→	)
    44→
    45→	// Check if we're in a subagent context by looking for an active pre-task file
    46→	taskToolUseID, found := FindActivePreTaskFile()
    47→	if !found {
    48→		// Not in subagent context - this is a main agent TodoWrite, skip
    49→		return nil
    50→	}

can you do a short summary for a PR describing the refactor?

> AGENT

## PR Summary: Agent Interface Refactor (Phase 4)

### Overview
Refactors the agent hook system to use a generic lifecycle dispatcher, making it easier to add new agents. This is part of the work to create a contributor-ready plugin surface.

### Key Changes

**New Generic Lifecycle Dispatcher**
- `DispatchLifecycleEvent()` in `lifecycle.go` handles all agent lifecycle events uniformly
- Agents implement `ParseHookEvent(hookName, stdin) → *Event` to convert hook inputs to generic events
- Single code path for TurnStart, TurnEnd, TaskComplete, Compaction, etc.

**Removed Legacy Handler Files** (~1,545 lines deleted)
- Deleted `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` (886 lines)
- Deleted `hooks_geminicli_handlers.go` (659 lines)
- Logic consolidated into `lifecycle.go`

**Simplified Hook Registry**
- `hook_registry.go` now routes all hooks through the lifecycle dispatcher
- PostTodo remains Claude-specific (extracted to `hooks_claudecode_posttodo.go`)

**Added EventCompaction**
- New event type in `session/phase.go` for mid-turn context compression
- Stays in ACTIVE phase (unlike TurnEnd which transitions to IDLE)

**Terminology Rename**
- `SaveChanges()` → `SaveStep()` 
- `SaveTaskCheckpoint()` → `SaveTaskStep()`
- `SaveContext` → `StepContext`
- `TaskCheckpointContext` → `TaskStepContext`

### Testing
All existing tests pass. No behavioral changes to end users.

     1→// Package id provides the CheckpointID type for identifying checkpoints.
     2→// This is a separate package to avoid import cycles between paths, trailers, and checkpoint.
     3→package id
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"crypto/rand"
     7→	"encoding/hex"
     8→	"encoding/json"
     9→	"fmt"
    10→	"regexp"
    11→)
    12→
    13→// CheckpointID is a 12-character hex identifier for checkpoints.
    14→// It's used to link code commits to metadata on the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
    15→//
    16→//nolint:recvcheck // UnmarshalJSON requires pointer receiver, others use value receiver - standard pattern
    17→type CheckpointID string
    18→
    19→// EmptyCheckpointID represents an unset or invalid checkpoint ID.
    20→const EmptyCheckpointID CheckpointID = ""
    21→
    22→// Pattern is the regex pattern for a valid checkpoint ID: exactly 12 lowercase hex characters.
    23→// Exported for use in other packages (e.g., trailers) to avoid pattern duplication.
    24→const Pattern = `[0-9a-f]{12}`
    25→
    26→// checkpointIDRegex validates the format: exactly 12 lowercase hex characters.
    27→var checkpointIDRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`^` + Pattern + `$`)
    28→
    29→// NewCheckpointID creates a CheckpointID from a string, validating its format.
    30→// Returns an error if the string is not a valid 12-character hex ID.
    31→func NewCheckpointID(s string) (CheckpointID, error) {
    32→	if err := Validate(s); err != nil {
    33→		return EmptyCheckpointID, err
    34→	}
    35→	return CheckpointID(s), nil
    36→}
    37→
    38→// MustCheckpointID creates a CheckpointID from a string, panicking if invalid.
    39→// Use only when the ID is known to be valid (e.g., from trusted sources).
    40→func MustCheckpointID(s string) CheckpointID {
    41→	id, err := NewCheckpointID(s)
    42→	if err != nil {
    43→		panic(err)
    44→	}
    45→	return id
    46→}
    47→
    48→// Generate creates a new random 12-character hex checkpoint ID.
    49→func Generate() (CheckpointID, error) {
    50→	bytes := make([]byte, 6) // 6 bytes = 12 hex chars
    51→	if _, err := rand.Read(bytes); err != nil {
    52→		return EmptyCheckpointID, fmt.Errorf("failed to generate random checkpoint ID: %w", err)
    53→	}
    54→	return CheckpointID(hex.EncodeToString(bytes)), nil
    55→}
    56→
    57→// Validate checks if a string is a valid checkpoint ID format.
    58→// Returns an error if invalid, nil if valid.
    59→func Validate(s string) error {
    60→	if !checkpointIDRegex.MatchString(s) {
    61→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid checkpoint ID %q: must be 12 lowercase hex characters", s)
    62→	}
    63→	return nil
    64→}
    65→
    66→// String returns the checkpoint ID as a string.
    67→func (id CheckpointID) String() string {
    68→	return string(id)
    69→}
    70→
    71→// IsEmpty returns true if the checkpoint ID is empty or unset.
    72→func (id CheckpointID) IsEmpty() bool {
    73→	return id == EmptyCheckpointID
    74→}
    75→
    76→// Path returns the sharded path for this checkpoint ID on entire/checkpoints/v1.
    77→// Uses first 2 characters as shard (256 buckets), remaining as folder name.
    78→// Example: "a3b2c4d5e6f7" -> "a3/b2c4d5e6f7"
    79→func (id CheckpointID) Path() string {
    80→	if len(id) < 3 {
    81→		return string(id)
    82→	}
    83→	return string(id[:2]) + "/" + string(id[2:])
    84→}
    85→
    86→// MarshalJSON implements json.Marshaler.
    87→func (id CheckpointID) MarshalJSON() ([]byte, error) {
    88→	data, err := json.Marshal(string(id))
    89→	if err != nil {
    90→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal checkpoint ID: %w", err)
    91→	}
    92→	return data, nil
    93→}
    94→
    95→// UnmarshalJSON implements json.Unmarshaler with validation.
    96→// Returns an error if the JSON string is not a valid 12-character hex ID.
    97→// Empty strings are allowed and result in EmptyCheckpointID.
    98→func (id *CheckpointID) UnmarshalJSON(data []byte) error {
    99→	var s string
   100→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &s); err != nil {
   101→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal checkpoint ID: %w", err)
   102→	}
   103→	// Allow empty strings (represents unset checkpoint ID)
   104→	if s == "" {
   105→		*id = EmptyCheckpointID
   106→		return nil
   107→	}
   108→	if err := Validate(s); err != nil {
   109→		return err
   110→	}
   111→	*id = CheckpointID(s)
   112→	return nil
   113→}
   114→

### TurnEnd misses files modified by subagents

**High Severity**

<!-- DESCRIPTION START -->
The old `commitWithMetadata` called `claudecode.ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptPath, transcriptOffset, subagentsDir)` which scanned both the main transcript and all subagent transcripts in the subagents directory. The new generic `handleLifecycleTurnEnd` only calls `analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset)`, which for Claude Code only reads the main transcript file. Files modified by subagents during the turn are no longer included in the `ModifiedFiles` list for the TurnEnd checkpoint, resulting in incomplete checkpoint metadata and potentially missed files in strategy processing.
<!-- DESCRIPTION END -->

<!-- BUGBOT_BUG_ID: 0e77fd80-660c-4a42-bb34-3b1828617854 -->

<!-- LOCATIONS START
cmd/entire/cli/lifecycle.go#L226-L234
LOCATIONS END -->

### Subagent token usage excluded with empty directory

**Medium Severity**

<!-- DESCRIPTION START -->
`CalculateTokenUsage` passes an empty string for the `subagentsDir` parameter to `CalculateTotalTokenUsage`. The old code computed the actual subagents directory path and passed it, so token usage from subagent sessions was included. The comment on line 128 acknowledges this gap ("For now, compute subagentsDir from the transcript path structure") but then passes `""` instead of actually computing it. This results in underreported token counts for sessions using Claude Code subagents.
<!-- DESCRIPTION END -->

<!-- BUGBOT_BUG_ID: d80e1cf8-748a-4281-96ec-3d134b59701c -->

<!-- LOCATIONS START
cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/lifecycle.go#L123-L130
LOCATIONS END -->

### Sentinel timestamp check removes upper bound validation

**Low Severity**

<!-- DESCRIPTION START -->
The new `checkStopSentinel` only checks that the sentinel timestamp is after `hookStartTime - maxSkew`, removing the upper bound check (`ts.Before(hookStartTime.Add(maxSkew))`) that existed in the old implementation. This means any sentinel with a sufficiently recent timestamp will match, even one from a concurrent or future stop hook invocation, weakening the staleness protection.
<!-- DESCRIPTION END -->

<!-- BUGBOT_BUG_ID: b0a576c1-d5c2-40d8-a4ea-2cda621a74c1 -->
