     1→// hooks_claudecode_posttodo.go contains the PostTodo hook handler for Claude Code.
     2→// This is a Claude-specific hook that creates incremental checkpoints during subagent execution.
     3→// It's not part of the generic lifecycle dispatcher because it requires special handling:
     4→// - Only fires for TodoWrite tool invocations
     5→// - Creates incremental checkpoints (not full checkpoints)
     6→// - Only activates when in subagent context (pre-task file exists)
     7→package cli
     8→
     9→import (
    10→	"context"
    11→	"fmt"
    12→	"log/slog"
    13→	"os"
    14→
    15→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    16→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging"
    17→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    18→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy"
    19→)
    20→
    21→// handleClaudeCodePostTodo handles the PostToolUse[TodoWrite] hook for subagent checkpoints.
    22→// Creates a checkpoint if we're in a subagent context (active pre-task file exists).
    23→// Skips silently if not in subagent context (main agent).
    24→func handleClaudeCodePostTodo() error {
    25→	input, err := parseSubagentCheckpointHookInput(os.Stdin)
    26→	if err != nil {
    27→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PostToolUse[TodoWrite] input: %w", err)
    28→	}
    29→
    30→	// Get agent for logging context
    31→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
    32→	if err != nil {
    33→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
    34→	}
    35→
    36→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
    37→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-todo",
    38→		slog.String("hook", "post-todo"),
    39→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
    40→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
    41→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.TranscriptPath),
    42→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
    43→	)
    44→
    45→	// Check if we're in a subagent context by looking for an active pre-task file
    46→	taskToolUseID, found := FindActivePreTaskFile()
    47→	if !found {
    48→		// Not in subagent context - this is a main agent TodoWrite, skip
    49→		return nil
    50→	}
    51→
    52→	// Skip on default branch to avoid polluting main/master history
    53→	if skip, branchName := ShouldSkipOnDefaultBranch(); skip {
    54→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping incremental checkpoint on branch '%s'\n", branchName)
    55→		return nil
    56→	}
    57→
    58→	// Detect file changes since last checkpoint
    59→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(nil)
    60→	if err != nil {
    61→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to detect changed files: %v\n", err)
    62→		return nil
    63→	}
    64→
    65→	// If no file changes, skip creating a checkpoint
    66→	if len(changes.Modified) == 0 && len(changes.New) == 0 && len(changes.Deleted) == 0 {
    67→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping incremental checkpoint\n")
    68→		return nil
    69→	}
    70→
    71→	// Get git author
    72→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
    73→	if err != nil {
    74→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to get git author: %v\n", err)
    75→		return nil
    76→	}
    77→
    78→	// Get the active strategy
    79→	strat := GetStrategy()
    80→
    81→	// Get the session ID from the transcript path or input, then transform to Entire session ID
    82→	sessionID := input.SessionID
    83→	if sessionID == "" {
    84→		sessionID = paths.ExtractSessionIDFromTranscriptPath(input.TranscriptPath)
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	// Get next checkpoint sequence
    88→	seq := GetNextCheckpointSequence(sessionID, taskToolUseID)
    89→
    90→	// Extract the todo content from the tool_input.
    91→	// PostToolUse receives the NEW todo list where the just-completed work is
    92→	// marked as "completed". The last completed item is the work that was just done.
    93→	todoContent := ExtractLastCompletedTodoFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)
    94→	if todoContent == "" {
    95→		// No completed items - this is likely the first TodoWrite (planning phase).
    96→		// Check if there are any todos at all to avoid duplicate messages.
    97→		todoCount := CountTodosFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)
    98→		if todoCount > 0 {
    99→			// Use "Planning: N todos" format for the first TodoWrite
   100→			todoContent = fmt.Sprintf("Planning: %d todos", todoCount)
   101→		}
   102→		// If todoCount == 0, todoContent remains empty and FormatIncrementalMessage
   103→		// will fall back to "Checkpoint #N" format
   104→	}
   105→
   106→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   107→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   108→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   109→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   110→	}
   111→
   112→	// Build incremental task step context
   113→	ctx := strategy.TaskStepContext{
   114→		SessionID:           sessionID,
   115→		ToolUseID:           taskToolUseID,
   116→		ModifiedFiles:       changes.Modified,
   117→		NewFiles:            changes.New,
   118→		DeletedFiles:        changes.Deleted,
   119→		TranscriptPath:      input.TranscriptPath,
   120→		AuthorName:          author.Name,
   121→		AuthorEmail:         author.Email,
   122→		IsIncremental:       true,
   123→		IncrementalSequence: seq,
   124→		IncrementalType:     input.ToolName,
   125→		IncrementalData:     input.ToolInput,
   126→		TodoContent:         todoContent,
   127→		AgentType:           agentType,
   128→	}
   129→
   130→	// Save incremental task step
   131→	if err := strat.SaveTaskStep(ctx); err != nil {
   132→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to save incremental task step: %v\n", err)
   133→		return nil
   134→	}
   135→
   136→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Created incremental checkpoint #%d for %s (task: %s)\n",
   137→		seq, input.ToolName, taskToolUseID[:min(12, len(taskToolUseID))])
   138→	return nil
   139→}
   140→

     1→// lifecycle.go implements the generic lifecycle event dispatcher.
     2→// It routes normalized events from any agent to the appropriate framework actions.
     3→//
     4→// The dispatcher inverts the current flow from "agent handler calls framework functions"
     5→// to "framework dispatcher calls agent methods." Agents are passive data providers;
     6→// the dispatcher handles all orchestration: state transitions, strategy calls,
     7→// file change detection, metadata generation.
     8→package cli
     9→
    10→import (
    11→	"context"
    12→	"errors"
    13→	"fmt"
    14→	"log/slog"
    15→	"os"
    16→	"path/filepath"
    17→	"strings"
    18→	"time"
    19→
    20→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    21→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging"
    22→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    23→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session"
    24→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy"
    25→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/transcript"
    26→)
    27→
    28→// DispatchLifecycleEvent routes a normalized lifecycle event to the appropriate handler.
    29→// Returns nil if the event was handled successfully.
    30→func DispatchLifecycleEvent(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
    31→	if ag == nil {
    32→		return errors.New("agent cannot be nil")
    33→	}
    34→	if event == nil {
    35→		return errors.New("event cannot be nil")
    36→	}
    37→
    38→	switch event.Type {
    39→	case agent.SessionStart:
    40→		return handleLifecycleSessionStart(ag, event)
    41→	case agent.TurnStart:
    42→		return handleLifecycleTurnStart(ag, event)
    43→	case agent.TurnEnd:
    44→		return handleLifecycleTurnEnd(ag, event)
    45→	case agent.Compaction:
    46→		return handleLifecycleCompaction(ag, event)
    47→	case agent.SessionEnd:
    48→		return handleLifecycleSessionEnd(ag, event)
    49→	case agent.SubagentStart:
    50→		return handleLifecycleSubagentStart(ag, event)
    51→	case agent.SubagentEnd:
    52→		return handleLifecycleSubagentEnd(ag, event)
    53→	default:
    54→		return fmt.Errorf("unknown lifecycle event type: %d", event.Type)
    55→	}
    56→}
    57→
    58→// handleLifecycleSessionStart handles session start: shows banner, checks concurrent sessions,
    59→// fires state machine transition.
    60→func handleLifecycleSessionStart(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
    61→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
    62→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-start",
    63→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
    64→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
    65→		slog.String("session_ref", event.SessionRef),
    66→	)
    67→
    68→	if event.SessionID == "" {
    69→		return fmt.Errorf("no session_id in %s event", event.Type)
    70→	}
    71→
    72→	// Build informational message
    73→	message := "\n\nPowered by Entire:\n  This conversation will be linked to your next commit."
    74→
    75→	// Check for concurrent sessions and append count if any
    76→	strat := GetStrategy()
    77→	if concurrentChecker, ok := strat.(strategy.ConcurrentSessionChecker); ok {
    78→		if count, err := concurrentChecker.CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints(event.SessionID); err == nil && count > 0 {
    79→			message += fmt.Sprintf("\n  %d other active conversation(s) in this workspace will also be included.\n  Use 'entire status' for more information.", count)
    80→		}
    81→	}
    82→
    83→	// Output informational message
    84→	if event.ResponseMessage != "" {
    85→		message = event.ResponseMessage
    86→	}
    87→	if err := outputHookResponse(message); err != nil {
    88→		return err
    89→	}
    90→
    91→	// Fire EventSessionStart for the current session (if state exists).
    92→	if state, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(event.SessionID); loadErr != nil {
    93→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state on start: %v\n", loadErr)
    94→	} else if state != nil {
    95→		if transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStart, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
    96→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: session start transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
    97→		}
    98→		if saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); saveErr != nil {
    99→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state on start: %v\n", saveErr)
   100→		}
   101→	}
   102→
   103→	return nil
   104→}
   105→
   106→// handleLifecycleTurnStart handles turn start: captures pre-prompt state,
   107→// ensures strategy setup, initializes session.
   108→func handleLifecycleTurnStart(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   109→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   110→	logging.Info(logCtx, "turn-start",
   111→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   112→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   113→		slog.String("session_ref", event.SessionRef),
   114→	)
   115→
   116→	sessionID := event.SessionID
   117→	if sessionID == "" {
   118→		return fmt.Errorf("no session_id in %s event", event.Type)
   119→	}
   120→
   121→	// Capture pre-prompt state (including transcript position via TranscriptAnalyzer)
   122→	if err := CapturePrePromptState(ag, sessionID, event.SessionRef); err != nil {
   123→		return err
   124→	}
   125→
   126→	// Ensure strategy setup and initialize session
   127→	strat := GetStrategy()
   128→
   129→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   130→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   131→	}
   132→
   133→	if initializer, ok := strat.(strategy.SessionInitializer); ok {
   134→		agentType := ag.Type()
   135→		if err := initializer.InitializeSession(sessionID, agentType, event.SessionRef, event.Prompt); err != nil {
   136→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to initialize session state: %v\n", err)
   137→		}
   138→	}
   139→
   140→	return nil
   141→}
   142→
   143→// handleLifecycleTurnEnd handles turn end: validates transcript, extracts metadata,
   144→// detects file changes, saves step + checkpoint, transitions phase.
   145→//
   146→//nolint:maintidx // high complexity due to sequential orchestration of 8 steps (validation, extraction, file detection, filtering, token calc, step save, phase transition, cleanup) - splitting would obscure the flow
   147→func handleLifecycleTurnEnd(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   148→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   149→	logging.Info(logCtx, "turn-end",
   150→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   151→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   152→		slog.String("session_ref", event.SessionRef),
   153→	)
   154→
   155→	sessionID := event.SessionID
   156→	if sessionID == "" {
   157→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
   158→	}
   159→
   160→	transcriptRef := event.SessionRef
   161→	if transcriptRef == "" || !fileExists(transcriptRef) {
   162→		return fmt.Errorf("transcript file not found or empty: %s", transcriptRef)
   163→	}
   164→
   165→	// Early check: bail out quickly if the repo has no commits yet.
   166→	if repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository(); err == nil && strategy.IsEmptyRepository(repo) {
   167→		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping checkpoint. Will activate after first commit.")
   168→		return NewSilentError(strategy.ErrEmptyRepository)
   169→	}
   170→
   171→	// Create session metadata directory
   172→	sessionDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)
   173→	sessionDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(sessionDir)
   174→	if err != nil {
   175→		sessionDirAbs = sessionDir
   176→	}
   177→	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessionDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
   178→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session directory: %w", err)
   179→	}
   180→
   181→	// If agent implements TranscriptPreparer, wait for transcript to be ready
   182→	if preparer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptPreparer); ok {
   183→		if err := preparer.PrepareTranscript(transcriptRef); err != nil {
   184→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to prepare transcript: %v\n", err)
   185→		}
   186→	}
   187→
   188→	// Copy transcript to session directory
   189→	transcriptData, err := ag.ReadTranscript(transcriptRef)
   190→	if err != nil {
   191→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   192→	}
   193→	logFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   194→	if err := os.WriteFile(logFile, transcriptData, 0o600); err != nil {
   195→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   196→	}
   197→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Copied transcript to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.TranscriptFileName)
   198→
   199→	// Load pre-prompt state (captured on TurnStart)
   200→	preState, err := LoadPrePromptState(sessionID)
   201→	if err != nil {

   203→	}
   204→
   205→	// Determine transcript offset
   206→	transcriptOffset := resolveTranscriptOffset(preState, sessionID)
   207→
   208→	// Extract metadata via agent interface (prompts, summary, modified files)
   209→	var allPrompts []string

   211→	var modifiedFiles []string

   222→		// Extract summary

   229→		// Extract modified files (agent handles its own format)

   238→	// Write prompts file

   273→	// Detect file changes via git status

   279→	// Filter and normalize all paths

   287→	// Check if there are any changes

   289→	if totalChanges == 0 {

   291→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipping commit\n")
   292→		transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   293→		if cleanupErr := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); cleanupErr != nil {
   294→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", cleanupErr)
   295→		}
   296→		return nil

   298→
   299→	// Log file changes
   300→	logFileChanges(relModifiedFiles, relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles)
   301→
   302→	// Create context file

   314→
   315→	// Get strategy and agent type
   316→	strat := GetStrategy()
   317→	agentType := ag.Type()
   318→
   319→	// Get transcript position/identifier from pre-prompt state

   322→	if preState != nil {

   331→		if tokenErr != nil {

   333→		} else {

   336→	}
   337→
   338→	// Build fully-populated step context and delegate to strategy
   339→	ctx := strategy.StepContext{
   340→		SessionID:                sessionID,
   341→		ModifiedFiles:            relModifiedFiles,
   342→		NewFiles:                 relNewFiles,
   343→		DeletedFiles:             relDeletedFiles,
   344→		MetadataDir:              sessionDir,
   345→		MetadataDirAbs:           sessionDirAbs,
   346→		CommitMessage:            commitMessage,
   347→		TranscriptPath:           transcriptRef,
   348→		AuthorName:               author.Name,
   349→		AuthorEmail:              author.Email,
   350→		AgentType:                agentType,
   351→		StepTranscriptIdentifier: transcriptIdentifierAtStart,
   352→		StepTranscriptStart:      transcriptLinesAtStart,
   353→		TokenUsage:               tokenUsage,
   354→	}
   355→
   356→	if err := strat.SaveStep(ctx); err != nil {
   357→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save step: %w", err)
   358→	}
   359→
   360→	// Update session state transcript position for auto-commit strategy
   361→	if strat.Name() == strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit && newTranscriptPosition > 0 {
   362→		updateAutoCommitTranscriptPosition(sessionID, newTranscriptPosition)
   363→	}
   364→
   365→	// Transition session phase and cleanup
   366→	transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   367→	if cleanupErr := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); cleanupErr != nil {
   368→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", cleanupErr)
   369→	}
   370→
   371→	return nil
   372→}
   373→
   374→// handleLifecycleCompaction handles context compaction: saves current progress
   375→// but stays in ACTIVE phase (unlike TurnEnd which transitions to IDLE).
   376→// Also resets the transcript offset since the transcript may be truncated.
   377→func handleLifecycleCompaction(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   378→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   379→	logging.Info(logCtx, "compaction",
   380→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   381→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   382→	)
   383→
   384→	// Fire EventCompaction to trigger ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched (stays in ACTIVE)
   385→	sessionID := event.SessionID
   386→	sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   387→	if loadErr != nil {
   388→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state for compaction: %v\n", loadErr)
   389→	}
   390→	if sessionState != nil {
   391→		if transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(sessionState, session.EventCompaction, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
   392→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: compaction transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
   393→		}
   394→
   395→		// Reset transcript offset since the transcript may be truncated/reorganized
   396→		sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0
   397→
   398→		if saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); saveErr != nil {
   399→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to save session state after compaction: %v\n", saveErr)
   400→		}
   401→	}
   402→
   403→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Context compaction: transcript offset reset\n")
   404→	return nil
   405→}
   406→
   407→// handleLifecycleSessionEnd handles session end: marks the session as ended.
   408→func handleLifecycleSessionEnd(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   409→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   410→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-end",
   411→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   412→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   413→	)
   414→
   415→	if event.SessionID == "" {
   416→		return nil // No session to update
   417→	}
   418→
   419→	if err := markSessionEnded(event.SessionID); err != nil {
   420→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to mark session ended: %v\n", err)
   421→	}
   422→	return nil
   423→}
   424→
   425→// handleLifecycleSubagentStart handles subagent start: captures pre-task state.
   426→func handleLifecycleSubagentStart(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   427→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   428→	logging.Info(logCtx, "subagent-start",
   429→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   430→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   431→		slog.String("tool_use_id", event.ToolUseID),
   432→	)
   433→
   434→	// Log context
   435→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stdout, "[entire] Subagent started\n")
   436→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stdout, "  Session ID: %s\n", event.SessionID)
   437→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stdout, "  Tool Use ID: %s\n", event.ToolUseID)
   438→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stdout, "  Transcript: %s\n", event.SessionRef)
   439→
   440→	// Capture pre-task state
   441→	if err := CapturePreTaskState(event.ToolUseID); err != nil {
   442→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to capture pre-task state: %w", err)
   443→	}
   444→
   445→	return nil
   446→}
   447→
   448→// handleLifecycleSubagentEnd handles subagent end: detects changes, saves task checkpoint.
   449→func handleLifecycleSubagentEnd(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   450→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   451→	logging.Info(logCtx, "subagent-end",
   452→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   453→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   454→		slog.String("tool_use_id", event.ToolUseID),
   455→		slog.String("subagent_id", event.SubagentID),
   456→	)
   457→
   458→	// Extract subagent type and description from tool input
   459→	subagentType, taskDescription := ParseSubagentTypeAndDescription(event.ToolInput)
   460→
   461→	// Determine subagent transcript path
   462→	transcriptDir := filepath.Dir(event.SessionRef)
   463→	var subagentTranscriptPath string
   464→	if event.SubagentID != "" {
   465→		subagentTranscriptPath = AgentTranscriptPath(transcriptDir, event.SubagentID)
   466→		if !fileExists(subagentTranscriptPath) {
   467→			subagentTranscriptPath = ""
   468→		}
   469→	}
   470→
   471→	// Log context
   472→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stdout, "[entire] Subagent completed\n")
   473→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stdout, "  Session ID: %s\n", event.SessionID)
   474→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stdout, "  Tool Use ID: %s\n", event.ToolUseID)
   475→	if event.SubagentID != "" {
   476→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stdout, "  Agent ID: %s\n", event.SubagentID)
   477→	}
   478→	if subagentTranscriptPath != "" {
   479→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stdout, "  Subagent Transcript: %s\n", subagentTranscriptPath)
   480→	}
   481→
   482→	// Extract modified files from subagent transcript
   483→	var modifiedFiles []string
   484→	if analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer); ok {
   485→		transcriptToScan := event.SessionRef
   486→		if subagentTranscriptPath != "" {
   487→			transcriptToScan = subagentTranscriptPath
   488→		}
   489→		if files, _, fileErr := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(transcriptToScan, 0); fileErr != nil {
   490→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files from subagent: %v\n", fileErr)
   491→		} else {
   492→			modifiedFiles = files
   493→		}
   494→	}
   495→
   496→	// Load pre-task state and detect file changes
   497→	preState, err := LoadPreTaskState(event.ToolUseID)
   498→	if err != nil {
   499→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-task state: %v\n", err)
   500→	}
   501→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preState.PreUntrackedFiles())
   502→	if err != nil {
   503→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   504→	}
   505→
   506→	// Get repo root and normalize paths
   507→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   508→	if err != nil {
   509→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   510→	}
   511→
   512→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   513→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   514→	if changes != nil {
   515→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   516→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   517→	}
   518→
   519→	// If no changes, skip
   520→	if len(relModifiedFiles) == 0 && len(relNewFiles) == 0 && len(relDeletedFiles) == 0 {
   521→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping task checkpoint\n")
   522→		_ = CleanupPreTaskState(event.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   523→		return nil
   524→	}
   525→
   526→	// Find checkpoint UUID from main transcript (best-effort)
   527→	var checkpointUUID string
   528→	if analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer); ok {
   529→		// Use ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset on the main transcript to get position info
   530→		// and then look for checkpoint UUID via the legacy path
   531→		_ = analyzer // UUID lookup requires transcript parsing — delegate to existing helper
   532→	}
   533→	// Fall back to the existing CLI-level checkpoint UUID finder
   534→	mainLines, _, _ := parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID(event.SessionRef) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort
   535→	if mainLines != nil {
   536→		checkpointUUID, _ = FindCheckpointUUID(mainLines, event.ToolUseID)
   537→	}
   538→
   539→	// Get git author
   540→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   541→	if err != nil {
   542→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   543→	}
   544→
   545→	// Build task checkpoint context
   546→	strat := GetStrategy()
   547→	agentType := ag.Type()
   548→
   549→	ctx := strategy.TaskStepContext{
   550→		SessionID:              event.SessionID,
   551→		ToolUseID:              event.ToolUseID,
   552→		AgentID:                event.SubagentID,
   553→		ModifiedFiles:          relModifiedFiles,
   554→		NewFiles:               relNewFiles,
   555→		DeletedFiles:           relDeletedFiles,
   556→		TranscriptPath:         event.SessionRef,
   557→		SubagentTranscriptPath: subagentTranscriptPath,
   558→		CheckpointUUID:         checkpointUUID,
   559→		AuthorName:             author.Name,
   560→		AuthorEmail:            author.Email,
   561→		SubagentType:           subagentType,
   562→		TaskDescription:        taskDescription,
   563→		AgentType:              agentType,
   564→	}
   565→
   566→	if err := strat.SaveTaskStep(ctx); err != nil {
   567→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save task step: %w", err)
   568→	}
   569→
   570→	_ = CleanupPreTaskState(event.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   571→	return nil
   572→}
   573→
   574→// --- Helper functions ---
   575→
   576→// resolveTranscriptOffset determines the transcript offset to use for parsing.
   577→// Prefers pre-prompt state, falls back to session state.
   578→func resolveTranscriptOffset(preState *PrePromptState, sessionID string) int {
   579→	if preState != nil && preState.TranscriptOffset > 0 {
   580→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state found: parsing transcript from offset %d\n", preState.TranscriptOffset)
   581→		return preState.TranscriptOffset
   582→	}
   583→
   584→	// Fall back to session state (e.g., auto-commit strategy updates it after each save)
   585→	sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   586→	if loadErr != nil {
   587→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   588→		return 0
   589→	}
   590→	if sessionState != nil && sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart > 0 {
   591→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session state found: parsing transcript from offset %d\n", sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   592→		return sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart
   593→	}
   594→
   595→	return 0
   596→}
   597→
   598→// updateAutoCommitTranscriptPosition updates the session state with the new transcript position
   599→// for the auto-commit strategy.
   600→func updateAutoCommitTranscriptPosition(sessionID string, newPosition int) {
   601→	sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   602→	if loadErr != nil {
   603→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   604→		return
   605→	}
   606→	if sessionState == nil {
   607→		sessionState = &strategy.SessionState{
   608→			SessionID: sessionID,
   609→		}
   610→	}
   611→	sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = newPosition
   612→	sessionState.StepCount++
   613→	if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); updateErr != nil {
   614→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", updateErr)
   615→	} else {
   616→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Updated session state: transcript position=%d, checkpoint=%d\n",
   617→			newPosition, sessionState.StepCount)
   618→	}
   619→}
   620→
   621→// createContextFile creates a context.md file for the session checkpoint.
   622→// This is a unified version that works for all agents.
   623→func createContextFile(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID string, prompts []string, summary string) error {
   624→	var sb strings.Builder
   625→
   626→	sb.WriteString("# Session Context\n\n")
   627→	sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("Session ID: %s\n", sessionID))
   628→	sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("Commit Message: %s\n\n", commitMessage))
   629→
   630→	if len(prompts) > 0 {
   631→		sb.WriteString("## Prompts\n\n")
   632→		for i, p := range prompts {
   633→			sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("### Prompt %d\n\n%s\n\n", i+1, p))
   634→		}
   635→	}
   636→
   637→	if summary != "" {
   638→		sb.WriteString("## Summary\n\n")
   639→		sb.WriteString(summary)
   640→		sb.WriteString("\n")
   641→	}
   642→
   643→	if err := os.WriteFile(contextFile, []byte(sb.String()), 0o600); err != nil {
   644→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write context file: %w", err)
   645→	}
   646→	return nil
   647→}
   648→
   649→// parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID is a thin wrapper around transcript parsing for checkpoint UUID lookup.
   650→// Returns parsed transcript lines for use with FindCheckpointUUID.
   651→func parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID(transcriptPath string) ([]transcriptLine, int, error) {
   652→	lines, total, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(transcriptPath, 0)
   653→	if err != nil {
   654→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("parsing transcript for checkpoint UUID: %w", err)
   655→	}
   656→	return lines, total, nil
   657→}
   658→
   659→// transitionSessionTurnEnd transitions the session phase to IDLE and dispatches turn-end actions.
   660→func transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID string) {
   661→	turnState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   662→	if loadErr != nil {
   663→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state for turn end: %v\n", loadErr)
   664→		return
   665→	}
   666→	if turnState == nil {
   667→		return
   668→	}
   669→	if err := strategy.TransitionAndLog(turnState, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); err != nil {
   670→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end transition failed: %v\n", err)
   671→	}
   672→
   673→	// Always dispatch to strategy for turn-end handling. The strategy reads
   674→	// work items from state (e.g. TurnCheckpointIDs), not the action list.
   675→	strat := GetStrategy()
   676→	if handler, ok := strat.(strategy.TurnEndHandler); ok {
   677→		if err := handler.HandleTurnEnd(turnState); err != nil {
   678→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end action dispatch failed: %v\n", err)
   679→		}
   680→	}
   681→
   682→	if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(turnState); updateErr != nil {
   683→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session phase on turn end: %v\n", updateErr)
   684→	}
   685→}
   686→
   687→// markSessionEnded transitions the session to ENDED phase via the state machine.
   688→func markSessionEnded(sessionID string) error {
   689→	state, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   690→	if err != nil {
   691→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   692→	}
   693→	if state == nil {
   694→		return nil // No state file, nothing to update
   695→	}
   696→
   697→	if transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStop, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
   698→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: session stop transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
   699→	}
   700→
   701→	now := time.Now()
   702→	state.EndedAt = &now
   703→
   704→	if err := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   705→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   706→	}
   707→	return nil
   708→}
   709→
   710→// logFileChanges logs the files modified, created, and deleted during a session.
   711→func logFileChanges(modified, newFiles, deleted []string) {
   712→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files modified during session (%d):\n", len(modified))
   713→	for _, file := range modified {
   714→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   715→	}
   716→	if len(newFiles) > 0 {
   717→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "New files created (%d):\n", len(newFiles))
   718→		for _, file := range newFiles {
   719→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   720→		}
   721→	}
   722→	if len(deleted) > 0 {
   723→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files deleted (%d):\n", len(deleted))
   724→		for _, file := range deleted {
   725→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   726→		}
   727→	}
   728→}

     1→// Package agent provides interfaces and types for integrating with coding agents.
     2→// It abstracts agent-specific behavior (hooks, log parsing, session storage) so that
     3→// the same Strategy implementations can work with any coding agent.
     4→package agent
     5→
     6→import (
     7→	"io"
     8→)
     9→
    10→// Agent defines the interface for interacting with a coding agent.
    11→// Each agent implementation (Claude Code, Cursor, Aider, etc.) converts its
    12→// native format to the normalized types defined in this package.
    13→//
    14→// The interface is organized into four groups:
    15→//
    16→//   - Identity (5 methods): Name, Type, Description, DetectPresence, ProtectedDirs
    17→//   - Event Mapping (2 methods): HookNames, ParseHookEvent
    18→//   - Transcript Storage (3 methods): ReadTranscript, ChunkTranscript, ReassembleTranscript
    19→//   - Legacy (8 methods): Will be moved to optional interfaces or removed in a future phase
    20→type Agent interface {
    21→	// --- Identity ---
    22→
    23→	// Name returns the agent registry key (e.g., "claude-code", "gemini")
    24→	Name() AgentName
    25→
    26→	// Type returns the agent type identifier (e.g., "Claude Code", "Gemini CLI")
    27→	// This is stored in metadata and trailers.
    28→	Type() AgentType
    29→
    30→	// Description returns a human-readable description for UI
    31→	Description() string
    32→
    33→	// DetectPresence checks if this agent is configured in the repository
    34→	DetectPresence() (bool, error)
    35→
    36→	// ProtectedDirs returns repo-root-relative directories that should never be
    37→	// modified or deleted during rewind or other destructive operations.
    38→	// Examples: [".claude"] for Claude, [".gemini"] for Gemini.
    39→	ProtectedDirs() []string
    40→
    41→	// --- Event Mapping ---
    42→
    43→	// HookNames returns the hook verbs this agent supports.
    44→	// These become subcommands under `entire hooks <agent>`.
    45→	// e.g., ["stop", "user-prompt-submit", "session-start", "session-end"]
    46→	HookNames() []string
    47→
    48→	// ParseHookEvent translates an agent-native hook into a normalized lifecycle Event.
    49→	// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance (e.g., pass-through hooks).
    50→	// This is the core contribution surface for new agent implementations.
    51→	ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*Event, error)
    52→
    53→	// --- Transcript Storage ---
    54→
    55→	// ReadTranscript reads the raw transcript bytes for a session.
    56→	ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error)
    57→
    58→	// ChunkTranscript splits a transcript into chunks if it exceeds maxSize.
    59→	// Returns a slice of chunks. If the transcript fits in one chunk, returns single-element slice.
    60→	// The chunking is format-aware: JSONL splits at line boundaries, JSON splits message arrays.
    61→	ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error)
    62→
    63→	// ReassembleTranscript combines chunks back into a single transcript.
    64→	// Handles format-specific reassembly (JSONL concatenation, JSON message merging).
    65→	ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error)
    66→
    67→	// --- Legacy methods (will move to optional interfaces in Phase 4) ---
    68→
    69→	// GetHookConfigPath returns path to hook config file (empty if none).
    70→	GetHookConfigPath() string
    71→
    72→	// SupportsHooks returns true if agent supports lifecycle hooks.
    73→	SupportsHooks() bool
    74→
    75→	// ParseHookInput parses hook callback input from stdin.
    76→	ParseHookInput(hookType HookType, reader io.Reader) (*HookInput, error)
    77→
    78→	// GetSessionID extracts session ID from hook input.
    79→	GetSessionID(input *HookInput) string
    80→
    81→	// GetSessionDir returns where agent stores session data for this repo.
    82→	GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error)
    83→
    84→	// ResolveSessionFile returns the path to the session transcript file.
    85→	ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string
    86→
    87→	// ReadSession reads session data from agent's storage.
    88→	ReadSession(input *HookInput) (*AgentSession, error)
    89→
    90→	// WriteSession writes session data for resumption.
    91→	WriteSession(session *AgentSession) error
    92→
    93→	// FormatResumeCommand returns command to resume a session.
    94→	FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string
    95→}
    96→
    97→// HookSupport is implemented by agents with lifecycle hooks.
    98→// This optional interface allows agents like Claude Code and Cursor to
    99→// install and manage hooks that notify Entire of agent events.
   100→type HookSupport interface {
   101→	Agent
   102→
   103→	// InstallHooks installs agent-specific hooks.
   104→	// If localDev is true, hooks point to local development build.
   105→	// If force is true, removes existing Entire hooks before installing.
   106→	// Returns the number of hooks installed.
   107→	InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error)
   108→
   109→	// UninstallHooks removes installed hooks
   110→	UninstallHooks() error
   111→
   112→	// AreHooksInstalled checks if hooks are currently installed
   113→	AreHooksInstalled() bool
   114→
   115→	// GetSupportedHooks returns the hook types this agent supports
   116→	GetSupportedHooks() []HookType
   117→}
   118→
   119→// HookHandler is implemented by agents that define their own hook vocabulary.
   120→// HookNames() is now part of the core Agent interface.
   121→// This interface is kept for backward compatibility during migration.
   122→type HookHandler interface {
   123→	Agent
   124→
   125→	// GetHookNames returns the hook verbs this agent supports.
   126→	GetHookNames() []string
   127→}
   128→
   129→// FileWatcher is implemented by agents that use file-based detection.
   130→// Agents like Aider that don't support hooks can use file watching
   131→// to detect session activity.
   132→type FileWatcher interface {
   133→	Agent
   134→
   135→	// GetWatchPaths returns paths to watch for session changes
   136→	GetWatchPaths() ([]string, error)
   137→
   138→	// OnFileChange handles a detected file change and returns session info
   139→	OnFileChange(path string) (*SessionChange, error)
   140→}
   141→
   142→// TranscriptAnalyzer provides format-specific transcript parsing.
   143→// Agents that implement this get richer checkpoints (transcript-derived file lists,
   144→// prompts, summaries). Agents that don't still participate in the checkpoint lifecycle
   145→// via git-status-based file detection and raw transcript storage.
   146→type TranscriptAnalyzer interface {
   147→	Agent
   148→
   149→	// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current position (length) of a transcript.
   150→	// For JSONL formats (Claude Code), this is the line count.
   151→	// For JSON formats (Gemini CLI), this is the message count.
   152→	// Returns 0 if the file doesn't exist or is empty.
   153→	GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error)
   154→
   155→	// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given offset.
   156→	// For JSONL formats (Claude Code), offset is the starting line number.
   157→	// For JSON formats (Gemini CLI), offset is the starting message index.
   158→	// Returns:
   159→	//   - files: list of file paths modified by the agent (from Write/Edit tools)
   160→	//   - currentPosition: the current position (line count or message count)
   161→	//   - error: any error encountered during reading
   162→	ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error)
   163→
   164→	// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompts from the transcript starting at the given offset.
   165→	ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error)
   166→
   167→	// ExtractSummary extracts a summary of the session from the transcript.
   168→	ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error)
   169→}
   170→
   171→// TranscriptPreparer is called before ReadTranscript to handle agent-specific
   172→// flush/sync requirements (e.g., Claude Code's async transcript writing).
   173→// The framework calls PrepareTranscript before ReadTranscript if implemented.
   174→type TranscriptPreparer interface {
   175→	Agent
   176→
   177→	// PrepareTranscript ensures the transcript is ready to read.
   178→	// For Claude Code, this waits for the async transcript flush to complete.
   179→	PrepareTranscript(sessionRef string) error
   180→}
   181→
   182→// TokenCalculator provides token usage calculation for a session.

   186→

   188→	CalculateTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) (*TokenUsage, error)

    14→// TranscriptLine is an alias to the shared transcript.Line type.
    15→type TranscriptLine = transcript.Line
    16→
    17→// Type aliases for internal use.
    18→type (
    19→	userMessage      = transcript.UserMessage
    20→	assistantMessage = transcript.AssistantMessage

    23→
    24→// SerializeTranscript converts transcript lines back to JSONL bytes
    25→func SerializeTranscript(lines []TranscriptLine) ([]byte, error) {
    26→	var buf bytes.Buffer
    27→	for _, line := range lines {
    28→		data, err := json.Marshal(line)
    29→		if err != nil {
    30→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal line: %w", err)
    31→		}
    32→		buf.Write(data)
    33→		buf.WriteByte('\n')
    34→	}
    35→	return buf.Bytes(), nil
    36→}
    37→
    38→// ExtractModifiedFiles extracts files modified by tool calls from transcript
    39→func ExtractModifiedFiles(lines []TranscriptLine) []string {
    40→	fileSet := make(map[string]bool)
    41→	var files []string
    42→
    43→	for _, line := range lines {
    44→		if line.Type != "assistant" {
    45→			continue
    46→		}
    47→
    48→		var msg assistantMessage
    49→		if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
    50→			continue
    51→		}
    52→
    53→		for _, block := range msg.Content {
    54→			if block.Type != "tool_use" {
    55→				continue
    56→			}
    57→
    58→			// Check if it's a file modification tool
    59→			isModifyTool := false
    60→			for _, name := range FileModificationTools {
    61→				if block.Name == name {
    62→					isModifyTool = true
    63→					break
    64→				}
    65→			}
    66→
    67→			if !isModifyTool {
    68→				continue
    69→			}
    70→
    71→			var input toolInput
    72→			if err := json.Unmarshal(block.Input, &input); err != nil {
    73→				continue
    74→			}
    75→
    76→			file := input.FilePath
    77→			if file == "" {
    78→				file = input.NotebookPath
    79→			}
    80→
    81→			if file != "" && !fileSet[file] {
    82→				fileSet[file] = true
    83→				files = append(files, file)
    84→			}
    85→		}
    86→	}
    87→
    88→	return files
    89→}
    90→
    91→// ExtractLastUserPrompt extracts the last user message from transcript
    92→func ExtractLastUserPrompt(lines []TranscriptLine) string {
    93→	for i := len(lines) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
    94→		if lines[i].Type != "user" { //nolint:goconst // already present in codebase
    95→			continue
    96→		}
    97→
    98→		var msg userMessage
    99→		if err := json.Unmarshal(lines[i].Message, &msg); err != nil {
   100→			continue
   101→		}
   102→
   103→		// Handle string content
   104→		if str, ok := msg.Content.(string); ok {
   105→			return str
   106→		}
   107→
   108→		// Handle array content (text blocks)
   109→		if arr, ok := msg.Content.([]interface{}); ok {
   110→			var texts []string
   111→			for _, item := range arr {
   112→				if m, ok := item.(map[string]interface{}); ok {
   113→					if m["type"] == "text" {
   114→						if text, ok := m["text"].(string); ok {
   115→							texts = append(texts, text)
   116→						}
   117→					}
   118→				}
   119→			}
   120→			if len(texts) > 0 {
   121→				return strings.Join(texts, "\n\n")
   122→			}
   123→		}
   124→	}
   125→	return ""
   126→}
   127→
   128→// TruncateAtUUID returns transcript lines up to and including the line with given UUID
   129→func TruncateAtUUID(lines []TranscriptLine, uuid string) []TranscriptLine {
   130→	if uuid == "" {
   131→		return lines
   132→	}
   133→
   134→	for i, line := range lines {
   135→		if line.UUID == uuid {
   136→			return lines[:i+1]
   137→		}
   138→	}
   139→
   140→	// UUID not found, return full transcript
   141→	return lines
   142→}
   143→
   144→// toolResultBlock represents a tool_result in a user message
   145→type toolResultBlock struct {
   146→	Type      string `json:"type"`
   147→	ToolUseID string `json:"tool_use_id"`
   148→}
   149→
   150→// userMessageWithToolResults represents a user message that may contain tool results
   151→type userMessageWithToolResults struct {
   152→	Content []toolResultBlock `json:"content"`
   153→}
   154→
   155→// FindCheckpointUUID finds the UUID of the message containing the tool_result
   156→// for the given tool_use_id
   157→func FindCheckpointUUID(lines []TranscriptLine, toolUseID string) (string, bool) {
   158→	for _, line := range lines {
   159→		if line.Type != "user" {
   160→			continue
   161→		}
   162→
   163→		var msg userMessageWithToolResults
   164→		if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   165→			continue
   166→		}
   167→
   168→		for _, block := range msg.Content {
   169→			if block.Type == "tool_result" && block.ToolUseID == toolUseID {
   170→				return line.UUID, true
   171→			}
   172→		}
   173→	}
   174→	return "", false
   175→}
   176→
   177→// CalculateTokenUsage calculates token usage from a Claude Code transcript.
   178→// This is specific to Claude/Anthropic's API format where each assistant message
   179→// contains a usage object with input_tokens, output_tokens, and cache tokens.
   180→//
   181→// Due to streaming, multiple transcript rows may share the same message.id.
   182→// We deduplicate by taking the row with the highest output_tokens for each message.id.
   183→func CalculateTokenUsage(transcript []TranscriptLine) *agent.TokenUsage {
   184→	// Map from message.id to the usage with highest output_tokens
   185→	usageByMessageID := make(map[string]messageUsage)
   186→
   187→	for _, line := range transcript {
   188→		if line.Type != "assistant" {
   189→			continue
   190→		}
   191→
   192→		var msg messageWithUsage
   193→		if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   194→			continue
   195→		}
   196→
   197→		if msg.ID == "" {
   198→			continue
   199→		}
   200→
   201→		// Keep the entry with highest output_tokens (final streaming state)
   202→		existing, exists := usageByMessageID[msg.ID]
   203→		if !exists || msg.Usage.OutputTokens > existing.OutputTokens {
   204→			usageByMessageID[msg.ID] = msg.Usage
   205→		}
   206→	}
   207→
   208→	// Sum up all unique messages
   209→	usage := &agent.TokenUsage{
   210→		APICallCount: len(usageByMessageID),
   211→	}
   212→	for _, u := range usageByMessageID {
   213→		usage.InputTokens += u.InputTokens
   214→		usage.CacheCreationTokens += u.CacheCreationInputTokens
   215→		usage.CacheReadTokens += u.CacheReadInputTokens
   216→		usage.OutputTokens += u.OutputTokens
   217→	}
   218→
   219→	return usage
   220→}
   221→
   222→// CalculateTokenUsageFromFile calculates token usage from a Claude Code transcript file.
   223→// If startLine > 0, only considers lines from startLine onwards.
   224→func CalculateTokenUsageFromFile(path string, startLine int) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   225→	if path == "" {
   226→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}, nil
   227→	}
   228→
   229→	lines, _, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(path, startLine)
   230→	if err != nil {
   231→		return nil, err //nolint:wrapcheck // caller adds context
   232→	}
   233→
   234→	return CalculateTokenUsage(lines), nil
   235→}
   236→
   237→// ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs extracts agent IDs from Task tool results in a transcript.
   238→// When a Task tool completes, the tool_result contains "agentId: <id>" in its content.
   239→// Returns a map of agentID -> toolUseID for all spawned agents.
   240→func ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs(transcript []TranscriptLine) map[string]string {
   241→	agentIDs := make(map[string]string)
   242→
   243→	for _, line := range transcript {
   244→		if line.Type != "user" {
   245→			continue
   246→		}
   247→
   248→		// Parse as array of content blocks (tool results)
   249→		var contentBlocks []struct {
   250→			Type      string          `json:"type"`
   251→			ToolUseID string          `json:"tool_use_id"`
   252→			Content   json.RawMessage `json:"content"`
   253→		}
   254→
   255→		var msg struct {
   256→			Content json.RawMessage `json:"content"`
   257→		}
   258→		if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   259→			continue
   260→		}
   261→
   262→		if err := json.Unmarshal(msg.Content, &contentBlocks); err != nil {
   263→			continue
   264→		}
   265→
   266→		for _, block := range contentBlocks {
   267→			if block.Type != "tool_result" {
   268→				continue
   269→			}
   270→
   271→			// Content can be a string or array of text blocks
   272→			var textContent string
   273→
   274→			// Try as array of text blocks first
   275→			var textBlocks []struct {
   276→				Type string `json:"type"`
   277→				Text string `json:"text"`
   278→			}
   279→			if err := json.Unmarshal(block.Content, &textBlocks); err == nil {
   280→				var textContentSb361 strings.Builder
   281→				for _, tb := range textBlocks {
   282→					if tb.Type == "text" {
   283→						textContentSb361.WriteString(tb.Text + "\n")
   284→					}
   285→				}
   286→				textContent += textContentSb361.String()
   287→			} else {
   288→				// Try as plain string
   289→				var str string
   290→				if err := json.Unmarshal(block.Content, &str); err == nil {
   291→					textContent = str
   292→				}
   293→			}
   294→
   295→			// Look for agentId in the text
   296→			if agentID := extractAgentIDFromText(textContent); agentID != "" {
   297→				agentIDs[agentID] = block.ToolUseID
   298→			}
   299→		}
   300→	}
   301→
   302→	return agentIDs
   303→}
   304→
   305→// extractAgentIDFromText extracts an agent ID from text containing "agentId: <id>".
   306→func extractAgentIDFromText(text string) string {
   307→	const prefix = "agentId: "
   308→	idx := strings.Index(text, prefix)
   309→	if idx == -1 {
   310→		return ""
   311→	}
   312→
   313→	// Extract the ID (alphanumeric characters after the prefix)
   314→	start := idx + len(prefix)
   315→	end := start
   316→	for end < len(text) && (text[end] >= 'a' && text[end] <= 'z' ||
   317→		text[end] >= 'A' && text[end] <= 'Z' ||
   318→		text[end] >= '0' && text[end] <= '9') {
   319→		end++
   320→	}
   321→
   322→	if end > start {
   323→		return text[start:end]
   324→	}
   325→	return ""
   326→}
   327→
   328→// CalculateTotalTokenUsage calculates token usage for a turn, including subagents.
   329→// It parses the main transcript from startLine, extracts spawned agent IDs,
   330→// and calculates their token usage from transcripts in subagentsDir.
   331→func CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptPath string, startLine int, subagentsDir string) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   332→	if transcriptPath == "" {
   333→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}, nil
   334→	}
   335→
   336→	// Parse transcript ONCE
   337→	parsed, _, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(transcriptPath, startLine)
   338→	if err != nil {
   339→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   340→	}
   341→
   342→	// Calculate token usage from parsed transcript
   343→	mainUsage := CalculateTokenUsage(parsed)
   344→
   345→	// Extract spawned agent IDs from the same parsed transcript
   346→	agentIDs := ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs(parsed)
   347→
   348→	// Calculate subagent token usage
   349→	if len(agentIDs) > 0 {
   350→		subagentUsage := &agent.TokenUsage{}
   351→		for agentID := range agentIDs {
   352→			agentPath := filepath.Join(subagentsDir, fmt.Sprintf("agent-%s.jsonl", agentID))
   353→			agentUsage, err := CalculateTokenUsageFromFile(agentPath, 0)
   354→			if err != nil {
   355→				// Agent transcript may not exist yet or may have been cleaned up
   356→				continue
   357→			}
   358→			subagentUsage.InputTokens += agentUsage.InputTokens
   359→			subagentUsage.CacheCreationTokens += agentUsage.CacheCreationTokens
   360→			subagentUsage.CacheReadTokens += agentUsage.CacheReadTokens
   361→			subagentUsage.OutputTokens += agentUsage.OutputTokens
   362→			subagentUsage.APICallCount += agentUsage.APICallCount
   363→		}
   364→		if subagentUsage.APICallCount > 0 {
   365→			mainUsage.SubagentTokens = subagentUsage
   366→		}
   367→	}
   368→
   369→	return mainUsage, nil
   370→}
   371→
   372→// ExtractAllModifiedFiles extracts files modified by both the main agent and
   373→// any subagents spawned via the Task tool. It parses the main transcript from
   374→// startLine, collects modified files from the main agent, then reads each
   375→// subagent's transcript from subagentsDir to collect their modified files too.
   376→// The result is a deduplicated list of all modified file paths.
   377→func ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptPath string, startLine int, subagentsDir string) ([]string, error) {
   378→	if transcriptPath == "" {
   379→		return nil, nil
   380→	}
   381→
   382→	// Parse main transcript once
   383→	parsed, _, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(transcriptPath, startLine)
   384→	if err != nil {
   385→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   386→	}
   387→
   388→	// Collect modified files from main agent
   389→	fileSet := make(map[string]bool)
   390→	var files []string
   391→	for _, f := range ExtractModifiedFiles(parsed) {
   392→		if !fileSet[f] {
   393→			fileSet[f] = true
   394→			files = append(files, f)
   395→		}
   396→	}
   397→
   398→	// Find spawned subagents and collect their modified files
   399→	agentIDs := ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs(parsed)
   400→	for agentID := range agentIDs {
   401→		agentPath := filepath.Join(subagentsDir, fmt.Sprintf("agent-%s.jsonl", agentID))
   402→		agentLines, _, agentErr := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(agentPath, 0)
   403→		if agentErr != nil {
   404→			// Subagent transcript may not exist yet or may have been cleaned up
   405→			continue
   406→		}
   407→		for _, f := range ExtractModifiedFiles(agentLines) {
   408→			if !fileSet[f] {
   409→				fileSet[f] = true
   410→				files = append(files, f)
   411→			}
   412→		}
   413→	}
   414→
   415→	return files, nil
   416→}

    25→// ClaudeCodeAgent implements the Agent interface for Claude Code.
    26→//
    27→//nolint:revive // ClaudeCodeAgent is clearer than Agent in this context
    28→type ClaudeCodeAgent struct{}
    29→
    30→// NewClaudeCodeAgent creates a new Claude Code agent instance.
    31→func NewClaudeCodeAgent() agent.Agent {
    32→	return &ClaudeCodeAgent{}
    33→}
    34→
    35→// Name returns the agent registry key.
    36→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) Name() agent.AgentName {

    38→}
    39→
    40→// Type returns the agent type identifier.

    43→}

    45→// Description returns a human-readable description.
    46→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) Description() string {

    48→}
    49→
    50→// DetectPresence checks if Claude Code is configured in the repository.
    51→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) DetectPresence() (bool, error) {
    52→	// Get repo root to check for .claude directory
    53→	// This is needed because the CLI may be run from a subdirectory
    54→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    55→	if err != nil {
    56→		// Not in a git repo, fall back to CWD-relative check
    57→		repoRoot = "."
    58→	}
    59→
    60→	// Check for .claude directory
    61→	claudeDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".claude")
    62→	if _, err := os.Stat(claudeDir); err == nil {
    63→		return true, nil
    64→	}
    65→	// Check for .claude/settings.json
    66→	settingsFile := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".claude", "settings.json")
    67→	if _, err := os.Stat(settingsFile); err == nil {
    68→		return true, nil
    69→	}
    70→	return false, nil
    71→}
    72→
    73→// GetHookConfigPath returns the path to Claude's hook config file.
    74→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetHookConfigPath() string {
    75→	return ".claude/settings.json"
    76→}
    77→
    78→// SupportsHooks returns true as Claude Code supports lifecycle hooks.
    79→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) SupportsHooks() bool {
    80→	return true
    81→}
    82→
    83→// ParseHookInput parses Claude Code hook input from stdin.
    84→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ParseHookInput(hookType agent.HookType, reader io.Reader) (*agent.HookInput, error) {
    85→	data, err := io.ReadAll(reader)
    86→	if err != nil {
    87→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read input: %w", err)
    88→	}
    89→
    90→	if len(data) == 0 {
    91→		return nil, errors.New("empty input")
    92→	}
    93→
    94→	input := &agent.HookInput{
    95→		HookType:  hookType,
    96→		Timestamp: time.Now(),
    97→		RawData:   make(map[string]interface{}),
    98→	}
    99→
   100→	// Parse based on hook type
   101→	switch hookType {
   102→	case agent.HookUserPromptSubmit:
   103→		var raw userPromptSubmitRaw
   104→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   105→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse user prompt submit: %w", err)
   106→		}
   107→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   108→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   109→		input.UserPrompt = raw.Prompt
   110→
   111→	case agent.HookSessionStart, agent.HookSessionEnd, agent.HookStop:
   112→		var raw sessionInfoRaw
   113→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   114→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse session info: %w", err)
   115→		}
   116→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   117→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   118→
   119→	case agent.HookPreToolUse:
   120→		var raw taskHookInputRaw
   121→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   122→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse pre-tool input: %w", err)
   123→		}
   124→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   125→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   126→		input.ToolUseID = raw.ToolUseID
   127→		input.ToolInput = raw.ToolInput
   128→
   129→	case agent.HookPostToolUse:
   130→		var raw postToolHookInputRaw
   131→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   132→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse post-tool input: %w", err)
   133→		}
   134→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   135→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   136→		input.ToolUseID = raw.ToolUseID
   137→		input.ToolInput = raw.ToolInput
   138→		// Store agent ID in raw data for Task tool results
   139→		if raw.ToolResponse.AgentID != "" {
   140→			input.RawData["agent_id"] = raw.ToolResponse.AgentID
   141→		}
   142→	}
   143→
   144→	return input, nil
   145→}
   146→
   147→// GetSessionID extracts the session ID from hook input.
   148→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetSessionID(input *agent.HookInput) string {
   149→	return input.SessionID
   150→}

   228→func TestHandleLifecycleTurnEnd_EmptyRepository(t *testing.T) {
   229→	// Cannot use t.Parallel() because we use t.Chdir()
   230→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   231→	t.Chdir(tmpDir)
   232→
   233→	// Initialize an empty git repo (no commits)
   234→	if err := os.MkdirAll(".git/objects", 0o755); err != nil {
   235→		t.Fatalf("Failed to create .git: %v", err)
   236→	}
   237→	if err := os.WriteFile(".git/HEAD", []byte("ref: refs/heads/main\n"), 0o644); err != nil {
   238→		t.Fatalf("Failed to create HEAD: %v", err)
   239→	}
   240→	paths.ClearRepoRootCache()
   241→
   242→	// Create a transcript file
   243→	transcriptPath := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "transcript.jsonl")
   244→	if err := os.WriteFile(transcriptPath, []byte(`{"type":"user","message":"test"}`+"\n"), 0o644); err != nil {
   245→		t.Fatalf("Failed to create transcript: %v", err)
   246→	}
   247→
   248→	ag := newMockAgent()
   249→	event := &agent.Event{
   250→		Type:       agent.TurnEnd,
   251→		SessionID:  "test-session",
   252→		SessionRef: transcriptPath,
   253→	}
   254→
   255→	err := handleLifecycleTurnEnd(ag, event)
   256→
   257→	// Should return a SilentError wrapping ErrEmptyRepository
   258→	if err == nil {
   259→		t.Error("expected error for empty repository, got nil")
   260→	}
   261→
   262→	var silentErr *SilentError
   263→	if !errors.As(err, &silentErr) {
   264→		t.Errorf("expected SilentError, got: %T", err)
   265→	}
   266→	if !errors.Is(silentErr.Unwrap(), strategy.ErrEmptyRepository) {
   267→		t.Errorf("expected ErrEmptyRepository, got: %v", silentErr.Unwrap())
   268→	}
   269→}

   271→// --- handleLifecycleCompaction tests ---
   272→
   273→func TestHandleLifecycleCompaction_ResetsTranscriptOffset(t *testing.T) {
   274→	// Cannot use t.Parallel() because we use t.Chdir()
   275→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   276→	t.Chdir(tmpDir)
   277→
   278→	// Initialize git repo with a commit (not empty)
   279→	setupGitRepoWithCommit(t, tmpDir)
   280→	paths.ClearRepoRootCache()
   281→
   282→	// Create .entire directory structure
   283→	if err := os.MkdirAll(paths.EntireDir, 0o755); err != nil {
   284→		t.Fatalf("Failed to create .entire: %v", err)
   285→	}
   286→
   287→	// Create a transcript file
   288→	transcriptPath := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "transcript.jsonl")
   289→	transcriptContent := `{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"test prompt"}}` + "\n"
   290→	if err := os.WriteFile(transcriptPath, []byte(transcriptContent), 0o644); err != nil {
   291→		t.Fatalf("Failed to create transcript: %v", err)
   292→	}
   293→
   294→	sessionID := "compaction-test-session"
   295→
   296→	// Create session state with non-zero transcript offset
   297→	sessionState := &strategy.SessionState{
   298→		SessionID:                 sessionID,
   299→		CheckpointTranscriptStart: 50, // Non-zero offset to be reset
   300→	}
   301→	if err := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); err != nil {
   302→		t.Fatalf("Failed to save session state: %v", err)
   303→	}
   304→
   305→	ag := newMockAgent()
   306→	event := &agent.Event{
   307→		Type:       agent.Compaction,
   308→		SessionID:  sessionID,
   309→		SessionRef: transcriptPath,
   310→	}
   311→
   312→	// Note: handleLifecycleCompaction calls handleLifecycleTurnEnd first, which may
   313→	// fail for various reasons in this minimal test setup. We're specifically testing
   314→	// that IF TurnEnd succeeds, the transcript offset is reset.
   315→	// For now, we test the error case and verify the reset logic is attempted.
   316→	//nolint:errcheck // We deliberately ignore errors here as we're testing partial behavior
   317→	_ = handleLifecycleCompaction(ag, event)
   318→
   319→	// Load session state and check if offset was reset
   320→	// (In a real scenario with full setup, this would verify reset to 0)
   321→	//nolint:errcheck // Error doesn't affect test assertions
   322→	loadedState, _ := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   323→	if loadedState != nil && loadedState.CheckpointTranscriptStart != 0 {
   324→		// The compaction handler resets the offset to 0 after successful TurnEnd
   325→		// Due to test setup limitations, TurnEnd may fail, but we're testing the structure
   326→		t.Logf("Note: CheckpointTranscriptStart=%d (reset may not happen due to TurnEnd failure in test)",
   327→			loadedState.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   328→	}
   329→}

   388→// --- createContextFile tests ---
   389→
   390→func TestCreateContextFile_Format(t *testing.T) {
   391→	t.Parallel()
   392→
   393→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   394→	contextFile := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "context.md")
   395→
   396→	prompts := []string{"What is the meaning of life?", "Follow-up question here"}
   397→	summary := "This session explored philosophical questions."
   398→
   399→	err := createContextFile(contextFile, "feat: add philosophy", "session-123", prompts, summary)
   400→	if err != nil {
   401→		t.Fatalf("createContextFile failed: %v", err)
   402→	}
   403→
   404→	content, err := os.ReadFile(contextFile)
   405→	if err != nil {
   406→		t.Fatalf("failed to read context file: %v", err)
   407→	}
   408→
   409→	contentStr := string(content)
   410→
   411→	// Check for expected sections
   412→	if !strings.Contains(contentStr, "# Session Context") {
   413→		t.Error("expected '# Session Context' header")
   414→	}
   415→	if !strings.Contains(contentStr, "Session ID: session-123") {
   416→		t.Error("expected session ID in context file")
   417→	}
   418→	if !strings.Contains(contentStr, "Commit Message: feat: add philosophy") {
   419→		t.Error("expected commit message in context file")
   420→	}
   421→	if !strings.Contains(contentStr, "## Prompts") {
   422→		t.Error("expected '## Prompts' section")
   423→	}
   424→	if !strings.Contains(contentStr, "### Prompt 1") {
   425→		t.Error("expected '### Prompt 1' subsection")
   426→	}
   427→	if !strings.Contains(contentStr, "What is the meaning of life?") {
   428→		t.Error("expected first prompt content")
   429→	}
   430→	if !strings.Contains(contentStr, "### Prompt 2") {
   431→		t.Error("expected '### Prompt 2' subsection")
   432→	}
   433→	if !strings.Contains(contentStr, "## Summary") {
   434→		t.Error("expected '## Summary' section")
   435→	}
   436→	if !strings.Contains(contentStr, "philosophical questions") {
   437→		t.Error("expected summary content")
   438→	}
   439→}

   473→// --- Event type routing tests ---
   474→
   475→func TestDispatchLifecycleEvent_RoutesToCorrectHandler(t *testing.T) {
   476→	t.Parallel()
   477→
   478→	// Test that each event type is routed (we can't easily verify which handler
   479→	// was called without dependency injection, but we can verify no panic and
   480→	// expected error types for each event type with minimal required data)
   481→
   482→	testCases := []struct {
   483→		name        string
   484→		eventType   agent.EventType
   485→		sessionID   string
   486→		expectError bool
   487→		errorSubstr string
   488→	}{
   489→		{
   490→			name:        "SessionStart with empty session ID",
   491→			eventType:   agent.SessionStart,
   492→			sessionID:   "",
   493→			expectError: true,
   494→			errorSubstr: "no session_id",
   495→		},
   496→		{
   497→			name:        "TurnStart with empty session ID",
   498→			eventType:   agent.TurnStart,
   499→			sessionID:   "",
   500→			expectError: true,
   501→			errorSubstr: "no session_id",
   502→		},
   503→		{
   504→			name:        "TurnEnd with empty transcript",
   505→			eventType:   agent.TurnEnd,
   506→			sessionID:   "test",
   507→			expectError: true,
   508→			errorSubstr: "transcript file not found",
   509→		},
   510→		{
   511→			name:        "Compaction with empty transcript is no-op",
   512→			eventType:   agent.Compaction,
   513→			sessionID:   "test",
   514→			expectError: false, // Compaction just resets offset; doesn't read transcript
   515→		},
   516→		{
   517→			name:        "SessionEnd with empty session ID is no-op",
   518→			eventType:   agent.SessionEnd,
   519→			sessionID:   "",
   520→			expectError: false,
   521→		},
   522→		{
   523→			name:        "SubagentStart with valid data",
   524→			eventType:   agent.SubagentStart,
   525→			sessionID:   "test",
   526→			expectError: true, // Will fail due to CapturePreTaskState needing git repo
   527→			errorSubstr: "failed to capture pre-task state",
   528→		},
   529→		{
   530→			name:        "SubagentEnd with valid data",
   531→			eventType:   agent.SubagentEnd,
   532→			sessionID:   "test",
   533→			expectError: false, // Succeeds when run from a valid git repo
   534→		},
   535→	}
   536→
   537→	for _, tc := range testCases {
   538→		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
   539→			t.Parallel()
   540→
   541→			ag := newMockAgent()
   542→			event := &agent.Event{
   543→				Type:      tc.eventType,
   544→				SessionID: tc.sessionID,
   545→				Timestamp: time.Now(),
   546→			}
   547→
   548→			err := DispatchLifecycleEvent(ag, event)
   549→
   550→			if tc.expectError {
   551→				if err == nil {
   552→					t.Errorf("expected error containing %q, got nil", tc.errorSubstr)
   553→				} else if !strings.Contains(err.Error(), tc.errorSubstr) {
   554→					t.Errorf("expected error containing %q, got: %v", tc.errorSubstr, err)
   555→				}
   556→			} else {
   557→				if err != nil {
   558→					t.Errorf("expected no error, got: %v", err)
   559→				}
   560→			}
   561→		})
   562→	}
   563→}

   565→// --- Helper functions for test setup ---
   566→
   567→// setupGitRepoWithCommit initializes a git repo with an initial commit.
   568→func setupGitRepoWithCommit(t *testing.T, dir string) {
   569→	t.Helper()
   570→
   571→	// Initialize git repo
   572→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(dir, ".git", "objects"), 0o755); err != nil {
   573→		t.Fatalf("Failed to create .git/objects: %v", err)
   574→	}
   575→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(dir, ".git", "refs", "heads"), 0o755); err != nil {
   576→		t.Fatalf("Failed to create .git/refs/heads: %v", err)
   577→	}
   578→	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, ".git", "HEAD"), []byte("ref: refs/heads/main\n"), 0o644); err != nil {
   579→		t.Fatalf("Failed to create HEAD: %v", err)
   580→	}
   581→
   582→	// Create a dummy file to commit
   583→	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "README.md"), []byte("# Test\n"), 0o644); err != nil {
   584→		t.Fatalf("Failed to create README.md: %v", err)
   585→	}
   586→
   587→	// Use go-git to create an initial commit
   588→	repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository()
   589→	if err != nil {
   590→		// If we can't open with go-git, the empty repo check will work differently
   591→		t.Logf("Note: Could not open repository with go-git: %v", err)
   592→		return
   593→	}
   594→
   595→	wt, err := repo.Worktree()
   596→	if err != nil {
   597→		t.Logf("Note: Could not get worktree: %v", err)
   598→		return
   599→	}
   600→
   601→	if _, err := wt.Add("README.md"); err != nil {
   602→		t.Logf("Note: Could not add file: %v", err)
   603→		return
   604→	}
   605→
   606→	if _, err := wt.Commit("Initial commit", &git.CommitOptions{
   607→		Author: &object.Signature{
   608→			Name:  "Test",
   609→			Email: "test@test.com",
   610→			When:  time.Now(),
   611→		},
   612→	}); err != nil {
   613→		t.Logf("Note: Could not create commit: %v", err)
   614→	}
   615→}
   616→

   152→// ResolveSessionFile returns the path to a Claude session file.
   153→// Claude names files directly as <id>.jsonl.
   154→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string {
   155→	return filepath.Join(sessionDir, agentSessionID+".jsonl")
   156→}
   157→
   158→// ProtectedDirs returns directories that Claude uses for config/state.
   159→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ProtectedDirs() []string { return []string{".claude"} }
   160→
   161→// GetSessionDir returns the directory where Claude stores session transcripts.
   162→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {
   163→	// Check for test environment override
   164→	if override := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR"); override != "" {
   165→		return override, nil
   166→	}
   167→
   168→	homeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   169→	if err != nil {
   170→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get home directory: %w", err)
   171→	}
   172→
   173→	projectDir := SanitizePathForClaude(repoPath)
   174→	return filepath.Join(homeDir, ".claude", "projects", projectDir), nil
   175→}
   176→
   177→// ReadSession reads a session from Claude's storage (JSONL transcript file).
   178→// The session data is stored in NativeData as raw JSONL bytes.
   179→// ModifiedFiles is computed by parsing the transcript.
   180→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReadSession(input *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   181→	if input.SessionRef == "" {
   182→		return nil, errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   183→	}
   184→
   185→	// Read the raw JSONL file
   186→	data, err := os.ReadFile(input.SessionRef)
   187→	if err != nil {
   188→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   189→	}
   190→
   191→	// Parse to extract computed fields
   192→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(data)
   193→	if err != nil {
   194→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   195→	}
   196→
   197→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   198→		SessionID:     input.SessionID,
   199→		AgentName:     c.Name(),
   200→		SessionRef:    input.SessionRef,
   201→		StartTime:     time.Now(),
   202→		NativeData:    data,
   203→		ModifiedFiles: ExtractModifiedFiles(lines),
   204→	}, nil
   205→}
   206→
   207→// WriteSession writes a session to Claude's storage (JSONL transcript file).
   208→// Uses the NativeData field which contains raw JSONL bytes.
   209→// The session must have been created by Claude Code (AgentName check).
   210→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) WriteSession(session *agent.AgentSession) error {
   211→	if session == nil {
   212→		return errors.New("session is nil")
   213→	}
   214→
   215→	// Verify this session belongs to Claude Code
   216→	if session.AgentName != "" && session.AgentName != c.Name() {
   217→		return fmt.Errorf("session belongs to agent %q, not %q", session.AgentName, c.Name())
   218→	}
   219→
   220→	if session.SessionRef == "" {
   221→		return errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   222→	}
   223→
   224→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   225→		return errors.New("session has no native data to write")
   226→	}
   227→
   228→	// Write the raw JSONL data
   229→	if err := os.WriteFile(session.SessionRef, session.NativeData, 0o600); err != nil {
   230→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   231→	}
   232→
   233→	return nil
   234→}
   235→
   236→// FormatResumeCommand returns the command to resume a Claude Code session.
   237→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string {
   238→	return "claude -r " + sessionID
   239→}
   240→
   241→// Session helper methods - work on AgentSession with Claude's native JSONL data
   242→
   243→// GetLastUserPrompt extracts the last user prompt from the session.
   244→// Requires NativeData to be populated (call ReadSession first).
   245→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetLastUserPrompt(session *agent.AgentSession) string {
   246→	if session == nil || len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   247→		return ""
   248→	}
   249→
   250→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(session.NativeData)
   251→	if err != nil {
   252→		return ""
   253→	}
   254→
   255→	return ExtractLastUserPrompt(lines)
   256→}
   257→
   258→// TruncateAtUUID returns a new session truncated at the given UUID (inclusive).
   259→// This is used for rewind operations to restore transcript state.
   260→// Requires NativeData to be populated.
   261→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) TruncateAtUUID(session *agent.AgentSession, uuid string) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   262→	if session == nil {
   263→		return nil, errors.New("session is nil")
   264→	}
   265→
   266→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   267→		return nil, errors.New("session has no native data")
   268→	}
   269→
   270→	if uuid == "" {
   271→		// No truncation needed, return copy
   272→		return &agent.AgentSession{
   273→			SessionID:     session.SessionID,
   274→			AgentName:     session.AgentName,
   275→			RepoPath:      session.RepoPath,
   276→			SessionRef:    session.SessionRef,
   277→			StartTime:     session.StartTime,
   278→			NativeData:    session.NativeData,
   279→			ModifiedFiles: session.ModifiedFiles,
   280→		}, nil
   281→	}
   282→
   283→	// Parse, truncate, re-serialize
   284→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(session.NativeData)
   285→	if err != nil {
   286→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   287→	}
   288→
   289→	truncated := TruncateAtUUID(lines, uuid)
   290→
   291→	newData, err := SerializeTranscript(truncated)
   292→	if err != nil {
   293→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to serialize truncated transcript: %w", err)
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   297→		SessionID:     session.SessionID,
   298→		AgentName:     session.AgentName,
   299→		RepoPath:      session.RepoPath,
   300→		SessionRef:    session.SessionRef,
   301→		StartTime:     session.StartTime,
   302→		NativeData:    newData,
   303→		ModifiedFiles: ExtractModifiedFiles(truncated),
   304→	}, nil
   305→}
   306→
   307→// FindCheckpointUUID finds the UUID of the message containing the tool_result
   308→// for the given tool use ID. Used for task checkpoint rewind.
   309→// Returns the UUID and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.
   310→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) FindCheckpointUUID(session *agent.AgentSession, toolUseID string) (string, bool) {
   311→	if session == nil || len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   312→		return "", false
   313→	}
   314→
   315→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(session.NativeData)
   316→	if err != nil {
   317→		return "", false
   318→	}
   319→
   320→	return FindCheckpointUUID(lines, toolUseID)
   321→}
   322→
   323→// ReadSessionFromPath is a convenience method that reads a session directly from a file path.
   324→// This is useful when you have the path but not a HookInput.
   325→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReadSessionFromPath(transcriptPath, sessionID string) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   326→	return c.ReadSession(&agent.HookInput{
   327→		SessionID:  sessionID,
   328→		SessionRef: transcriptPath,
   329→	})
   330→}
   331→
   332→// SanitizePathForClaude converts a path to Claude's project directory format.
   333→// Claude replaces any non-alphanumeric character with a dash.
   334→var nonAlphanumericRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`[^a-zA-Z0-9]`)
   335→
   336→func SanitizePathForClaude(path string) string {
   337→	return nonAlphanumericRegex.ReplaceAllString(path, "-")
   338→}
