   237→// --- Transcript flush sentinel ---
   238→
   239→// stopHookSentinel is the string that appears in Claude Code's hook_progress
   240→// entry when the stop hook has been invoked, indicating the transcript is fully flushed.
   241→const stopHookSentinel = "hooks claude-code stop"
   242→
   243→// waitForTranscriptFlush polls the transcript file for the stop hook sentinel.
   244→// Falls back silently after a timeout.
   245→func waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath string, hookStartTime time.Time) {
   246→	const (
   247→		maxWait      = 3 * time.Second
   248→		pollInterval = 50 * time.Millisecond
   249→		tailBytes    = 4096
   250→		maxSkew      = 2 * time.Second
   251→	)
   252→
   253→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "agent.claudecode")
   254→	deadline := time.Now().Add(maxWait)
   255→	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
   256→		if checkStopSentinel(transcriptPath, tailBytes, hookStartTime, maxSkew) {
   257→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel found",
   258→				slog.Duration("wait", time.Since(hookStartTime)),
   259→			)
   260→			return
   261→		}
   262→		time.Sleep(pollInterval)
   263→	}
   264→	logging.Warn(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel not found within timeout, proceeding",
   265→		slog.Duration("timeout", maxWait),
   266→	)
   267→}
   268→
   269→// checkStopSentinel reads the tail of the transcript file and looks for the sentinel.
   270→func checkStopSentinel(path string, tailBytes int64, hookStartTime time.Time, maxSkew time.Duration) bool {
   271→	f, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // path comes from agent hook input
   272→	if err != nil {
   273→		return false
   274→	}
   275→	defer f.Close()
   276→
   277→	info, err := f.Stat()
   278→	if err != nil {
   279→		return false
   280→	}
   281→	offset := info.Size() - tailBytes
   282→	if offset < 0 {
   283→		offset = 0
   284→	}
   285→	buf := make([]byte, info.Size()-offset)
   286→	if _, err := f.ReadAt(buf, offset); err != nil {
   287→		return false
   288→	}
   289→
   290→	lines := strings.Split(string(buf), "\n")
   291→	for _, line := range lines {
   292→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   293→		if line == "" || !strings.Contains(line, stopHookSentinel) {
   294→			continue
   295→		}
   296→
   297→		var entry struct {
   298→			Timestamp string `json:"timestamp"`
   299→		}
   300→		if json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry) != nil || entry.Timestamp == "" {
   301→			continue
   302→		}
   303→		ts, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, entry.Timestamp)
   304→		if err != nil {
   305→			ts, err = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, entry.Timestamp)
   306→			if err != nil {
   307→				continue
   308→			}
   309→		}
   310→		cutoff := hookStartTime.Add(-maxSkew)
   311→		if ts.After(cutoff) {
   312→			return true
   313→		}
   314→	}
   315→	return false
   316→}
   317→

   340→// TranscriptAnalyzer interface implementation
   341→
   342→// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current line count of a Claude Code transcript.
   343→// Claude Code uses JSONL format, so position is the number of lines.
   344→// This is a lightweight operation that only counts lines without parsing JSON.
   345→// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines (no size limit).
   346→// Returns 0 if the file doesn't exist or is empty.
   347→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
   348→	if path == "" {
   349→		return 0, nil
   350→	}
   351→
   352→	file, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from Claude Code transcript location
   353→	if err != nil {
   354→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   355→			return 0, nil
   356→		}
   357→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to open transcript file: %w", err)
   358→	}
   359→	defer file.Close()
   360→
   361→	reader := bufio.NewReader(file)
   362→	lineCount := 0
   363→
   364→	for {
   365→		line, err := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
   366→		if err != nil {
   367→			if err == io.EOF {
   368→				if len(line) > 0 {
   369→					lineCount++ // Count final line without trailing newline
   370→				}
   371→				break
   372→			}
   373→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   374→		}
   375→		lineCount++
   376→	}
   377→
   378→	return lineCount, nil
   379→}
   380→
   381→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given line number.
   382→// For Claude Code (JSONL format), offset is the starting line number.
   383→// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines (no size limit).
   384→// Returns:
   385→//   - files: list of file paths modified by Claude (from Write/Edit tools)
   386→//   - currentPosition: total number of lines in the file
   387→//   - error: any error encountered during reading
   388→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error) {
   389→	if path == "" {
   390→		return nil, 0, nil
   391→	}
   392→
   393→	file, openErr := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from Claude Code transcript location
   394→	if openErr != nil {
   395→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to open transcript file: %w", openErr)
   396→	}
   397→	defer file.Close()
   398→
   399→	reader := bufio.NewReader(file)
   400→	var lines []TranscriptLine
   401→	lineNum := 0
   402→
   403→	for {
   404→		lineData, readErr := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
   405→		if readErr != nil && readErr != io.EOF {
   406→			return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", readErr)
   407→		}
   408→
   409→		if len(lineData) > 0 {
   410→			lineNum++
   411→			if lineNum > startOffset {
   412→				var line TranscriptLine
   413→				if parseErr := json.Unmarshal(lineData, &line); parseErr == nil {
   414→					lines = append(lines, line)
   415→				}
   416→				// Skip malformed lines silently
   417→			}
   418→		}
   419→
   420→		if readErr == io.EOF {
   421→			break
   422→		}
   423→	}
   424→
   425→	return ExtractModifiedFiles(lines), lineNum, nil
   426→}
   427→
   428→// ChunkTranscript splits a JSONL transcript at line boundaries.
   429→// Claude Code uses JSONL format (one JSON object per line), so chunking
   430→// is done at newline boundaries to preserve message integrity.
   431→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
   432→	chunks, err := agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
   433→	if err != nil {
   434→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk JSONL transcript: %w", err)
   435→	}
   436→	return chunks, nil
   437→}
   438→

> AGENT

All PR feedback issues have been addressed. Here's a summary of the fixes:

**Fixes Applied:**

1. **Transcript error text** (lifecycle.go:162): Changed error message from "not found or empty" to "not found" since we only check existence. Updated corresponding tests.

2. **PostTodo testability** (hooks_claudecode_posttodo.go): Refactored `handleClaudeCodePostTodo()` to call `handleClaudeCodePostTodoFromReader(io.Reader)`, making it testable by accepting an io.Reader parameter.

3. **HIGH - TurnEnd missing subagent files** (lifecycle.go + agent/agent.go + claudecode/claude.go): 
   - Added new `SubagentAwareExtractor` interface with `ExtractAllModifiedFiles()` and `CalculateTotalTokenUsage()` methods
   - Implemented interface for ClaudeCodeAgent
   - Updated `handleLifecycleTurnEnd` to prefer `SubagentAwareExtractor` when available

4. **MEDIUM - Subagent token usage excluded** (lifecycle.go): Updated token calculation to use `CalculateTotalTokenUsage()` via `SubagentAwareExtractor` when available, falling back to basic `CalculateTokenUsage()`.

5. **Compaction test verification** (lifecycle_test.go): Updated `TestHandleLifecycleCompaction_ResetsTranscriptOffset` to properly assert that `CheckpointTranscriptStart` is reset to 0 after compaction.

6. **LOW - Sentinel timestamp upper bound** (claudecode/lifecycle.go:310-314): Added upper bound check to `checkStopSentinel()` so timestamps too far in the future are also rejected (was only checking lower bound).
